<text bytes="4619" sha1="tqvtvfykrjjt6lmvuofvdyhtso5iee4" xml:space="preserve"><!--This is the default structure of how you can usually create a page about a character, whether from a movie, TV series, video game, book, or comic. They typically share common elements, such as an introduction or a biography. These instructions between arrows (using the source editor) or with the exclamation mark icon (visual editor) are internal and are not shown in the public version to read, and you can remove them once you have learnt how to create the page. If you feel a page shouldn't exist on a wiki, you can add {{Delete|<reason>}} to it and add reasons for the removal. This helps admins to identify these pages and delete them. On the other hand, {{Stub}} is usually placed on new pages that are recently created with a few details, to coordinate editors in order to improve it in the future.--> {{Character | name = {{PAGENAME}} | image = Wiki Character.jpeg|alt=A knight wearing golden armor, holding a sword with a pink hilt pointing down. The background is a brown, hazy wooded area with a stone building. | imagecaption = | aliases = Other names | relatives = Their relatives | affiliation = | occupation = | marital = | birthDate = | birthPlace = | deathDate = | deathPlace = | species = | gender = | height = | weight = | eyes = | portrayedby = | appearsin = | debut = }} {{Quote | text =The text of the quote. | speaker =Author | receiver = | attribution = | source = }} <!-- The first elements you see above are two templates: the Character infobox and Quote template. Infoboxes are used to display general relevant information. Once you add data in one of the fields, the infobox would be displayed. You can add a custom name in the infobox replacing the {{PAGENAME}} code. View more details on this in Template:Character. For real-life people, you can use Template:Cast. Some character pages also use templates to show quotes. You can replace the text of the quote and the author, or remove them if you don't plan to use it. You can also visit Template:Quote for usage instructions.--> '''{{PAGENAME}}''' is the name of the character. This is the introduction where you briefly describe the character. Don't forget to replace this text. ==Biography== <!-- When creating a new page, remember to use different sections, add proper formatting using wikitext, and most importantly, add images and add links to other related articles whenever possible. You have more info on Help:New page, Help:Formatting text, Help:Wikitext, Help:Adding images and Help:Links.--> Start adding a general biography about this character, replacing this text. ==Extra section== <!--Sometimes you need to add contextual links between articles to answer readers' questions. You can visit Template:Hatnote to find out about the different related templates editors use.--> You can replace the "Extra section" title and all this text to add any other relevant sections talking about the character. Character pages usually describe character's relationships, abilities, skills, appearances, and also trivia facts. <!-- When you need to display data, creating a table is a good option. For simpler sections that list items, using a list is easier.--> These sections can also include [[w:Help:Lists|lists]] and [[w:Help:Tables|tables]], and adding the [[w:Help:Table Progress Tracking|progress tracking feature]] allows all users to keep track of their personal progress related to these elements. * Item list 1 * Item list 2 * Item list 3 * Item list 4 <table-progress-tracking table-id="1" unique-column-index="1"> {| class="wikitable sortable" |- ! Column 1 header !! Column 2 header !! Column 3 header |- | Text for row 1 cell 1 || Text for row 1 cell 2 || Text for row 1 cell 3 |- | Text for row 2 cell 1 || Text for row 2 cell 2 || Text for row 2 cell 3 |- | Text for row 3 cell 1 || Text for row 3 cell 2 || Text for row 3 cell 3 |} </table-progress-tracking> <!--The template below is a navbox, typically used as a navigation element to link to related articles quickly and easily. Once you have several pages about a group, consider creating a specific navbox for it. Find out more about using them at Template:Navbox. --> {{Navbox|header=Characters|body=Add links to character pages, like [[{{PAGENAME}}]].}} <!-- When you finish creating an article, don't forget to add the corresponding categories, which are used to group articles. Read more about them at Help:Categories.--> <includeonly>[[Category:Characters]]</includeonly><noinclude>[[Category:Example templates]]</noinclude></text>
<sha1>tqvtvfykrjjt6lmvuofvdyhtso5iee4</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="4967" sha1="qpjvoqomlztqadahtv4mj8pdhbk5xkp" xml:space="preserve"><!--This is the default structure of how you can usually create a page about a product, based on the topic of the wiki. Users create pages about TV shows, movies, video games, books, comics, toys or any other creation. They typically share common elements, such as an introduction, plot, cast and crew, and production details. These instructions between arrows (using the source editor) or with the exclamation mark icon (visual editor) are internal and are not shown in the public version to read, and you can remove them once you have learnt how to create the page. If you feel a page shouldn't exist on a wiki, you can add {{Delete|<reason>}} to it and add reasons for the removal. This helps admins to identify these pages and delete them. On the other hand, {{Stub}} is usually placed on new pages that are recently created with a few details, to coordinate editors in order to improve it in the future.--> {{Series | title = {{PAGENAME}} | image = Wiki Product.jpeg|alt=Stylized text reading "PRODUCT LOGO" in bold, multi-colored letters with a vintage texture, set against a deep purple background filled with stars and abstract geometric shapes. | caption = | release = | seasons = Number of seasons | episodes = Number of episodes | runtime = | genre = | network = | distrib = | creator = | writer = | director = | composer = | based on = | exec prod = | producer = | prod co = | country = | language = }} {{Quote | text =The text of the quote. | speaker =Author | receiver = | attribution = | source = }} <!-- The first elements you see above are two templates: the Series infobox and Quote template. Infoboxes are used to display general relevant information. Once you add data in one of the fields, the infobox would be displayed. You can add a custom name in the infobox replacing the {{PAGENAME}} code. View more details on this in Template:Series. If the page is about another kind of product, consider using Template:Film, Template:Game, Template:Book or Template:Album. For a page about a concrete season, you can also use Template:Season. Some character pages also use templates to show quotes, but you can also include them on pages about products. You need to replace the text of the quote and the author, or remove them if you don't plan to use it. You can also visit Template:Quote to find out more.--> '''{{PAGENAME}}''' is the name of the product. This is the introduction where you briefly describe the details about it. Don't forget to replace this text. ==Plot== <!-- When creating a new page, remember to use different sections, add proper formatting using wikitext, and most importantly, add images and add links to other related articles whenever possible. You have more info on Help:New page, Help:Formatting text, Help:Wikitext, Help:Adding images and Help:Links.--> Start adding a summary of the plot with a short synopsis, which can then be expanded, replacing this text. ==Extra section== <!--Sometimes you need to add contextual links between articles to answer readers' questions. You can visit Template:Hatnote to find out more about the different related templates editors use.--> You can replace the "Extra section" title and all this text to add any other relevant sections talking about the product. Pages about TV shows usually describe cast, characters, the list of episodes and seasons, production or critical reception. Pages about video games are more focused on the gameplay and development. <!-- When you need to display data, creating a table is a good option. For simpler sections that list items, using a list is easier.--> These sections can also include [[w:Help:Lists|lists]] and [[w:Help:Tables|tables]], and adding the [[w:Help:Table Progress Tracking|progress tracking feature]] allows all users to keep track of their personal progress related to these elements. * Item list 1 * Item list 2 * Item list 3 * Item list 4 <table-progress-tracking table-id="1" unique-column-index="1"> {| class="wikitable sortable" |- ! Column 1 header !! Column 2 header !! Column 3 header |- | Text for row 1 cell 1 || Text for row 1 cell 2 || Text for row 1 cell 3 |- | Text for row 2 cell 1 || Text for row 2 cell 2 || Text for row 2 cell 3 |- | Text for row 3 cell 1 || Text for row 3 cell 2 || Text for row 3 cell 3 |} </table-progress-tracking> <!--The template below is a navbox, typically used as a navigation element to link to related articles quickly and easily. Once you have several pages about a group, consider creating a specific navbox for it. Find out more at Template:Navbox.--> {{Navbox|header=Products|body=Add links to product pages, like [[{{PAGENAME}}]].}} <!-- When you finish creating an article, don't forget to add the corresponding categories, which are used to group articles. Read more at Help:Categories.--> <includeonly>[[Category:Series]]</includeonly><noinclude>[[Category:Example templates]]</noinclude></text>
<sha1>qpjvoqomlztqadahtv4mj8pdhbk5xkp</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="2383" sha1="5gtdrd3qkchyra4j769eptz3qivs29m" xml:space="preserve"><blockquote class="pull-quote"> <div class="pull-quote__text">{{{text|Text...}}}</div> <p style="text-align: right"><cite>—{{{speaker|speaker}}}{{#if:{{{receiver|}}}|, to {{{receiver|}}}}}{{#if:{{{attribution|}}}|, {{{attribution|}}}}}{{#if:{{{source|}}}|, {{{source|}}}}}</cite></p> </blockquote> <noinclude> ==Description== A template used for displaying Structured Quotes ( ''<nowiki>{{#SQuote:}}</nowiki>'' ). If you want to unlock the full potential of Structured Quotes, please avoid using this template directly, and consider ''<nowiki>{{#SQuote:}}</nowiki>'' markup instead. See https://community.fandom.com/wiki/Help:Structured_Quotes for extra information about Structured Quotes. ==Syntax== <pre> {{StructuredQuote | text = | speaker = | receiver = | attribution = | source = }} </pre> ==Samples== {{StructuredQuote |text=Size matters not. Look at me. Judge me by my size, do you? Hmm? Hmm. And well you should not. (...) |speaker=[[w:c:en.starwars:Yoda|Yoda]] |receiver=[[w:c:en.starwars:Luke_Skywalker|Luke Skywalker]] |source=[[w:c:en.starwars:Star_Wars:_Episode_V_The_Empire_Strikes_Back|Star Wars: Episode V The Empire Strikes Back]] }} <pre> {{StructuredQuote |text=Size matters not. Look at me. Judge me by my size, do you? Hmm? Hmm. And well you should not. (...) |speaker=[[Yoda]] |receiver=[[Luke Skywalker]] |source=[[Star Wars: Episode V The Empire Strikes Back]] }} </pre> == TemplateData == <templatedata> { "params": { "text": { "label": "Quote text", "description": "quote text", "type": "content", "required": true }, "speaker": { "label": "Person(s) quoted", "description": "individual(s) who uttered or wrote the quoted words (wikitext links, comma-separated)", "type": "content", "required": true }, "receiver": { "label": "Person(s) to whom the quote was spoken", "description": "person the quote was spoken to (wikitext links, comma-separated)", "type": "content", "suggested": true }, "attribution": { "label": "Attribution(s)", "description": "attribution (wikitext links, comma-separated)", "type": "content", "suggested": true }, "source": { "label": "Quote source", "description": "place where it was spoken (wikitext link)", "type": "content", "suggested": true } }, "format": "block" } </templatedata> </noinclude></text>
<sha1>5gtdrd3qkchyra4j769eptz3qivs29m</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<comment>Created page with "<!--This is the default structure of how you can usually create a page about an episode from a TV show. Pages about book or comic chapters are similar. They typically share common elements, such as an introduction, summary, plot, cast and crew, and production details. These instructions between arrows (using the source editor) or with the exclamation mark icon (visual editor) are internal and are not shown in the public version to read, and you can remove them once you..."</comment>
<origin>149</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="4768" sha1="gu7v7ybn77i6atwiwnwhzrm6ry6xiel" xml:space="preserve"><!--This is the default structure of how you can usually create a page about an episode from a TV show. Pages about book or comic chapters are similar. They typically share common elements, such as an introduction, summary, plot, cast and crew, and production details. These instructions between arrows (using the source editor) or with the exclamation mark icon (visual editor) are internal and are not shown in the public version to read, and you can remove them once you have learnt how to create the page. If you feel a page shouldn't exist on a wiki, you can add {{Delete|<reason>}} to it and add reasons for the removal. This helps admins to identify these pages and delete them. On the other hand, {{Stub}} is usually placed on new pages that are recently created with a few details, to coordinate editors in order to improve it in the future.--> {{Episode | title = {{PAGENAME}} | partOfSeason = Season number | episodeNumber = Episode number | image = Wiki Episode.jpeg|alt=A detective in a trench coat and fedora holding a flashlight and magnifying glass, rendered in purple and blue tones with a halftone texture against a dark window backdrop. | airDate = | writer = | director = | previousEpisode = | nextEpisode = }} {{Quote | text =The text of the quote. | speaker =Author | receiver = | attribution = | source = }} <!-- The first elements you see above are two templates: the Episode infobox and Quote template. Infoboxes are used to display general relevant information. Once you add data in one of the fields, the infobox would be displayed. You can add a custom name in the infobox replacing the {{PAGENAME}} code. View more details on this in Template:Episode. For a page about a season, you can also use Template:Season. Some character pages also use templates to show quotes, but you can also include them on pages about episodes. You need to replace the text of the quote and the author, or remove them if you don't plan to use it. You can also visit Template:Quote to find out more.--> '''{{PAGENAME}}''' is the name of the episode. It's part of a season of a TV series. You can write when it was broadcasted and its writer and director. This is the introduction where you briefly describe the details about it. Don't forget to replace this text. ==Synopsis== <!-- When creating a new page, remember to use different sections, add proper formatting using wikitext, and most importantly, add images and add links to other related articles whenever possible. You have more info on Help:New page, Help:Formatting text, Help:Wikitext, Help:Adding images and Help:Links.--> Start adding a summary of the plot with a short synopsis, which can then be expanded, replacing this text. ==Extra section== <!--Sometimes you need to add contextual links between articles to answer readers' questions. You can visit Template:Hatnote to find out more about the different related templates editors use.--> You can replace the "Extra section" title and all this text to add any other relevant sections talking about the episode. These pages usually contain the cast, other production details, promotional images, trivia facts or additional quotes. <!-- When you need to display data, creating a table is a good option. For simpler sections that list items, using a list is easier.--> Users also describe everything appearing, such as characters, locations, vehicles or objects. They usually use [[w:Help:Lists|lists]] and [[w:Help:Tables|tables]], and adding the [[w:Help:Table Progress Tracking|progress tracking feature]] allows them to keep track of their personal progress related to these elements. * Item list 1 * Item list 2 * Item list 3 * Item list 4 <table-progress-tracking table-id="1" unique-column-index="1"> {| class="wikitable sortable" |- ! Column 1 header !! Column 2 header !! Column 3 header |- | Text for row 1 cell 1 || Text for row 1 cell 2 || Text for row 1 cell 3 |- | Text for row 2 cell 1 || Text for row 2 cell 2 || Text for row 2 cell 3 |- | Text for row 3 cell 1 || Text for row 3 cell 2 || Text for row 3 cell 3 |} </table-progress-tracking> <!--The template below is a navbox, typically used as a navigation element to link to related articles quickly and easily. Once you have several pages about a group, consider creating a specific navbox for it. Find out more at Template:Navbox. --> {{Navbox|header=Episodes|body=Add links to episode pages, like [[{{PAGENAME}}]].}} <!-- When you finish creating an article, don't forget to add the corresponding categories, which are used to group articles. Read more at Help:Categories.--> <includeonly>[[Category:Episodes]]</includeonly><noinclude>[[Category:Example templates]]</noinclude></text>
<sha1>gu7v7ybn77i6atwiwnwhzrm6ry6xiel</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="4782" sha1="1nwq9qf6achgala5cez2pu10nry2f79" xml:space="preserve"><!--This is the default structure of how you can usually create a page about a mission or quest from a video game. Pages about events are similar. They typically share common elements, such as an introduction, plot, walkthrough about gameplay, advice to complete it, goals, rewards and trivia. These instructions between arrows (using the source editor) or with the exclamation mark icon (visual editor) are internal and are not shown in the public version to read, and you can remove them once you have learnt how to create the page. If you feel a page shouldn't exist on a wiki, you can add {{Delete|<reason>}} to it and add reasons for the removal. This helps admins to identify these pages and delete them. On the other hand, {{Stub}} is usually placed on new pages that are recently created with a few details, to coordinate editors in order to improve it in the future.--> {{Quest | title = {{PAGENAME}} | image = Wiki Mission.jpeg|alt=Two swords clashing, one with a turquoise blade, the other dark blue. The background is a grey. barren, grassy area, with dead trees. | imagecaption = | start = How do you start | end = How do you finish | prerequisites = | level = | location = | rewards = | previous = | next = }} {{Quote | text =The text of the quote. | speaker =Author | receiver = | attribution = | source = }} <!-- The first elements you see above are two templates: the Quest infobox and Quote template. Infoboxes are used to display general relevant information. Once you add data in one of the fields, the infobox would be displayed. You can add a custom name in the infobox replacing the {{PAGENAME}} code. View more details on this in Template:Quest. For a page about an event, you can also use Template:Event. Some character pages also use templates to show quotes, but you can also include them on pages about missions. You need to replace the text of the quote and the author, or remove them if you don't plan to use it. You can also visit Template:Quote to find out more.--> '''{{PAGENAME}}''' is the name of the mission. This is the introduction where you briefly describe the details about it, explain its part of a game, which characters it involves and which character gives it to you, alongside other contextual notes about the game plot. Don't forget to replace this text. ==Walkthrough== <!-- When creating a new page, remember to use different sections, add proper formatting using wikitext, and most importantly, add images and add links to other related articles whenever possible. You have more info on Help:New page, Help:Formatting text, Help:Wikitext, Help:Adding images and Help:Links.--> Start adding a summary of the walkthrough, explaining the actions you have to do to complete the mission or quest. Remember to replace this text. ==Extra section== <!--Sometimes you need to add contextual links between articles to answer readers' questions. You can visit Template:Hatnote to find out more about the different related templates editors use.--> You can replace the "Extra section" title and all this text to add any other relevant sections talking about the mission. These pages usually contain the plot, advice, goals to achieve and general notes. <!-- When you need to display data, creating a table is a good option. For simpler sections that list items, using a list is easier.--> You may also want to log data on a page, such as characters, locations, vehicles or objects. You can use [[w:Help:Lists|lists]] and [[w:Help:Tables|tables]], and add the [[w:Help:Table Progress Tracking|progress tracking feature]] to allow users to track personal progress related to these elements. * Item list 1 * Item list 2 * Item list 3 * Item list 4 <table-progress-tracking table-id="1" unique-column-index="1"> {| class="wikitable sortable" |- ! Column 1 header !! Column 2 header !! Column 3 header |- | Text for row 1 cell 1 || Text for row 1 cell 2 || Text for row 1 cell 3 |- | Text for row 2 cell 1 || Text for row 2 cell 2 || Text for row 2 cell 3 |- | Text for row 3 cell 1 || Text for row 3 cell 2 || Text for row 3 cell 3 |} </table-progress-tracking> <!--The template below is a navbox, typically used as a navigation element to link to related articles quickly and easily. Once you have several pages about a group, consider creating a specific navbox for it. Find out more at Template:Navbox. --> {{Navbox|header=Missions|body=Add links to mission pages, like [[{{PAGENAME}}]].}} <!-- When you finish creating an article, don't forget to add the corresponding categories, which are used to group articles. Read more at Help:Categories.--> <includeonly>[[Category:Missions]]</includeonly><noinclude>[[Category:Example templates]]</noinclude></text>
<sha1>1nwq9qf6achgala5cez2pu10nry2f79</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="4953" sha1="b4jace62jwdolgs49vsmz9kopys97qh" xml:space="preserve"><!--This is the default structure of how you can usually create a page about a location, whether from a movie, TV series, video game, book, or comic. They typically share common elements, such as an introduction, a physical description, background or related events, people who live or interact, objects you can find, appearances and trivia facts. These instructions between arrows (using the source editor) or with the exclamation mark icon (visual editor) are internal and are not shown in the public version to read, and you can remove them once you have learnt how to create the page. If you feel a page shouldn't exist on a wiki, you can add {{Delete|<reason>}} to it and add reasons for the removal. This helps admins to identify these pages and delete them. On the other hand, {{Stub}} is usually placed on new pages that are recently created with a few details, to coordinate editors in order to improve it in the future.--> {{Location | title = {{PAGENAME}} | image = Wiki Location.jpeg|alt=A red and white striped lighthouse emitting beams of light made of white dots, set against a dark, textured sea and sky background. | imagecaption = | map = | mapcaption = | type = | level = | location = Where it is placed | inhabitants = People living or interacting }} {{Quote | text =The text of the quote. | speaker =Author | receiver = | attribution = | source = }} <!-- The first elements you see above are two templates: the Location infobox and Quote template. Infoboxes are used to display general relevant information. Once you add data in one of the fields, the infobox would be displayed. You can add a custom name in the infobox replacing the {{PAGENAME}} code. View more details on this in Template:Location. Some character pages also use templates to show quotes, but you can also include them on pages about locations. You need to replace the text of the quote and the author, or remove them if you don't plan to use it. You can also visit Template:Quote to find out more.--> '''{{PAGENAME}}''' is the name of the location. This is the introduction where you briefly describe the details about it, which kind of location or building it is, where it's placed, in which product appears or any relevant event happening there. Don't forget to replace this text. ==Description== <!-- When creating a new page, remember to use different sections, add proper formatting using wikitext, and most importantly, add images and add links to other related articles whenever possible. You have more info on Help:New page, Help:Formatting text, Help:Wikitext, Help:Adding images and Help:Links.--> Start adding a general description of this location: if it’s an area or a building, geography, any important background, famous inhabitants and events or anything interesting for the reader. Remember to replace this text. ==Extra section== <!--Sometimes you need to add contextual links between articles to answer readers' questions. You can visit Template:Hatnote to find out more about the different related templates editors use.--> You can replace the "Extra section" title and all this text to add any other relevant sections talking about the location. These pages usually contain a list of people, other minor locations such as buildings or rooms, objects you can find, where this location appears in media relating to the topic, and any relevant detail for the gameplay in the case of video games. <!-- When you need to display data, creating a table is a good option. For simpler sections that list items, using a list is easier.--> To describe everything appearing you have the option of [[w:Help:Lists|lists]] and [[w:Help:Tables|tables]], and adding the [[w:Help:Table Progress Tracking|progress tracking feature]] to allow users to keep track of their personal progress related to these elements. * Item list 1 * Item list 2 * Item list 3 * Item list 4 <table-progress-tracking table-id="1" unique-column-index="1"> {| class="wikitable sortable" |- ! Column 1 header !! Column 2 header !! Column 3 header |- | Text for row 1 cell 1 || Text for row 1 cell 2 || Text for row 1 cell 3 |- | Text for row 2 cell 1 || Text for row 2 cell 2 || Text for row 2 cell 3 |- | Text for row 3 cell 1 || Text for row 3 cell 2 || Text for row 3 cell 3 |} </table-progress-tracking> <!--The template below is a navbox, typically used as a navigation element to link to related articles quickly and easily. Once you have several pages about a group, consider creating a specific navbox for it. Find out more at Template:Navbox. --> {{Navbox|header=Locations|body=Add links to location pages, like [[{{PAGENAME}}]].}} <!-- When you finish creating an article, don't forget to add the corresponding categories, which are used to group articles. Read more at Help:Categories.--> <includeonly>[[Category:Locations]]</includeonly><noinclude>[[Category:Example templates]]</noinclude></text>
<sha1>b4jace62jwdolgs49vsmz9kopys97qh</sha1>

<text bytes="4945" sha1="rs6j3pfyyp3dj8yjji7iboojwgwl6gh" xml:space="preserve"><!--This is the default structure of how you can usually create a page about an object or item, whether from a movie, TV series, video game, book or comic. They typically share common elements, such as an introduction, a physical description, background or related events, locations where you can find them, characters or people who use them, appearances and trivia facts. These instructions between arrows (using the source editor) or with the exclamation mark icon (visual editor) are internal and are not shown in the public version to read, and you can remove them once you have learnt how to create the page. If you feel a page shouldn't exist on a wiki, you can add {{Delete|<reason>}} to it and add reasons for the removal. This helps admins to identify these pages and delete them. On the other hand, {{Stub}} is usually placed on new pages that are recently created with a few details, to coordinate editors in order to improve it in the future.--> {{Item | title = {{PAGENAME}} | image = Wiki Object.jpeg|alt=A red ruby jewell, with a golden border. The background is a religious building with an alter, stained glass windows, and stone walls. | imagecaption = | type = How the object is | effects = How it works | source = | buy = | sell = }} {{Quote | text =The text of the quote. | speaker =Author | receiver = | attribution = | source = }} <!-- The first elements you see above are two templates: the Item infobox and Quote template. Infoboxes are used to display general relevant information. Once you add data in one of the fields, the infobox would be displayed. You can add a custom name in the infobox replacing the {{PAGENAME}} code. View more details on this in Template:Item. Some character pages also use templates to show quotes, but you can also include them on pages about objects. You need to replace the text of the quote and the author, or remove them if you don't plan to use it. You can also visit Template:Quote to find out more.--> '''{{PAGENAME}}''' is the name of the object. This is the introduction where you (where applicable) can briefly describe it, who uses it, how it's used, where you can find it, which media relating to the topic it appears in, and any relevant events related to it. Give more detailed descriptions in the sections below. Don't forget to replace this text. ==Description== <!-- When creating a new page, remember to use different sections, add proper formatting using wikitext, and most importantly, add images and add links to other related articles whenever possible. You have more info on Help:New page, Help:Formatting text, Help:Wikitext, Help:Adding images and Help:Links.--> Start adding a general description of this object: what kind of item it is (weapon, vehicle, costume), how it is used, or any relevant power or special property. Remember to replace this text. ==Extra section== <!--Sometimes you need to add contextual links between articles to answer readers' questions. You can visit Template:Hatnote to find out more about the different related templates editors use.--> You can replace the "Extra section" title and all this text to add any other relevant sections talking about the object. These pages usually contain information about the places where you can find it, people or characters using it, the object's background or related events when they were important, as well as any other relevant trivia. <!-- When you need to display data, creating a table is a good option. For simpler sections that list items, using a list is easier.--> To describe everything related to these objects you have the option of [[w:Help:Lists|lists]] and [[w:Help:Tables|tables]], and adding the [[w:Help:Table Progress Tracking|progress tracking feature]] allows users to keep track of their personal progress related to these elements. * Item list 1 * Item list 2 * Item list 3 * Item list 4 <table-progress-tracking table-id="1" unique-column-index="1"> {| class="wikitable sortable" |- ! Column 1 header !! Column 2 header !! Column 3 header |- | Text for row 1 cell 1 || Text for row 1 cell 2 || Text for row 1 cell 3 |- | Text for row 2 cell 1 || Text for row 2 cell 2 || Text for row 2 cell 3 |- | Text for row 3 cell 1 || Text for row 3 cell 2 || Text for row 3 cell 3 |} </table-progress-tracking> <!--The template below is a navbox, typically used as a navigation element to link to related articles quickly and easily. Once you have several pages about a group, consider creating a specific navbox for it. Find out more at Template:Navbox. --> {{Navbox|header=Objects|body=Add links to object pages, like [[{{PAGENAME}}]].}} <!-- When you finish creating an article, don't forget to add the corresponding categories, which are used to group articles. Read more at Help:Categories.--> <includeonly>[[Category:Objects]]</includeonly><noinclude>[[Category:Example templates]]</noinclude></text>

<title>Duncan - The Devil</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>159</id>
<revision>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2639" sha1="dzoi06rcthz2p0ua7ju3ugafimofjow" xml:space="preserve">{{DISPLAYTITLE:Duncan - The Devil}}{{Character|name=Duncan|imagecaption=Hullo Method|aliases=Duncle|relatives=Hecktor, Marcus|affiliation=|occupation=|marital=Art|birthDate=9/10/2009|birthPlace=In the warmth of my friends and family|species=Satan|gender=Boyish|height=6'2|weight=150|eyes=Green with a malevolent sparkle|race=White (Polish)|hair=Curly Dirty Blonde|image=Screenshot 2026-03-26 220642.png}} === Known Usernames: Freakasaurus Rex, DeinoChei, DeinoFreak, Freakemi, Ghost^3 DeinoFreak, Akemi, Akemi Kenzo, Kaori, DeinoCheirus-chan, Akemieye, Redhobbitman, Hobbitred, Redhobbitlord, Iwanttodothesillywithmeowcles, Larry Goat, Beasley. === '''Duncan''', commonly known as '''"The Devil"''', or simply '''"DeinoFreak"''', is a member of the [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People group chat]]. He was the founder of the [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]] server, and helped bring [[4 Only (orgin)|"The better 4"]] into [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM2025]] on the night of December 31st 2024. Duncan has been one of the most active members of Less People since the founding of [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM25]], especially seeing a spike in activity at the start of 2026 after the forming of [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM26]], being active almost everyday and being deemed '''"The Screensharer"'''. Formerly considered a dictator due to his rich history as a server owner in the origins of The better 4, he gave up his ownership powers to [[Teddy Pemberton]] when [[The Great Merger]] happened. Duncan was also a very important factor in [[The Great Merger]], being close friends with [[Matthew]], which upon Duncan's purchasing of a PC led to him being integrated into the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]], which kicked off [[4 Only (orgin)|4 only's]] involvement with the rest of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] and therefore The Great Merger. In the [[The Evans Split|Evans split]] to kick off high school, Duncan traveled with the [[McKinney High School|High group]], being unfortunately only himself, [[Daniel]], [[Vuke]], and what was going to be [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] before he moved away. Despite the lack of [[anomalies]] and overall interesting pupil at [[McKinney High School|High]], Duncan managed to create more than decent relationships with everyone at High. Duncan also helped introduce [[Miqdid|Miqdiddy]] to [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM25]], a high student. They are good friends at High, and share hobbies over [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]]. {{DEFAULTSORT:Duncan - The Devil}} [[Category:Characters]]</text>
<sha1>dzoi06rcthz2p0ua7ju3ugafimofjow</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Teddy Pemberton</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>161</id>
<revision>
<id>1176</id>
<parentid>1144</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-31T03:54:15Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>

</contributor>
<origin>1176</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="6228" sha1="siri720vghdnvtz0dzq82cg7f0lze2m" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Theodore Pemberton|aliases=Bug311|relatives=Gregory, Eliana|affiliation=Less People|birthDate=3/11/2010|birthPlace=McKinney|gender=Male|height=5'10|weight=120ib|eyes=Brown|image=Teddy sunglasses.jpg|imagecaption=Teddy at BoomerJack's on Brady's birthday|race=White|hair=Black/Dark Brown}}[[File:Teddy swing.jpg|thumb|Teddy in a swing at [[Finch Park]]]] '''Theodore Pemberton''', commonly nicknamed '''"Teddy"''', or known by his username '''"Bug311"''', is basically the leader and founder of the [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People friend group]]. Teddy has also been consistently one of the most active members of Less People. He was born on '''March 11, 2010''', in [[McKinney, Texas]] to the [[Pemberton]] family as the oldest amongst his siblings. Because of his birthday, the number [[311 (number)|311]] is synonymous with him. Teddy was a founding member of the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] of Less People, serving as the owner of various servers before and after [[The Great Merger|"The Great Merger"]]. Growing up, Teddy attended MISD schools including: Wolford Elementary, [[Evans Middle School]], [[McKinney Boyd High School]]'''.''' Teddy is well known for his kindness and generosity, with all of Less People being in his debt. == History == === Pre-School === After living in Frisco for a couple of years, Teddy moved to [[McKinney, Texas|McKinney]]. === Elementary School === After beginning Wolford Elementary in 2015, Teddy befriended Logan, River, Riley, and Jack, as well as future Less People members like Brady, Matthew, [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], and Christian. Teddy also joined cub scout Pack 909 alongside many of his friends in 1st grade. After school, he would hang with the [[Cul De Sac Gang]] alongside [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], the future loser of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. After the gang fell apart with [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] moving, Teddy's focus shifted to his Wolford friend group (Brady, Riley, Jack). Due to their living proximity, Teddy and Brady began commonly meeting at the nearby [[Willow Brook Park]], and also began playing Roblox at Brady's household. Here, Teddy was introduced to Blake. This however would also be short lived after Jack moved to Washington, but during the effort to stay in contact, Teddy and Brady both began using Discord. This led to Teddy creating [[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|"The Good Gang" (TGG)]] server in 2019, marking the beginning of what would become [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. During the [[COVID-19 Pandemic]], Teddy spent most of his time in this Server with Brady, Blake, and Vaughn. Teddy joined [[Troop 909]] at the end of 5th grade. === Middle School === Each year, Teddy was separated from his last year friends by his schedule, and Brady and Blake's sudden decline in activity during the school year left him very very lonely between the breaks outside of school. [[File:Teddy eagle baseball3.jpg|thumb|Freshman Teddy holding baseball on [[Bus 177]] - Taken by Brayden]] In 7th grade, Teddy joined [[Evans XC Team|Cross Country]] and Track to be with Brady more. This year would be the prime of Teddy's middle school years. In late 8th grade, Matthew would ask Teddy to gift him the game [[Lethal Company|"Lethal Company"]], as Brady had told him about it and he wanted to play with [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People]]. This led to the creation of the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]], which would start a sequence of events leading to [[The Great Merger]] in the summer of 8th grade. === High School === Teddy spent the majority of his Freshman year with [[Brayden]], whom he shared many classes with. His place on the [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|Boyd Cross Country team]] would lead him to befriend Ryan and Drew. Teddy and Brayden would become greatly associated with the [[Anomalies]] during this year. In Sophomore year, Teddy would reach the prime of his school social life, but this was overshadowed by his prioritization of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] due to his overwhelming love and support of his friends. He would consistently maintain daily activity in the [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM26 server]]. At the end of the school year, Teddy would earn his drivers license, and would get his first job at Cinemark over the summer. == Less People Group Chats == * '''[[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|The Good Gang (later TEO)]] :''' September 2019-March 2021 --- Brady, Blake, Vaughn, Brandon, Michael, Devin, * '''[[Post-TGG Discord Group Chat Era|Various lost discord GCs]] :''' April 2021-December 2021 --- Brady, Blake, Vaughn, Brandon, Michael, Riley * '''[[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] :''' December 2021-December 2023 --- Brady, Blake, Vaughn, Brandon, Michael, Riley * '''[[Lethal (Discord Server)|lethal]] :''' January 2024-April 2024 --- Brady, Matthew, Ellison, Sawyer, Duncan, Christian, Brayden, Blake, Vaughn, Rahand, Grant, Carmelo, Vuke, Alden, Riley, Daniel * '''[[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]] :''' April 2024-December 2024 --- Duncan, Brayden, Rahand, Daniel, Matthew, Christian, Brady, Blake, Vaughn * '''[[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]] :''' December 28 2024-December 31 2024 --- Duncan, Brayden, Rahand, Daniel, Matthew, Christian, Brady, Blake, Vaughn[[File:Teddy philosopher.png|thumb|189x189px|Teddy's facecam in call [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM25]] call]] * '''[[Milo's Masquerade (Discord Server)|Milo's Masquerade]] :''' December 31 2024 --- Blake * '''[[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]] :''' January 2025 --- December 2025 --- Duncan, Brayden, Rahand, Daniel, Matthew, Christian, Brady, Blake, Vaughn, Drew, Sawyer, Vuke * '''[[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2026]] :''' January 2026 --- December 2026 --- Duncan, Brayden, Rahand, Daniel, Matthew, Christian, Brady, Blake, Vaughn, Drew, Sawyer, Vuke, Mcdiddy == Usernames, Nicknames, Aliases == * '''Teddy''' * '''Bug''' ** Bug311 ** Bug_7137 ** OfficerBugg *'''[[Streamer University Names|Bumble Bleshlight Biltskin Pemberton]]''' [[Category:Characters]]</text>
<sha1>siri720vghdnvtz0dzq82cg7f0lze2m</sha1>
</revision>

<title>Brayden</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>165</id>
<revision>
<id>1000</id>
<parentid>999</parentid>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="6396" sha1="dx4kda21nn60lqtj93z9t5253xxb4c0" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Brayden|aliases=Shirley the sheep, Laclapbomb|relatives=Carter, an older sister, Ludwig|affiliation=Less People, 4 only, Yugioh|occupation=|birthDate=02/19/2010|birthPlace=Fridley, MN|gender=Male|height=6'0|weight=138 LBs|image=Brayden sewer tunnel.jpg|eyes=Green/Blue|imagecaption=Brayden In the tunnels of celina during the 2026 new years sleepover|hair=Brown|race=White}}[[File:Brayden Phone.jpg|thumb|Brayden holding his phone to his ear on bus 177, taken by [[Teddy Pemberton|teddy]]]] '''Brayden''', commonly known online as '''[[Shirley The Sheep|"Shirley the Sheep"]]''' is a member of the [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People group]], coming from the [[4 Only (orgin)|4 only side]] of less people, also known as the "Better 4", due to its significantly higher activity rates throughout its members compared to the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] of Less People. Being born in Minnesota, and moving to Texas in 2021, Brayden was not involved in the earliest stages of either side of the less people friend group, with earliest involvement dating back to late 2021. He has still cemented himself as a notable member through 2021-Present day Less People. He spent most of his early years with Less People talking with [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and becoming friends with the rest of less people after [[The Great Merger|"The Great Merger"]] during the siege era. He currently Attends [[McKinney Boyd High School|Mckinney Boyd High School]] along with [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Blake Jones|Blake]], and [[Matthew]] following [[The Evans Split|the Evans split]]. Brayden's original surname wasn't 'Watne', but the first surname is now lost. His older brother, [[Ludwig]], changed it and then wiped his families memory. == History == === Early Life === [[File:Brayden baby.jpg|left|thumb|180x180px|A young Brayden standing in the downstairs floor of his humble home in Minnesota.]] Brayden was born on February 19th, 2010. He grew up alongside his older brother, [[Carter]]. Before all the fame, Brayden spent the first 11 years of his life living in Brooklyn Park, Minnesota, where he attended Woodland Elementary during his elementary school years. It was in this period he would find his love for ice hockey, which he would play from 2015-Present day. === Middle School === Due to many factors, Brayden and his family would move to [[McKinney, Texas|Mckinney, Texas]] during the summer of 2021. It was then when he would enroll in Evans Middle School, Where he would go on to meet everyone In less people. in 6th grade, Brayden had little to no interaction with less people, besides a conversation here and there with [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], and joining a discord group chat with [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], who he met via '''Austin Northcut.''' 7th grade was a pivotal moment for Brayden, who shared a math class with [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], right before their lunch period. During the early year, Brayden would undergo ACL surgery, and would require for the next 2 weeks that someone help carry his stuff between periods, where he would elect [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] to fill this roll. They would go on to manipulate Ms. James into letting them leave 10 minutes early to lunch everyday for the rest of the year, where Duncan would famously say '''"Its time".''' [[Brayden]] would then sit at [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand's]] lunch table, and become good friends with him and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], also joining their discord group chat to play Fortnite and Dragon Blox. In 8th grade, Brayden would meet [[Matthew]] through [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and play [[Fortnite]] with him, but would not become good friends with him or the rest of less people until later in the year, when [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] moved to [[Thornton, Colorado|Colorado]], forcing Duncan and Brayden to seek refuge at the other [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] of Less people, where he would then meet everyone. He would not become good friends with them until his freshman year of high school, where he became close with [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Matthew]], and later meeting [[Blake Jones|Blake]] through Less People Hangouts. === High School === Freshman year was a pivotal year in Braydens connection to Less People, as he would become good friends with [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Matthew]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Blake Jones|Blake]] throughout their various classes together, and Less people discord calls using the [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]] and [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM25]] servers. Brayden shared the most classes with [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], with the most notable being 2nd Period Geometry, where the two of them would spend countless hours playing [[Getaway Shootout]], and 7th Period biology where many jokes would be made, such as the drawings of the [[Anomalies]], and need a hand. The A lunch period was also very important as it resulted in the creating of the [[Anomalies]]. == Less People Group Chats == Multiple different Group chats, most notably being [[Absolute Value Symbol (Discord Group Chat)|"|"]] with [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] (and very little [[Vuke]] and [[Daniel]]) : 2022-2023 [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal]] : January 2024 - April 2024 [[File:Brayden mars.jpg|thumb|240x240px|Brayden on top of the Celina construction site mountain during the 2026 New years sleepover]] [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]] (Formerly named Sea of thieves) : April 2024 - December 2024 [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM25]] : January 2025 - December 2025 [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM26]] : January 2026 - Present [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]] : December 28 2024 - December 31 2024 [[Milo's Masquerade (Discord Server)|Milo's Masquerade]] : December 31 2024 [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People +]] / Less People + D2 : late 2024 (Earlier activity before Brayden's Joining) == Usernames, Nicknames, Aliases == * '''Shirley The Sheep''' ** Shirley ** Ghost Shirley ** Shirley The Sheep Watne ** ShirleyTheSheep2 * '''Laclapbombbardownski''' ** Laclapbomb * '''Soupdog098''' ** Soup * '''Tryhardquesadilla''' * '''1Letters'''</text>
<sha1>dx4kda21nn60lqtj93z9t5253xxb4c0</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<title>Vaughn Lee Rutherford</title>
<ns>0</ns>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2465" sha1="hmskhr9dgcv7za5y0rrnrmyibc8q5qi" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Vaughn Rutherford|aliases=TIGER_TANK, Vonkmad27|birthDate=1/10/2010|gender=Male|height=5'10|weight=110ib|eyes=Brown|image=Vaughn foward.png|relatives=Jackie, Lauren|race=White-Asian (Korean)|hair=Black}} '''Vaughn Lee Rutherford''' is known as the "Loser of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]". Born on January 10th, 2010, he is [[Mistreatment of Vaughn|commonly dispised]] by many for his taunting, simple minded, and troubled personality. Despite this, Vaughn has immense dedication to his hobbys. He is currently obsessed with [[Vaughn Gym Arc|going to the gym]]. When this is combined with his fast learning talent, he can quickly adapt and grow in skill. He currently lives in [[Celina, Texas]], but he previously lived in [[McKinney, Texas|McKinney]] before moving in 2018. He comes form the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], after his inception to [[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|The Good Gang server]] in 2020 through [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. He has since remained a consistent and iconic member of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], despite his controversial image. == History == [[File:Vaughn juan two big cocks.png|thumb|279x279px|Vaughn in a discord call]] === McKinney === Starting out in [[McKinney, Texas|McKinney]], Vaughn attended Wolford Elementary from 2015-2018, where he befriended [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] in the [[cul de sac gang]]. Around this time his parents would become divorced. He would move away to Allen in 2018. === The Good Gang === [[File:Vaughn gripping head.png|thumb|Vaughn at [[Finch Park]] in some weird position]]After moving away, Vaughn would revisit [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] sometime in 2020. At the time, Teddy had recently created [[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|The Good Gang discord server]] with Brady and Blake, and he invited Vaughn to join the server. He would soon become a core part of the main four [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] members. He would move to [[Celina, Texas|Celina]] in 2021. == Fun Facts == * Vaughn was almost named "Christian" or "Travers". == Nicknames, Usernames, Aliases == See also: [[Vaughn (accounts)|Vaughn accounts]] * '''Von''' * '''[[Vonk]]''' ** '''Vonkmad27''' (Steam & Minecraft Username) ** '''Vonky''' (Steam display name) * '''TIGER_TANK''' * '''artic ships''' * '''roblox_player''' * '''coolrichmank56''' (past Roblox username) * '''coolrobloxguy5002''' (current Roblox username)</text>
<sha1>hmskhr9dgcv7za5y0rrnrmyibc8q5qi</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Post-TGG Discord Group Chat Era</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>177</id>
<revision>

<origin>236</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1818" sha1="mxv5qbvc573mrbo1cjmfe4pp204fqrq" xml:space="preserve">An era of the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] mainly between April 2021 and December 2021, but technically lasting up until December 2023. Following the dying of the The Good Gang discord server. The ex-TGG members would abuse the discord group chat feature, making upwards to hundreds group chats with random names. The era ended with the creation of [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] in late December 2021, which would mark the end of the majority of group chat spam slop. When it came time to actually call, the group (commonly consisting of [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], Brady, Blake, Vaughn, Brandon, Ellison, and Michael) would often settle for a random one. Sometimes they would stick to one gc for long periods of time, while other times they would switch between multiple in one day. The main reason for switching was due to power struggles and admin abuse, which was very common at the time. The era ended after one of the group chats, known as [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]], ended up sticking for 2 years with minimal deviations. [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] would remain the primary GC until December 2023, before being replaced by the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]]. Despite this, it would still be occasionally used up to October 2024. == List of Known GC from this era == * Bug311's Group * Cool people * Pizza, artic ships, gosi21 * THE VONK CHAT * Bedwars * Von doing dumb stuff * Sea of Thieves * Brady doing stuff * brady does bad stuff * TEDDY DOES BAD BAD STUFF * MUCK MUCK MUCK MUCK MUCK * DOLLRO DOLLOR * Less People 2.0 * Less People 3.0 * Less People 4.0 * less people bot * Anarchy Less People * Less People 5.0 * Less People 5.1 * Less People 5.0b * Commonwealth * [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]]</text>
<sha1>mxv5qbvc573mrbo1cjmfe4pp204fqrq</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>179</id>
<revision>
<id>1292</id>
<parentid>1060</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-10-28T22:16:12Z</timestamp>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="3252" sha1="0glhb7lzcivydhvzmoepkix5euv29gr" xml:space="preserve">''not to be confused with the general [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People friend group]].'' '''Less People+''' was a [[List of Discord Servers and GC|discord group chat]] owned by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] during the [[Post-TGG Discord Group Chat Era]] created on '''Christmas, 2021'''. It served as the primary group chat for the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] for 2 years before being replaced by the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]]. Its prime was from January 2022-December 2023. == History == === Creation === Created on December 25, 2021, it was originally named "Crab Game Squad", as it was first made for Crab Game and [[Muck]], before it was renamed "Less People+" the next day. The original members consist of [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], Vaughn, Brady, Blake, and Michael. === Vaughn Account Deletion === In March 2022, Vaughn's discord account "[[Vaughn (accounts)|roblox_player]]" was banned due to being underage. === First Brady-Vaughn Drama === During June 2022, the primary members returned to [[Raft Playthroughs|Raft]] to complete the game after the full game released. During this time, [[First Brady-Vaughn Drama|Brady blocked Vaughn]] after he ragebaited him with the in-game PVP. === Lethal & Fortnite OG Revival === Brady and Blake would often ghost the group chat during the school year, so whole group chat only got on during major breaks like Winter Break or Summer Break. This pattern was broken after the return of Fortnite OG, causing Brady and Blake to return mid school year for the first time since the [[TEO (Discord Server)|TGG server]]. After the end of Fortnite OG, Teddy asked Brady at school to try [[Lethal Company]]. This prevented Brady and Blake from falling back into obscurity. === Ending === The group chat moved to the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]] January 2024, after Matthew some of his friends wanted to play [[Lethal Company]]. It was fully abandoned by the end of October 2024. == [[Less People+ D2 (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+ D2]] == Because of the 10 member limit in discord group chats, when [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] and [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] were converging in late 2024, a second Less People was created to fit more members without removing old members from the original. The full name is "less.people+ D2 - The hidden dark side of less people that Blake doesn’t want you to know about 🤫" == Members == === Current === * '''43old''' (Blake) * '''43oldsfootballfusionacc''' (Blake) * '''[[Vaughn (accounts)|artic ships]]''' ([[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]) * '''DeinoFreak''' ([[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]) * '''Bug311''' ([[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]) (owner) * '''Dani G. German''' (Christian) * '''hu''' (Riley) * '''ilymilo''' (Matthew) * '''Mrooshoo''' (Michael) * '''Pizza''' (Brady) === Former === * '''Gosi21''' (Brandon) * '''Marioawesome''' (Daniel) * '''Shirley the Sheep''' ([[Brayden]]) * '''43old2''' (Blake) * '''Buff Bass''' ([[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]]) * '''Evergreenmr''' (Ellison) * '''roblox_player''' (Vaughn) == Games == * Crab Game * Muck * [[Raft Playthroughs|Raft]] * Minecraft * Sea of Thieves * Fortnite (OG) * [[Lethal Company]] === Roblox === * Rise of Nations * Flicker [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>
<sha1>0glhb7lzcivydhvzmoepkix5euv29gr</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>The Great Merger</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>181</id>
<revision>
<id>707</id>
<parentid>476</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:25:49Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>707</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="3703" sha1="61p5avkrhbxaulp6ff860243s4bt2ag" xml:space="preserve">The gradual merging of the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] and [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only side]] during 2024, creating the [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People friend group]]. The merging was initiated after Matthew brought the two sides together in the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]], but it accelerated when the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]] came to the [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh server]]. After filtering out the switch-ups from [[Lethal (Discord Server)|lethal]], they bonded by playing Rainbow Six Siege. The merging accumulated in Buff Bass's Server and [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]], marking the completion of the merger. == Lethal Stage == After [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] bought Matthew and Sawyer [[Lethal Company]], the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal discord]] was created. After the birth of the server, it would quickly grow as the server expanded beyond Lethal Company and into other games like Football Fusion 2, Basketball Legends, and Siege. Around this time, Matthew would bring [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] into the server, causing the first true mixing of [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] and [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] beyond Brady. Around this time, the Evans lunch table also formed with mostly people from the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]]. Despite this, [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] and [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] would remain mainly separate as tensions were still high and interactions were still mostly unfriendly. == Less People D2 Stage == After the dying of [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal]], a [[Less People+ D2 (Discord Group Chat)|discord group chat]] was created by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] with both [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] and [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]], mostly to play [[Football Fusion 2|Football Fusion]], [[Lethal Company]], and [[Rainbow Six Siege]]. The group chat would later be named [[Less People+ D2 (Discord Group Chat)|"less.people D2"]]. This is when the two sides first integrated with each other without other people. Despite this, Duncan still kept [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]] seperated, demonstrating how the two friend groups were still seen as separated. The fact that [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] was much newer to [[Rainbow Six Siege|Siege]] than [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] also resulted in segregation within the stacks. Otherwise, this was the first time [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] was recognizable. == Yugioh Stage == After being added, [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] would gradually move to [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]]. This is where the two sides really began to converge as the distinction between the two disappeared due to the exchange of games, history, and culture. This is when Less People really first formed as one uniform friend group, as the two sides recognized each other as actually being apart of the same group. This is also when [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] embraced the Less People name from the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] as their own. By the end of 2024, [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] was undoubtably united. == Buff Bass's Server & new year new me 2025 Stage == At the end of 2024, a new server was created to get away from [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] admin abuse. This would be called [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]], owned by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. This server would also quickly devolve into admin abuse due to the [[Rukles]]. On January 1st, 2025, [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] moved to the brand new [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]] server, marking the completion of the merger. [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>61p5avkrhbxaulp6ff860243s4bt2ag</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1728" sha1="dwt0fsymy6jj4c6hf0glw8imy2b1ahm" xml:space="preserve">refers to the side of Less People before [[The Great Merger]] consisting of the members of The Good Gang discord server before and after the servers usage. It began in 2019 and ended in 2024 after merging with the [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only side]] to form the [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People friend group]]. == Members == === Primary === * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] * Brady * Blake * Vaughn === Occasional === * Michael * Brandon (Gosi21) * Riley * Ellison == History == In September 2019, the The Good Gang discord server was created by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], including Brady, Blake, and later Vaughn, Michael, Brandon, and multiple others. After the decline of [[TEO (Discord Server)|The Good Gang server]], the friend group moved into the [[Post-TGG Discord Group Chat Era]], with it finally settling down in [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] in late December 2021. The group was mixed in with For Only and Carmelo's friends in the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal server]] in January 2024. The group joined [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh server]] in September 2024, where [[The Great Merger]] happened & TGG merged with For Only to form the current [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People friend group]]. == Group Chats == * '''[[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|The Good Gang (Server)]]''' - September 2019-March 2021 * '''[[Post-TGG Discord Group Chat Era|Various lost discord GCs]]''' - April 2021-December 2021 * '''[[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]]''' - December 2021-December 2023 * '''[[Lethal (Discord Server)|lethal]]''' - January 2024-April 2024 * '''[[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]]''' - April 2024-December 2024</text>
<sha1>dwt0fsymy6jj4c6hf0glw8imy2b1ahm</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>First Brady-Vaughn Drama</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>183</id>
<revision>
<id>697</id>
<parentid>259</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T21:10:24Z</timestamp>
<contributor>

<text bytes="388" sha1="ch64j9kmich9wvmwak46v3xcknc19xw" xml:space="preserve">Happened in either July 2021 or 2022 (likely the latter) during one of the [[Raft Playthroughs|Raft playthroughs]]. After ragebaiting Brady in Raft by killing him with spears, spawn camping him, and stealing his items, Brady blocked Vaughn. This went on for about a week before Blake convinced him to unblock Vaughn so [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] could resume Raft. [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>ch64j9kmich9wvmwak46v3xcknc19xw</sha1>

<page>
<title>Raft Playthroughs</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>184</id>
<revision>
<id>900</id>
<parentid>257</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-06-15T09:17:22Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>900</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1917" sha1="btr9brj4nvs8kqsw5db6sg27f7a2djz" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Raft screenshot 2022.png|thumb|336x336px|Screenshot of Blake and Vaughn during the 2022 Second Playthrough.]] During June 2021 and June 2022, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], Brady, Blake, and Vaughn played through Raft, a survival game based around building a raft in a flooded post-apocalypse world. == First & Second Playthroughs == The first playthrough in June 2021 happened prior to the full release of the game. This took place in an unknown GC during the [[Post-TGG Discord Group Chat Era]]. The second playthrough happened a year later in June 2022, in [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]]. After the full game released, the four returned to the game to complete the story. == Somalians == The Rafters captured a lama and it got nicknamed "Somalia" by Brady and Teddy as an inside joke. Other goats and lamas captured were also called Somalians and were kept in a pen as pets, known as the "Somalian Pen". The original Somalia was lost by Vaughn when entering the Utopia. After finding the entry hatch to the Utopia, everyone agreed to go to bed and do the Utopia the next day. As everyone was about to get off, Vaughn ignored this and rushed the hatch with Somalia in his hands. After opening the hatch, water flooded the room, ensuing chaos which caused Brady to close the world. When we rebooted the world, Somalia was gone. == Toxicity in PVP & Brady-Vaughn Drama == During the world creation, PVP was enabled. Vaughn abused this, resulting in a constant back-and-forth revolving around killing, jumping, spawn camping beds, throwing bodies in the ocean, and stealing items. This amounted in[[First Brady-Vaughn Drama|Brady blocking Vaughn]] after he ragebaited him with the in-game PVP. == /\ Naming == Brady, Vaughn, and Teddy would change their in-game name to "/" or "\", or a combination of the two. This was done to confuse and anger Blake and each other. [[Category:Games]]</text>
<sha1>btr9brj4nvs8kqsw5db6sg27f7a2djz</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="592" sha1="7m5uyu7elisge47qf71yk7cusswp360" xml:space="preserve">Friend slop horror game that was popular in late 2024 based on collecting scrap and avoiding monsters. [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] played the game after [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] advertised it to Brady in school as "Space Muck". After Brady told them about it, Matthew, Sawyer, and some others wanted to get in on it. After [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] bought them the game, the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal discord server]] was created. This would later incite [[The Great Merger]]. In order to bypass the 4 max player count, r2modman was used. [[Category:Games]]</text>

<text bytes="494" sha1="3cbmwf7vp592r9jlrlysaewvn7bbe0w" xml:space="preserve">'''The Good Gang''' '''|''' '''Official Discord''' was the first [[List of Discord Servers and GC|Discord server]] in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. <text bytes="488" sha1="tlba7oa52spubv5djn0d82oelt4puok" xml:space="preserve">'''Yugioh''' was a discord server created by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]. originally called '''"Sea of Thieves"''', it was initially for [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] to play Sea of Thieves. It later shifted to play the card game Yugioh, and then again to [[Rainbow Six Siege|Siege]] after [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] came. After [[Lethal (Discord Server)|lethal]] began to die, [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] began moved to Yugioh, continuing [[The Great Merger]]. [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>
<sha1>tlba7oa52spubv5djn0d82oelt4puok</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="612" sha1="8v3p8xu36td4pqhvucusx38nwc55yap" xml:space="preserve">refers to the side of Less People before [[The Great Merger]] consisting of Duncan, [[Brayden]], Rahand, Vuke, Daniel. It began in 2021 and ended in 2024 after merging with the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] side to form the Less People friend group. == Members == === Primary === * [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] * [[Brayden]] * Rahand * Daniel * Vuke === Occasional === * Matthew == Group Chats == * '''[[Four Only (Discord Server)|4 Only (server)]]''' - ??? * '''[[Lethal (Discord Server)|lethal]]''' - January 2024-April 2024 * '''[[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]]''' - February 2024-December 2024</text>

<text bytes="617" sha1="lhawut3wwj0qykh0fppx86uv8leihf3" xml:space="preserve">[[File:4 only pfp.jpg|thumb|Server Icon for 4 only server]] '''4 only''' was a server which was used mainly from August 1 2023 - December 18-2023. It was created to merge [[Goofy Lil Goobers (Discord Server)|goofy lil goobers]], which at the time included [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], and [[Brayden]], with the group chat with Duncan, Rahand, and Vuke. It was used throughout early 8th grade before moving on to [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Sea of Thieves (Renamed later to Yugioh🥰).]] The server was used mainly to call and play games such as fortnite and various roblox games.</text>
<sha1>lhawut3wwj0qykh0fppx86uv8leihf3</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Less People (Friend Group)</title>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1666" sha1="02uj5ygvgubqu4rmwnjx9fbkk4wfgo4" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Less people minecraft movie.jpg|thumb|337x337px|(left to right) [[Brayden]], [[Christian]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] at [[Cinemark]] for the [[Minecraft Movie Meetup|Minecraft Movie]].]] [[File:Less people top golf quarter zips.jpg|thumb|285x285px|(left to right) [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Brayden]], [[Christian]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] at [[Topgolf Allen|Topgolf]] wearing [[quarterzips]].]] The '''Less People''' friend group was formed after [[The Great Merger]] of [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] and [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] in 2024. == Etymology == The name originates from the TGG side in 2021. After the [[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|TGG server]] died, making randomly named discord GCs became a daily practice, known as the [[Post-TGG Discord Group Chat Era]]. The name Less People comes from the later part of this era, from a series of discord GC's with the names "Less People", such as [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] and Less People 2.0. These group chats were created to be variants of other group chats like "Cool People", but with less people as the name suggests. == Members == ''[[Members of Less People|List of Less People Members]]'' === Primary === * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] * [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] * [[Brayden]] * [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] * [[Brady Jones|Brady]] * [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] * [[Daniel]] * [[Blake Jones|Blake]] * [[Christian]] * [[Matthew]] === Occasional Cameo's === * Vuke * Drew * Mcdiddy (deceased) * Mason * Sawyer * Michael * Riley</text>
<sha1>02uj5ygvgubqu4rmwnjx9fbkk4wfgo4</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<title>Rahand - The Muslim</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>193</id>

<id>1281</id>

<timestamp>2026-10-17T20:49:48Z</timestamp>

<origin>1281</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1695" sha1="2wez6u3yk32gfkw89372xvzod0kk4j9" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Rahand|birthDate=1/7/2010|birthPlace=Sulaymaniyah, Kurdistan|race=Kurdish|gender=Male|height=5'9|weight=180|eyes=Brown|hair=Black|image=rahand.png|relatives=Lawand, a younger sister|aliases=Buff Bass, ScrappyMammal}}'''Rahand''', also known as "'''Buff Bass'''" or "'''ScrappyMammal'''" online, is a kurdish muslim who currently lives in [[Thornton, Colorado]]. Born on '''January 7th, 2010''' in Kurdistan, he spent most of his childhood in [[McKinney, Texas|McKinney Texas]], where he joined [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. He has been strictly message-only since the [[BBC or Twink Incident]]. Before [[The Great Merger|the Merger]], he became apart of the [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only side]] after befriending [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] at [[Evans Middle School]]. His face is an iconic symbol of the [[Less People (YouTube Channel)|Less People YouTube channel]] and [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM2025]], with him sometimes being called '''"the mascot"''' of Less People. He also currently owns and runs the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia MC server]]. He has a very mathematically-based intelligence, showing interest in engineering. He claims to hold high academic success. He was seen as somewhat contraversial due to his pedantic [[Rahand Break Controversies|"breaks"]] from Less People when he was upset with it's members, often citing ragebait and tense emotions. == Nicknames, Usernames, Aliases == * '''ScrappyMammal''' * '''Buff Bass''' * '''Lawandurahand''' or just '''Law''' (Roblox) * '''Cool cat''' * '''rsmzxz''' (Minecraft username) * '''Ghost Rahford Lee Watne''' (Discord nickname assigned to him) [[Category:Characters]]</text>
<sha1>2wez6u3yk32gfkw89372xvzod0kk4j9</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1759" sha1="3dhikmiy8u9p3dzmvcqqo31i83xs0mq" xml:space="preserve">A list of accounts and usernames belonging to [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. == Discord == === roblox_player (DELETED) === Vaughn's original discord account created sometime in 2019. It was deleted on March 15, 2022 for being underage. === _tiger_tank === Vaughn's current discord account, created on March 15, 2022 immediately after the banning of his old account. Previously named "TIGER_TANK#0198". === artic ships (nickname) === A common nickname that was used on both his old and new accounts. It originates from [[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|The Good Gang server]], as a reference to a girl named "Artic" who agreed [[Vaughn-Eleven Ship|Vaughn should be shipped with Eleven]] (The character from Stranger Things). This was a big inside joke at the time. == Roblox == === coolrobloxguy5002<ref>https://www.roblox.com/users/519346083/profile?friendshipSourceType=PlayerSearch</ref> === Vaughn's current Roblox account, created on February 14th, 2018. He was previously named "coolrichmank56" === topvaughn2010roblox<ref>https://www.roblox.com/users/312992033/profile?friendshipSourceType=PlayerSearch</ref> === Vaughn's lost Roblox account, created on June 2nd, 2017. It was lost after he forgot the password. === vaughnindallas2010<ref>https://www.roblox.com/users/1609935677/profile</ref> === Vaughn's old alt account, created on May 4th, 2020. == Steam == === Vonky<ref>https://steamcommunity.com/profiles/76561199126495690</ref> === Vaughn's current Steam account, created on January 4th, 2021. It has 104 games on it. His most played game is Rainbow Six Siege, with over 2300 hours. Previously named "Vonkmad27". == Minecraft == === Vonkmad27 === Vaughn's Minecraft account since 2021. UUID: 2b9143d5-7f9c-44a6-a57d-8fad05598092</text>
<sha1>3dhikmiy8u9p3dzmvcqqo31i83xs0mq</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1584" sha1="nnxxkvz05yux7wx3fb8rjymbbs8c849" xml:space="preserve">McKinney, also known as "Rape City", is a city located in North Texas, part of the DFW Metroplex and Collin County. This is the residency for the majority of Less People members. Celina is it's number one enemy. Established on March 16th, 1848, it was named after Collin McKinney, one of the signers of the Texas Declaration of Independence. Today, it is the 3rd largest city in Collin County and it's county seat. It boasts a population of about 213,000 people, making it the 15th largest city in Texas by population. == Migrations == In 2014, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] moved from Frisco to McKinney, Texas. In 2020, [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] moved from McKinney to [[Celina, Texas|Celina, Texas.]] In 2021, [[Brayden]] moved from Brooklyn Park, Minnesota to Mckinney, Texas In 2024, [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] moved from McKinney to [[Thornton, Colorado]]. == McKinney ISD == McKinney Independent School District is the district that controls the vast majority of McKinney's student population. It contains 3 high schools, and 5 Middle Schools. === High Schools === * [[McKinney High School]] * [[McKinney Boyd High School]] * McKinney North High School === Middle Schools === * [[Evans Middle School]] * Cockrill Middle School * Dowell Middle School * Faubion Middle School * Scott Johnson Middle School == Residency == * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] * [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] * [[Brayden]] * Matthew * Daniel * [[Brady Jones|Brady]] * [[Blake Jones|Blake]] * Christian (Occasionally) * Drew * Sawyer * Mcdiddy [[Category:Locations]] [[Category:Cities]]</text>
<sha1>nnxxkvz05yux7wx3fb8rjymbbs8c849</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<title>43 (number)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>197</id>
<revision>
<id>1095</id>
<parentid>800</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T19:29:00Z</timestamp>

<origin>1095</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="806" sha1="o69axgbnetcd1hmoyqacc7yn1p4menl" xml:space="preserve">''from [[List of Significant Numbers]].'' '''43''' is a significant number & inside joke to [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] due to it's association with [[Blake Jones|Blake]]. It's reversal, 34, is associated with pornographic media. == Meaning == The number stems from "[[Blake (accounts)|43old]]", the username of Blake's Roblox account. Since it's inception, it has also become his username on various other platforms, such as Discord. The name "[[Blake (accounts)|43old]]" was created on July 25, 2019, when Brady and Blake thought it would be funny to troll on Roblox. Although originally being Blake's ironic alt account, it became his main account after the [[Blake Roblox Account Hijacking|hacking of his previous account.]] [[Category:Significant Numbers]] [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>o69axgbnetcd1hmoyqacc7yn1p4menl</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="419" sha1="0kth9jhzhcb1b9b62qepizeqc06l19t" xml:space="preserve">''from [[List of Significant Numbers]].'' <ns>0</ns>
<id>200</id>
<revision>
<id>809</id>

<text bytes="451" sha1="qcscs9hbyrb2nv5ljuwr6ogvyb9k6cb" xml:space="preserve">In 2019, Blakes Roblox account "hithereandwelcome" was hijacked. The culprit was likely in relation with one of Blake's friends, whom he had foolishly shared his password with. After the hacking, Blake was forced to move to his satirical "[[Blake (accounts)|43old]]" account he had recently created. This would later result in the inside joke of "[[43 (number)|43]]" in the [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People friend group]]. [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>qcscs9hbyrb2nv5ljuwr6ogvyb9k6cb</sha1>
</revision>

<page>
<title>42 (number)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>201</id>
<revision>
<id>1096</id>
<parentid>303</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T19:29:13Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1096</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="654" sha1="nmj7dbqsla443909u4fqxyf85876wo0" xml:space="preserve">''from [[List of Significant Numbers]].'' '''42''' is an inside joke mostly important to [[Brayden]] in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] due to it's association with [[43 (number)|43]] and popular culture. It is titled the '''"Less People Forgotten Number"''' == Meaning == The number 42 comes from a meme where 68 is dubbed "The Forgotten Number" because both 67 and 69 became major memes in internet culture. Because 41 was also previously a popular meme, and [[43 (number)|43]] is a inside joke in Less People, it is considered to be Less People's version of the forgotten number. [[Category:Significant Numbers]] [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>nmj7dbqsla443909u4fqxyf85876wo0</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
