<text bytes="635" sha1="9qytijbgi9381ba4831o8ovd8igwut6" xml:space="preserve">''from [[List of Significant Numbers]].'' '''27''' is a significant number & inside joke to [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] due to it's association with [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. == Meaning == The number derives from [[Vaughn (accounts)|"Vonkmad27"]], a username made up by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] for [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] when he was making his Minecraft and Steam account. The number itself holds no significant meaning and was entirely randomly picked. It may also now be associated with '''MasonJarz27''', [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand's]] friend who later came with coincidently the same number.</text>
<sha1>9qytijbgi9381ba4831o8ovd8igwut6</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1119" sha1="ni0ofsjswptvd7gbf4rxcrj5us0qvm8" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Duncan mars.jpg|thumb|258x258px|[[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] on the mountain at the [[Second Celina Sleepover]]]] Big dirt mountain in a [[Celina, Texas|Celina]] construction site. At both the [[First Celina Sleepover|first]] and [[Second Celina Sleepover|second]] [[List of Sleepovers|Celina Sleepovers]], [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] scaled the mountain and took photos. The mountain was noticeably flatter on the [[Second Celina Sleepover|second visit]]. The mountain was also commonly mistaken for Mars. The site can be entered through a broken fence and a big opening. == Weird Sticky Plants == There are weird sticky thorn plant things that get stuck all over your clothes. Many of these got stuck in [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] pants, forcing him to take them off. == Wasp Nests == The fence opening had a giant wasp nest on it, but nobody noticed this on the way in. == Role == The role [[RIP mt con celina tx (role)|"construction site mountain got leveled, thats too bad ig. RIP mt con celina tx or some dumbshit liat"]] references this mountain getting leveled. [[Category:Locations]]</text>
<sha1>ni0ofsjswptvd7gbf4rxcrj5us0qvm8</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="506" sha1="l4n16tiz1dechrjc0yts235o2n647jr" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Betty Ann Rutherford|relatives=Vaughn, Lauren, Jackie|race=Asian (Korean)|gender=Female|hair=Black}} '''Betty Ann Rutherford''' is [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] mom. She is divorced and has 3 kids: Lauren, [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], and Jackie. She notably hosted the first [[List of Sleepovers|two sleepovers]], and kicked [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] out at the [[Second Celina Sleepover|second]]. She lives in [[Celina, Texas|Celina]]. [[Category:Characters]]</text>
<sha1>l4n16tiz1dechrjc0yts235o2n647jr</sha1>

<text bytes="611" sha1="mo8k45hg5jkxj833w3ooxl7ypppvblo" xml:space="preserve">'''Celina''', also known as '''"Consent city"''', is a city located in North Texas. Part of Collin county, it is just Northwest of [[McKinney, Texas|McKinney]] (It's primary enemy). It has a tiny population of about [[43 (number)|43]],717 people. It is thanked for containing [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. == Points of Intrest == * [[Betty Rutherford|Vaughn's Mom's]] House * [[Celina Construction Site Mountain|The Construction Site Mountain]] * The Police Station * The Tunnels == Residency == * [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] * [[Betty Rutherford|Betty]] [[Category:Cities]] [[Category:Locations]]</text>
<sha1>mo8k45hg5jkxj833w3ooxl7ypppvblo</sha1>

<text bytes="521" sha1="7zv0ph7tshxyukowr185ssr0fez4sni" xml:space="preserve">'''Daniel Huddleson''' is a chemistry teacher at [[McKinney Boyd High School|McKinney Boyd]]. He is very popular among his students as he is good at teaching chemistry while making his lessons entertaining. He is important to [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less people]] as he teaches 6th period chemistry with [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and 7th period chemistry with [[Brayden]], Brady, and Matthew. His room is also the site of many events during his flex time, Mr Huddleston's Chemistry Tutoring. [[Category:Characters]]</text>

<text bytes="472" sha1="c5u9sm8lma1h6tjx7fh4d0le97ldf3g" xml:space="preserve">'''McKinney Boyd High School''', or '''Boyd''' for short, is one of three high schools located in [[McKinney, Texas|McKinney]], Texas. <page>
<title>Getaway Shootout</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>213</id>
<revision>
<id>394</id>
<parentid>393</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-03-29T19:27:36Z</timestamp>

</contributor>
<comment>/* Characters */</comment>
<origin>394</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2132" sha1="3hc19v5n32oz9vn584m9ys7368c5z1a" xml:space="preserve">A 2D mobile-style game about racing to the end of a level & shooting your opponents. It was notably played by [[Brayden]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] in Hoyt's 2nd period geometry during their Freshman year at [[McKinney Boyd High School]]. == Mr. Rump == '''Mr. Rump''' is a character in Getaway Shootout based off of Donald Trump. He is best known for his helicopter escape, where he famously holds his ear with relief as he fly's off into the sunset. He would later be incarnated as an [[Anomalies|anomaly]]. == Skeleton's Head == Getaway Shootout is a split screen game. Once, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]] were playing and Brayden mistakenly played looking at Teddy's screen for half the game. Due to their refined and sharpened skill, their movements were in sync enough for Brayden to not notice. It wasn't until Teddy jumped off the skeleton's head and Brayden famously spoke "I just jumped off the Skeleton's head!" that he released his mistake. As it turns out, Brayden was still at the start and had been banging his head against the first obstacle. == Speedrunning == Due to the game getting too easy for [[Brayden]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], they began running the time trials mode to optimize the levels. Brayden famously mastered Plane, and Teddy was pretty good at Train. They tried to learn a skip they found on YouTube in the launch site map that allows you to jump up the tower from the start and skips half the level, but it was to no avail. == Characters == * Standard Steve * Standard Sarah * '''Stalker''' * Fat Man * The General * Harry Water * Indiana Loans * Ramflow * Astronomer * Bus Driver * Camper * Chauffuer * Chef Boy RPG * Dan * Flight Attendant * Hot Dog Bob * Hot Dog Worker * '''The Skeleton (Janitor Dan)''' * '''Panini Pete''' * Panini Worker * Parachuter * Pilot * Rocket Engineer * Truck Driver * Vorg General * Vorg Worker * Window Cleaner * Lebron (Baller) * Messi (Futballista) * Bigfoot * Doge * Ghost * Albert The Monkey * Mr. Narwhal * Hilldog * Rocket Man * '''Mr. Rump''' == Maps == * Tower * Marketplace * Bus * Train * Airport * Plane * Launch Site * Moon</text>
<sha1>3hc19v5n32oz9vn584m9ys7368c5z1a</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1248" sha1="175btl0o4bfh0wr7fsn6zr85yravv5o" xml:space="preserve">Role from [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2026]]. Full name: "construction site mountain got leveled, thats too bad ig. RIP mt con celina tx or some dumbshit liat" == Meaning == Refers to the [[Celina Construction Site Mountain]], which was visited in the [[First Celina Sleepover|First]] and [[Second Celina Sleepover|Second]] [[List of Sleepovers|Celina Sleepovers]]. On the [[First Celina Sleepover|first visit]], the [[Celina Construction Site Mountain|mountain]] was large and treacherous to climb. On the second visit, it was much flatter and shorter. Sadly, about a month after the last sleepover, [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] would confirm to [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] that the mountain was fully leveled in call. This role was made to honor it immediately after this news was recieved. == Holders == * '''43old''' (Blake) * '''Dani G. German''' (Christian) * '''Fraudizza''' (Brady) * '''Ghost Bug311''' ([[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]) * '''Ghost^3 DeinoFreak''' ([[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]) * '''Marioawesome''' (Daniel) * '''Shirley the Sheep Watne''' ([[Brayden]]) * '''[[Vaughn (accounts)|Theodore Lee Pemberton]]''' ([[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]) [[Category:Role]]</text>

<title>Juan two big cocks</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>216</id>
<revision>
<id>1189</id>
<parentid>1179</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-06T06:36:41Z</timestamp>

</contributor>
<origin>1189</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1442" sha1="dree96ph7ww3hcpblxkkyfhw3zjl2iq" xml:space="preserve">'''juan two big cocks''' is a nickname passed between [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] members after an unspecific period of time, though it is usually around four months. The name itself comes from [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], with a screenshot from one of his other friend's discord servers showing him nicknamed '''"juan two big cocks".''' After seeing this, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] changed [[Blake Jones|Blake's]] nickname in [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]] to '''"juan two big cocks"''' because he thought it was funny. After holding it for awhile, [[Blake Jones|Blake]] asked for the name to be taken away, but [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] didnt want to see it go quite yet, so he held a poll. The poll ended in a three way tie between [[Brayden]], [[Matthew]], and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]]. The people of [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM25]] then demanded that [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] take the name himself. Thus started the juan two big cocks tradition. The name has also seen minor deviations, like [[Matthew]] being named "juan two big shoops" in April 2026. This is based off a variation of [[Shirley The Sheep|Shirley the Sheep]]. == Succesion == # '''[[Blake Jones|Blake]]''' - February 2025 # '''[[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]''' - June 2025 # '''[[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]]''' - November 2025 # '''[[Matthew]]''' - January 2026 # '''[[Daniel]]''' - August 2026</text>
<sha1>dree96ph7ww3hcpblxkkyfhw3zjl2iq</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="3306" sha1="erickkxx10udk2c3l2z9dg2t99462l7" xml:space="preserve">{{Discord_Server|title1=new year new me 2026|owner=Bug311 (Teddy)|creation_date=December 31st, 2025|member_count=21|activity=January 2026-Present|image1=Lpc sleepover 1.jpg|caption-image1=server icon}}'''new year new me 2026''', sometimes abbreivated to '''"NYNM2026"''', is a [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] discord server created by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] on '''December 31st, 2025''', following [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]]. It is currently the main Less People group chat. The server is supposed to last for the entire duration of 2026, and will then be replaced by the future triquel to the [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me]] series. == Name Modifications == The name has been temporairly changed by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] numerous times. * Changed to "LOSER people" from July 20th-July 25th, 2026 after some people weren't abiding by [[Less People Lock-In|the Lockin]] or getting on. * Changed to "HATE people" from July 25th-July 26th, 2026 after one of [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] jokes was made fun of. * Changed to "[[NPC Builders Demo|ALIEN23 PLOKI52 WHYHELLOTHERE]]" from July 29th-August 13th, 2026 because of a joke from the [[NPC Builders Demo|AI Building Game]]. == Channels == === Text Channels === '''#general''' The main text channel for almost all communication '''#cracking-2026''' Private channel restricted to those with the [[Low taper fade (role)|low taper fade role]]. Usually features inappropiate content. '''#drawing-channel''' Private channel restricted to those with the [[Artistic people (role)|artistic people role]]. For sharing drawings. '''#low-taper_fade''' Channel made at the beginning of 2026 to drag the low taper fade meme into 2026. '''#dude-man''' Channel made at the beginning of 2026 to drag the dude man meme into 2026. '''#dank-meme-reset''' Channel made at the beginning of 2026 to mock the great meme reset meme. '''#2020204''' Channel restricted to only the owner, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. === Voice Channels === '''General''' The original voice channel '''2026''' Channel restricted to those with the 2026 role. Was created at the beginning of the year to symbolize the crossover at new year. '''2025''' Channel made at the beginning of the year to symbolize the crossover at new year. Isn't restricted, unlike 2026. '''XxGhostDeinoFreakxX's call''' Channel made for [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]. '''my safe space - dont move me from this call''' Channel made for [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] to get away from [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] moving people. == Notable Members == * '''[[Duncan - The Devil|Ghost^3 DeinoFreak]]''' * '''[[Teddy Pemberton|Ghost Bug311]]''' (owner) * '''Fraudizza''' aka '''AdminOf_ThePizza''' * '''[[Brayden|Shirley The Sheep Watne]]''' * '''Marioawesome''' * '''[[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahford Lee Watne]]''' * '''[[Christian|Dani G. German]]''' * '''43old''' * '''[[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Theodore Lee Pemberton]]''' * '''juan two big cocks''' aka '''ilymilo''' * '''MasonJarz27''' == Server Icon History == The server icon was originally the discord default server icon. It was later changed to it's current icon, which is from the [[Second Celina Sleepover]]. [[File:Newyearnewme2026.png|thumb|Original Icon]] [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>
<sha1>erickkxx10udk2c3l2z9dg2t99462l7</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1361" sha1="m3bxhpfitlmmvgm5df99o3p20s6rm30" xml:space="preserve">'''"Less.people+ D2 - The hidden dark side of less people that Blake doesn’t want you to know about 🤫"''' was a discord group chat created by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] on September 8th, 2024. The group chat served as a second [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] that could fit everyone from [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] and [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] during [[The Great Merger]] without having to remove people from the main [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+ GC]]. Despite this, it was originally created for [[Lethal Company]] and [[Football Fusion 2|Football Fusion]] after the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|lethal server]] died, as a short precursor and alternative to [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh,]] which [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] still wanted to keep separate from [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] at the time. It was used up until December 31st, 2024, when everything was moved to [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]]. == Name Changes == First it was named "Lethal and ff", but it would be changed to "Xanax overdose + soaking wet = 🤤", and then " Less people meat beating club", and it finally settled with its current name on September 25th, 2024. == Members == * Blake * Vaughn * Duncan * Teddy * Matthew * Vuke * Brady * Rahand * Christian * Brayden [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>
<sha1>m3bxhpfitlmmvgm5df99o3p20s6rm30</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Pain Pills Incident</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>230</id>
<revision>
<id>720</id>
<parentid>466</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:56:04Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>720</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1174" sha1="gp5jdshc82kejweo2a81593riej1sus" xml:space="preserve">From the [[Second Celina Sleepover]]. As dawn rose, The morning sunlight rays revealed tons of floating dust specs in [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] room. [[Brayden]] then discovered that [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] pillows were the culprit, and began banging on them to see how much dust he could get in the air. This disgusted everyone, who loudly expressed their thoughts about the Rutherford [[Vaughn's Mom's House|household's]] cleanliness. Vaughn's mom, [[Betty Rutherford|Betty]], was angered by this, and yelled at [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], accusing them of stealing [[Vaughn's Grandma (EVIL)|Vaughn's grandma's]] pain pills and threatening to call the police. While it's generally agreed that [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] wasn't responsible for this, it's suspected that Christian could've possibly taken them. The mystery remains unsolved. After this interaction, [[Betty Rutherford|Betty]] indirectly kicked [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] out by suggesting they leave. Less People cleaned [[Vaughn's Mom's House|the house]] and then escaped to the streets of [[Celina, Texas|Celina]]. [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>gp5jdshc82kejweo2a81593riej1sus</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Vaughn's Grandma (EVIL)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>231</id>
<revision>
<id>680</id>
<parentid>465</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T20:59:23Z</timestamp>

<text bytes="786" sha1="ei59ylwt8e9cli6zxudd4kkwfv46jp6" xml:space="preserve">'''Vaughn's Grandma''' from his [[Betty Rutherford|mother's]] side. Not much is known about her, other than she was born in Korea and has dementia. According to [[Betty Rutherford|Betty]], she had "military-style" parenting. She currently lives in [[Vaughn's Mom's House|Vaughn's mother's house]], staying in a room upstairs. In the [[First Celina Sleepover]], she was entirely absent, as she was staying in a room downstairs and never emerged. In the [[Second Celina Sleepover]], she emerged from her room multiple times to terramog all of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. She once stated that [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] were handsome, and then another time she gave everyone drinks before going back to her room. [[Category:Characters]]</text>
<sha1>ei59ylwt8e9cli6zxudd4kkwfv46jp6</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1092" sha1="7pvg15a5pbp1p6ahxnli7y9tq0834ks" xml:space="preserve">#REDIRECT [[Less People Immortality Theory]]The '''Immortal Less People Incident''' was a hypothetical scenario created in the Teddy Cuck Era where all of Less People received limitless bodies and infinite lifespans. People present for the discussion were Duncan, Teddy and Brady. ====== Idea #1: ====== Vaughn would be locked in an inescapable prison while the rest of Less People got super OP and terra-mogged him into the dirt. He would then be released and we would clown him for it. ====== Idea #2 ====== We would travel to every planet of the solar system and recreate civilization on all of them no matter how long it takes ====== Idea #3 ====== We would become god-like and unlock time-travel and world-manipulation to satisfy our boredom. ====== Idea #4 ====== We would travel so far into the universe and so fast that we would escape our reality and go to another universe where we met another friend group similar to Less People that would have similar powers. This would lead to us having a giant hypothetical battle royale and question our infinite existence even further.</text>
<sha1>7pvg15a5pbp1p6ahxnli7y9tq0834ks</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Lestopia (city)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>238</id>
<revision>
<id>715</id>
<parentid>714</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:50:11Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<comment>/* Buildings */</comment>
<origin>715</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="6157" sha1="hx9bv2q5rbj25mmnfavj39j5t3u5vp9" xml:space="preserve">''not to be confused with the larger [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia minecraft server]].'' '''Lestopia''' was a city within the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]]. It was the oldest established city, being founded on '''January 12, 2025''', and it serves as the capital of the world.[[File:Lestopia screenshot.png|thumb|523x523px|Screenshot of Lestopia by Duncan, 10/26/25|center]] == History == === Beginning === * [[File:The Glory hole.png|thumb|230x230px|The Glory Hole]]Lestopia was founded on January 12, 2025, after the first initial migration at the beginning of the server. "The Glory Hole" was the first structure made in the city, a small dirt hole in the ground. An execution stage and an underground courthouse soon followed. Early Lestopian buildings were mostly subterranean, for example: The Glory Hole, the Courthouse, The Temple of Whislam. * After the building of the court, the server inhabitants met to discuss the official creation of the city and it's constitution. Here the name '''"Lestopia"''' was coined, alongside it's flag. A democratic government system was established. Immediately after the government was established, a trial was held against [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] over the accusation of stealing Matthew's iron. [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] was executed soon after. * The city then made major expansions with new houses, a town hall, and a storage room being built. === Early History === * Construction on [[Mulity Tower (Lestopia MC Server)|Mulity tower]] would begin, but after the exterior was finished, progress would halt. * [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] house would be burnt down in what would be called "[[The Night of Broken Glass (Lestopia MC Server)|The Night of Broken Glass]]". * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] would dig out the Squid Game's red light green light game. * The Colosseum and Pyramid would be constructed. === September Return === * Construction of [[Mulity Tower (Lestopia MC Server)|Mulity tower]] resumed after the [[Lestopia September Return|return to the server in September 2025]], with the top dorm floors being complete. * Downtown Lestopia begins construction by [[Brayden]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. * On November 22nd, Daniel covered Lestopia with snow for the winter season. === March Return === * After returning to the Server on March 30th, 2026, the [[Whislam|Whislamic Church]] would be renovated by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], adding a [[Whiter Pits|Whiter Pit]]. * On April 8th 2026, Duncan and Christian would [[Lestopia Wither Attack|summon many Withers in Lestopia.]][[File:Lestopia chunk base.png|thumb|242x242px|Lestopia's natural biome map]] == Geography == Lestopia occupies a small flat plains biome at the coordinates (-1776, 0). To the North, there is a small sea with [[The Holy Lands|The Holy Land]] on the opposite coast. To the West, there is a much larger ocean. To the South, there is a steep hill overlooking the city. To the East, there is an oak and birch forest. == Buildings == [[File:Lestopia mulity tower.png|thumb|314x314px|Mulity Tower before the September 2025 return.]] * '''The Glory Hole''' * '''[[Mulity Tower (Lestopia MC Server)|Mulity Tower]]''' * '''The Court House''' * '''The Temple of Whislam''' * '''Teddy's House''' * '''The Storage House''' * '''The Farm''' * '''Town Hall''' * '''The Colosseum''' * '''The Villager Trading Hall''' * '''Satan's asshole of doom & despair''' * [[File:Lestopia court house.png|thumb|306x306px|The Courthouse and Temple of Whislam]]'''Automatic Iron Farm''' * '''Horse Stables''' * '''Luka Doncic Memorial''' * '''Hobbit Hole''' * '''Squid Game''' * '''Super Spruce Tree Farm''' * '''Christian's Water House''' * '''Gambling Machine''' * '''Lestopia Sign''' * '''Triple T Statue'''[[File:Lestopia sign.png|thumb|273x273px|Lestopia Sign on the Southern Hill]] * '''Industrial Coal Factory''' * '''Automatic Wood Farm''' * '''Villager Breeder''' * '''Carrot Farm''' * '''Pickaxe Statue''' * '''Cock Fighting Ring''' * '''Lighthouse''' * '''Blake Statue''' * '''Reverted Vaughn House''' === Destroyed Buildings === * '''Vaughn's House''' -Arson by Christian, [[Brayden]], and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] * '''Rahand's Carrot''' -Blown up by an unknown person on [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] account during the [[First Celina Sleepover]]. * '''Pond Home''' -Accidently burnt down during construction of the Industrial Coal Factory by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] == Government & Law == === Government Basics === Lestopia is an absolute democracy, with the citizens all being on an equal level of power. The citizens can gather to discuss the amendment of the laws, court cases, and other city matters. For a new document to be instated in Lestopian law, at least half of Citizens have to sign the document === Current Citizens === * Teddy * Duncan * Rahand * Christian * Matthew * Daniel * Brayden * Vaughn * Brady === Documents === * '''Lestopia Laws''' -Original set of laws * '''Amendment 13-18''' -Amendments added to Lestopia Laws * '''19-21 Amendment''' -Amendments added to Lestopia Laws * '''VR Citizenship''' -Grants [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] citizenship * '''BW Citizenship''' -Grants Brayden citizenship * '''BJ Citizenship''' -Grants Brady citizenship * '''Vaughn Restrict''' -Verdict from court case on 1/29/25, requires Vaughn to bring two cows to [[The Holy Lands|The Holy Land]], from which he is then banned. It also bars him from trading with villagers or enchanting items. <gallery caption="&quot;Lestopia Laws&quot; -By OfficerBugg ([[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]])"> File:Lestopian law 1-5.png File:Lestopian law 2-5.png File:Lestopian law 3-5.png File:Lestopian law 4-5.png File:Lestopian law 5-5.png File:Lestopia amend1 1-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend1 2-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend1 3-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend1 4-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend1 5-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend1 6-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend2 1-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend2 2-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend2 3-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend2 4-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend2 5-6.PNG File:Lestopia amend2 6-6.PNG </gallery> * [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]]</text>
<sha1>hx9bv2q5rbj25mmnfavj39j5t3u5vp9</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>The Holy Lands</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>239</id>
<revision>
<id>639</id>
<parentid>535</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T20:48:30Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>639</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2922" sha1="f2fml4j5unjogys7t7g0dmuu1v5ahgs" xml:space="preserve">'''The Holy Land''' was a region protected by the [[Whislamic|Whislam]] religion across the bridge north of [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] in the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server.]] The land was an oak forest biome which had all the trees removed, clearing a section for the main territory, as well as a large ravine which split the land into two sections. It was a very beautiful area, which was filled with flowers, lush grass, and bees. It featured an oak wood palisade fence surrounding the area, a pit separated into 3 sections holding chickens, sheep, and cows, a community storage room, and lastly, a villager breeder/trading building on one the north side of the ravine. On the south side was a large tower, built by Duncan and Christian, a stone temple build by Brayden, where he lived in the basement, a three story farm used to grow potatoes, carrots, and wheat, and a large harbor allowing boat travel between '''the Holy Land''' and [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] before the large wood bridge was built. along the east coast of the Holy Lands was a small home built into the side of the cliff, where Duncan would call home for the early days of the server, before spending most of his time at [[Mega Builds]], or in [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. == Structures == === North Side === [[File:Holy Land.png|thumb|360x360px|An aerial view of the Holy Lands (Post fire) from a happy ghast ridden by Duncan (left), Christian (front center), Vaughn (back center), and Brayden (right)]] *Animal Farm * Villager Building * Community Storage Building === South Side === * Tower * Temple * Crop Farm === Outskirts === * Harbor * Tree Farm == Holy Harvest == The holy harvest was a weekly event hosted in the '''Holy Land''' in which the members of Whislam, [[Brayden]], [[Members of Less People|Christian]], and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] would harvest all the crops from the farm, and slay all but two of each animal type in the animal farm. Each type of animal would be bred as much as possible during the one week periods in order to have the largest harvests possible. The Crops gained from the harvest would be used to breed cows and sheep, and the rest of the crops would be placed in the community storage chests, along with all the meat and various drops from the animals. These harvests became the main food source of Whislamic people in times of need, when emeralds were low and golden carrots were unavailable. == The Great Holy Land Fire == after months of playing on the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]], destruction throughout the world was inevitable. In an effort to repair damages done to the Holy Lands and restore it back to its former glory, a fire would break out in the forest east of the fence, which would destroy many trees around the Holy Land, and destroy a large part of the fence. [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]]</text>
<sha1>f2fml4j5unjogys7t7g0dmuu1v5ahgs</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Whislam</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>242</id>
<revision>
<id>633</id>
<parentid>631</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T20:30:19Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>633</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="10970" sha1="i79k6verbq0ihk2kk2qw1k80kfhpkcq" xml:space="preserve">== Whislam == Whislam is a religion created by the prophet [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi Kenzo]] in the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]]. Its original date of formation is unknown, but it is stated by the prophet that during the [[First Migration (Lestopia MC Server)|First Migration]], the [[The Whither|Whither]] (not to be confused with the Wither) spoke to him and told him of his ideals and overwhelming power if he were to rise to be the god of Minecraft. [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi Kenzo]] then dedicated himself to Whislam and served as the lead prophet of the religion. == Beliefs == The Whislamic people believe that beings such as [[The Whither]], Herobrine, The Ender Dragon, The Warden, and the unseen [[The Mulity Menace|Mulity Menace]] are all supreme beings that are created with the seed of a new Minecraft world. They believe that all of the other supreme beings are extremely evil and unjust, ESPECIALLY the [[The Mulity Menace|Mulity Menace]], who seems to haunt unsuspecting Whislamic people when they are in unprotected (lacking a Whither Portrait) domains. They serve the [[The Whither|Whither]] and attempt to bring him to power by sacrificing people, animals, and especially valuables to fuel him and lead him to be the strongest of the supreme beings so that he can be the God of the Minecraft world and have everybody live a happy and just life. [[File:Whislamic Church.png|thumb|Whislamic Church (gift box at front)|298x298px]] The Whislamic people pray to the [[The Whither|Whither]] in Whislamic Churches and give valuables every day to help make him stronger. Almost all sins in the Whislamic religion are forgiven by sacrificing important things and praying excessively to the [[The Whither|Whither]], which help him become stronger. Although when praying you gift in the [[The Whither|Whither]] gift boxes found in Whislamic churches and occasionally private domains, sacrifices by punishment happen in The Whither Pit, the only direct connection to the [[The Whither|Whither]] in any Minecraft worlds. The [[The Whither|Whither]] is not to be confused with Withers. Withers are very powerful servants of The [[The Whither|Whither]], summonable by common Minecraftians. But; If they are summoned without the permission of the Whither (which is relayed by the 1st prophet Akemi Kenzo), it is an extremely grave sin punishable by sacrificing yourself in a battle to the death against 100 Withers inside of the Great Wither Pit, an unconstructed enormous mouth leading to the fiery depths in which the [[The Whither|Whither]] gains his power. When the [[The Whither|Whither]] finally reaches full power, what the Whislamic people call "Doomsday" will occur, and a rumbling-esque would roll over the world all the way to the Farlands using millions upon millions of Withers and all sinners would be slaughtered. The lands would be scourged of any wrongdoings, all of the other Supreme beings would be executed, and the Whislamic peoples would be blessed to live in the Whislamic Heavenly cities for eternity. == Laws == * The slaughter of Pork-dropping mobs and the consumption of Pork in Minecraft is a sin and is punishable by: Sacrificing valuables or yourself to the Whither pit[[File:Closest view of The Whither Pit.png|thumb|Closest View of The Whither Pit|299x299px]] * The worship of any other beings, most importantly Herobrine or The Ender Dragon is insanely illegal and is punishable by: Sacrificing yourself to the Whither pit * Once per day you must pray to the [[The Whither|Whither]] in a Whislamic Church and drop a properly deemed amount of valuables into a Whither gift box (Usually found at the front of churches). Not doing this every day or providing an insufficient amount is punishable by: Sacrificing valuables to the Whither pit, and excessive skipping is punishable by Sacrificing yourself to the Whither pit. * Every Whislamic student must have a portrait of the [[The Whither|Whither]] in every building they enter. A properly created praying space in your home can with a proper Whither gift box serve as a substitute to visiting a Whislamic church to provide valuables to the [[The Whither|Whither]]. Not having a portrait is punishable sacrificing valuables to the Whither pit. * You are not allowed to slander the [[The Whither|Whither]] in any way. Slandering the Whither is punishable by: sacrificing valuables or yourself to the Whither pit. * Stealing from any churches or the Whither pit is an extremely grave sin and is punishable by: a one versus one no-armor fight against a Wither. Victory leads to forgiveness, and death leads to sacrifice. == Order of Known Events == <nowiki>*</nowiki>Due to the lack of documentation, there is a severe lack of dates and confirmation of order, so take this all with a grain of salt <nowiki>*</nowiki>Longest ago ---> Most recent * The Awakening - During the Great Migration The [[The Whither|Whither]] speaks to [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi Kenzo]], turning him into the 1st and lead prophet and telling him his ideals and the Laws of Whislam * [[File:First Whislamic Chuch.png|thumb|266x266px|First Whislamic Church]][[File:First Whislamic Church.png|thumb|First Whislamic Church|264x264px]][[File:The Watcher's Overview.png|thumb|The Watcher's View|281x281px]]Construction of the 1st Church - In [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia (the city)]], for his 1st home [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi]] constructed the 1st Whislamic church in which next to his underground bedroom he built the 1st Whither gift box under the 1st portrait of the [[The Whither|Whither]]. This is where the 1st Whislamic people prayed and provided valuables to the Whither and [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi]] preached the Whislamic ideals. Unfortunately the 1st prayer box was ruined, but the 1st portrait stands strong under the church. * Construction of The Whither Pit - Next to the 1st church, a beautiful mouth was built deep into the ground in where the overworld could take a glimpse into the world in which the [[The Whither|Whither]] rests. This would be used for sacrifices. * The Resurrection - [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi]] Sacrificed himself to The Whither Pit to set an example to the doubters of [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. Despite everyone in [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] seeing him jump into The Pit, he did not die and later emerged from The Pit unscathed and with steak and golden carrots. This helped convert [[Christian|Zinformer]] and [[Brayden|ShirleyTheSheep2]] to Whislam. * Remodeling the First Church and The Whither Pit - The pit was originally asymmetrical and needed to be fixed soon after original construction. The church was then remodeled once the pit was fully constructed to give a hauntingly beautiful walkup to The Pit (this was only on the surface, the underground church did not change). * The Watcher - During the peace and expansion period of [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]], on the large hill that watches over it (now being where the Lestopia sign resides), 4 soul sand appeared looking over [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]], untouched and according to Akemi "A dreamcatcher esc monument that watches over the Whislamic Church and protect [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]". He has been accused by many of placing it there himself, but this is unconfirmed. * The Holy Lands Migration - After a short exploration arc into the North, [[Duncan SYBAU picture|Akemi Kenzo]] and [[Christian|Zinformer]] found a beautiful Flower Forest on the coast of the land apart from [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. They then moved into the land, deeming it "[[The Holy Lands]]", and began to build it up and restricted any non Whislamic people from visiting the land. [[Brayden|ShirleyTheSheep2]] moved in shortly after. * [[File:The Holy Lands.png|thumb|[[The Holy Lands]] |380x380px]]Building up The Holy Lands - [[The Holy Lands]] was completely fenced off, except a small port facing [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. The construction of a Whislamic Temple and The Tower began on the south side of [[The Holy Lands]] (by [[Brayden|ShirleyTheSheep2]] and [[Christian|Zinformer]] each), and the construction of a small cliffside home on the east side by [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi Kenzo]]. a bridge ran over the ravine, parallel to the storage units, and led to the large Villager trading hall and the automatic sugarcane farm. On the west side, north of the temple, a 3-story farm and a 3-room animal farm were created for the Holy Harvests. * The Peace Period - For an extended stretch of time, nothing important happened in Whislam. The [[The Whither|Whither]] continued to grow strong and everyone moved out of [[The Holy Lands]] to start their own building projects. During this time [[The Holy Lands]] unfortunately got very worn down and became honestly not pretty. * [[File:Akemi's Appartment.png|thumb|398x398px|[[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi's]] Appartment in Mulity tower.]]The Mulity Arc - During the creation of The Mulity Apartments, the infamous nemesis of The Whither [[The Mulity Menace|"The Mulity Menace"]] was spotted and began to haunt Mulity. Out of fear, [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi]] prayed deeply to The Whither and asked for help. The Whither gave him many spells disguised as inspirational quotes, and told him to put them around his Mulity Apartment. This cleansed Mulity from [[The Mulity Menace]], making [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi's]] apartment a new church and Mulity a safe haven for Whislamic people. This caused the Mulity Menace to flee from Mulity, as he was spotted around [[Brayden|ShirleyTheSheep2's]] castle, most likely trying to find a home there. * Restoration of [[The Holy Lands]] - After another short peace period, by the Whither's instruction [[The Holy Lands]] were completely restored to its former beauty, including the renovation of all buildings, especially [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi's]] house going from unsightly to a pretty cherry blossom dock. During this rebuild of [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi's]] house, a new, smaller Whither Pit was formed inside the house in where [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi]] would begin receiving instructions for the next important event. * [[File:Symbolic Apartment Message to Mulity Menace.png|thumb|Symbolic Goodbye to [[The Mulity Menace|Mulity Menace]] in [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi's]] Appartment|303x303px]]Doomsday Preparation - After speaking with [[The Whither]] in the hidden Whither Pit, [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi]] visited The Watcher behind the [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] sign, and began construction of a large bunker inside the hill in which the doomsday preparation would begin. Before the [[Rahand Lestopia Shutdown|Rahand Server Shutdown]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi]] began placing hundreds of blocks of soul sand to prepare the Wither cleansing of [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. They look through a small window over [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]], and lay unfound by everyone except Zinformer. [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]]</text>
<sha1>i79k6verbq0ihk2kk2qw1k80kfhpkcq</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Rahand Realm Rampage Incident</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>246</id>
<revision>
<id>1240</id>
<parentid>1239</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-07T01:39:29Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>Tryhardquesadilla</username>
<id>54330142</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1240</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2386" sha1="nch59arsyhqjvm9gql1wyvjgw9h3tln" xml:space="preserve">[[Category:Events]] The '''Rahand Realm Rampage Incident''' refers to in 2024 when [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] got mad and [[Rahand Break Controversies|crashed out]] after [[Brayden]] cancelled his '''"Spirit Bomb"''' in the Roblox fighting game "Realm Rampage". As a result of this event, [[Brayden]] was blocked for 3 months. === Background === Realm Rampage was an anime themed fighting, or "battlegrounds" game on Roblox, known for an innovative approach to destruction, first popularized by Jujutsu Shenanigans, along with fun new methods of transportation including zip-lines around the map. While most battlegrounds games featured many different characters from a singular game or show, Realm Rampage had many different anime characters including Sukuna, Gojo, or Katakuri. With all the hype around the game on social media, Brayden, Rahand, and Duncan, who enjoyed other battlegrounds games decided to try it out. === The incident === While playing the game, Rahand had activated Goku's ultimate, granting him a new moveset with powerful abilities. The most notable ability was the spirit bomb, which had massive area of effect and damage, but a long start up. After activating the spirit bomb, Brayden then canceled the move by attacking Rahand, putting it on a cooldown which would last the entirety of Rahand's ultimate and preventing him from using it. In a fit of rage, Rahand started chasing Brayden around the map trying to kill him and take revenge, but Brayden constantly ran away and would not let Rahand kill him. After a while, Rahand famously shouted "Stop running away and let me kill you!". In response, Brayden did stop running away from Rahand, but instead of letting him get revenge, he hit Rahand through the outer wall of the map, sending him into the void and killing him. This moment sent Rahand over the edge, causing him to rage quit the game and leave call. They exchanged a few words general, and Rahand was even planning to rejoin call that same day, but a poorly timed message from Brayden reading "Its ur fault" caused Rahand to leave the call yet again, and block him for over 3 months until some time during the siege era when Brayden finally downloaded siege and him and Rahand made up. [[File:Spirit bomb incident messages.png|thumb|The only known evidence and proof of this incident, written in the Yugioh serveer|center|680x680px]]</text>
<sha1>nch59arsyhqjvm9gql1wyvjgw9h3tln</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1013" sha1="pqxuwtfrjokj5vgcqpqgq47kt3h3byh" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Breakfast box rules.png|thumb|439x439px|Rule channel from Waffle house, called "Breakfast box" at the time.[[File:Dream car waffle house.jpg|thumb|276x276px|Vuke and Daniel talking in Waffle House]]]] Waffle house, also called '''Breakfast box''', was a deleted discord server owned by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]. It was likely the first group chat from the [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only side]]. The exact name is unknown. While the history is mostly forgotten, it was likely deleted due to mass rule breaking toward the end of the server in 6th grade. == Members == The server had a high member count estimated to be around 30. * '''Larry''' ([[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]) (Owner) -Also known as Akemi ❤️ * '''Bass''' ([[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]]) * '''Marioawesome''' (Daniel) * '''Simon "Ghost" Riley''' ([[Vuke]]) -Also known as Tito, John "Bravo Six" Price * '''Sumgamer''' (Connor) [[File:Waffle house discord screenshot.jpg|thumb|297x297px|Rahand proving that Connor said the N word.|left]]</text>
<sha1>pqxuwtfrjokj5vgcqpqgq47kt3h3byh</sha1>

<title>List of Discord Servers and GC</title>

<page>
<title>Lestopia (Minecraft Server)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>262</id>
<revision>
<id>701</id>
<parentid>657</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T21:13:13Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<comment>/* September Return */</comment>
<origin>701</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="6348" sha1="hx78luxvlxuogg4447mft5y8kse1nf4" xml:space="preserve">''not to be confused with [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia city]].'' A [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] [[List of Minecraft Servers|minecraft server]] created on '''January 12th, 2025'''. Currently owned by [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], it is the longest lasting minecraft server in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] history. == Locations == === Cities === * [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] * [[The Holy Lands|The Holy Land]] * [[Mega Build City]] === Major Bases === * [[Las Cevas]] * [[Monk Mountain]] * [[Brady SMP (Lestopia MC Server)|Brady SMP]] * [[Mario's Monarch]] === Independent Builds/Bases === * Christian's First Base * Snow Tundra Cabin * Statue of Liberty * Riverside Home * Mob Farm * Teddy's Migration Hole * Red Light District * Migration Cherry Mongol Camp * Duncan's South Castle * Duncan's East Castle == Events == == Notable Players == === [[Duncan - The Devil|AkemiKenzo]] === Prophet and founder of [[Whislamic|Whislam]]. Co-founded [[The Holy Lands|The Holy Land]] and [[Mega Build City]]. === [[Teddy Pemberton|OfficerBugg]] === Original server owner. Major contributor to [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] and [[The Great Migration (Lestopia MC Server)|The Great Migration]]. === [[Christian|zinformer]] === Richest, most powerful, and most dangerous player. Owner of [[Las Cevas]]. Co-founded [[The Holy Lands|The Holy Land]] and [[Mega Build City]]. === [[Rahand - The Muslim|rsmzxz]] === Current server owner. Responsible for almost every automatic farm. === [[Brayden|ShirleyTheSheep2]] === Builder of the [[Monk Mountain|Monk Mountains]]. Major contributor to [[The Holy Lands]] and [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. === [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vonkmad27]] === The scapegoat of the server. === Marioawesome === Joined late as a laboring slave. Builder of [[Mario's Monarch]]. === way2fast91 === Joined late. Builder of [[Brady SMP (Lestopia MC Server)|Brady SMP]]. == Poltical Entidies == === Lestopia === The global governing body of the server based in [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. It exercises it's law across the entire server. Almost every server player is a Lestopian citizen and has an equal say in the Lestopian government. Lestopia acts as the "United Nations" of the server. === Whislam === A religious authority based on the [[Whislamic]] religion. The religion is ran by [[Duncan - The Devil|AkemiKenzo]], the leading prophet and founder. It governs over [[The Holy Lands|The Holy Land]], aswell as all of it's followers. It is known to enforce strict border policies agaisnt non-members. The religion also performs religious sacrifices and executions in [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]], with the religion being ingrained in [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopian]] government & law. === The Mongol Empire === Lead by [[Brady|way2fast91]], it originates from the [[The Great Migration (Lestopia MC Server)|Great Migration]]. It's primary goal is expansion across the entire world, establishing small encampments scattered around the server. == General History == === Beginning === Created in January 2025 by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], the server began with a [[First Migration (Lestopia MC Server)|short migration]]. Shortly after, the first city [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] was founded. A little later, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] created the first religion, [[Whislamic|Whislam]]. After the construction of the courthouse, the [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopians]] would meet to organize the government. [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Christian]] would be the first to leave [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] to build [[The Holy Lands|The Holy Land]] for [[Whislamic|Whislam]]. They would soon be joined by [[Brayden]]. When the nether was reached, the basics of the [[Nether Highway Network (Lestopia MC Server)|highway network]] were contructed. In the beginning of the server, elytras and all automatic farms were banned, but some exceptions would be made for more simple farms like sugar cane. === Progression Completion & First Shutdown === [[Christian]] would be the first to obtain netherite armor, being extreamly wealthy in the early days of the server. After the ender dragon was defeated (achievement stolen by [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]), the servers focus would shift away from progression to building, with [[Mega Build City|Mega City]] being founded. The server would begin to die out as people took on too many ambitious building projects. [[Christian]] and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] would continue to play on the server a little longer by themselves. However, due to the server taking up too much memory on [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] computer and lagging siege, he would take down the server more and more often. This prevented [[Christian]] and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] from playing, until the server was eventually abandoned and shutdown. === September Return === After the failure of the [[Rape River (Minecraft Server)|Rape River Server]], [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] would [[Lestopia September Return|return to this server in September 2025]]. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] transferred ownership of the server to [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], because he was no longer able to run the server. The return started with [[The Great Migration (Lestopia MC Server)|The Great Migration]], but this would quickly fail as most migrants returned home. After the [[Migration War]], the remaining migrants were reconnected via the Western [[Nether Highway Network (Lestopia MC Server)|Nether Highway]]. The server focus would once again return to building, but this major construction progress was actually made. === Second Shutdown === Due to a conflict with [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], [[ShirleyTheSheep2 Ban|Brayden was banned]]. This would begin the second decline of the server. [[Brayden]] played a major role in major building projects like Downtown [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]], and without him, motivation was ruined. With [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] refusing to unban [[Brayden]], the player count dropped until it was empty, and [[Rahand Lestopia Shutdown|Rahand shutdown the server for the second time]]. === March Return === On March 30th, the server was put back up by Rahand and [[Brayden]] was unbanned. == Ownership == === Transfering === === Admin Abuse === [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]]</text>
<sha1>hx78luxvlxuogg4447mft5y8kse1nf4</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="666" sha1="bd2qtsm8mcznvh7w5jrv5i0by1vfs12" xml:space="preserve">''not to be confused with [[Lesopotamia - Rape River]], a city within the server.'' Rape river was a Minecraft server created in between times Lestopia, created to bring new members of less people into the server who wanted a "reset". after migrating hundreds of blocks from spawn, the rape river valley was found. It was large mountain range with a beautiful valley at the foot. A winding river flowed through the valley-Rape River. The server was heavily based on rules and religions, with a rule preventing advancements in technology and materials to prolong the early game and prevent burnout, which is what happened to some in Lestopia. [[Category:Minecraft]]</text>
<sha1>bd2qtsm8mcznvh7w5jrv5i0by1vfs12</sha1>

<text bytes="3361" sha1="3jwd9030kvqx0vdcvongt3016hmhu5y" xml:space="preserve">A set of rules originating from [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]]. They were first created by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and were used as a justification for [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and Blake to admin abuse, leading to [[The Rukle War|The Rukle War.]] == The Rukles == From [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]], #da-rules '''rukle 1 - no being a werido''' '''rukle 2 - losers are NOT accepted''' '''rukle 3 - playing indian and muslim noises via voice chat makes u a loser''' '''rukle 4 - playing a dog wistle with no dog makes u a weirdo''' '''rukle 5 - look at this cool gif! this gif is cool''' [Gif of Trump aura farming with everyone pointing guns at him] '''ruckle 6 - i get more than one gif! gaZillion GIZZ!''' '''ruckle 7 - every body has to save the gif from rukle 5 to their favorites''' '''rukle 8 - every wednesday gets a name change! New Name: HUMPDAY!!!''' '''rukle 9 - tuesdays are also now tk tuesdays''' '''rule 10 - bottem fragger gets kicked from the siege stack (unless u 4-0)''' '''ruckle 11 - rukle 10 doesnt matter because it was mispelled, but this rukle requires every body to pretend it was spelled right so it counts again''' '''rukle 12 - FUCK VUAHGN!!!!!!!!!!!!!''' '''rukle 13 - no not being a liberal and going to a shooting range in a couple of days with your dad''' '''rukle 14 - "Booster BoyACEPTION - Liberal: " -Pizza, less people+ D2''' '''rukle 15 - vaughn is boosted''' '''rukle 16 - dont question da rules!!!''' '''rukle 17 - all said in this channel is universal to all lsp servers and gcs (including yugioh, less people, less people d2, and lethal)''' '''rukle 18 - no spoiling squid game season 2''' '''rukle 19 - mass vexbolts unfollowing on December 31st at 11:59 PM, 2024 (REQUIRED)''' '''rukle 20 - no banning @DeinoFreak , you can only bandit trick him''' '''rukle 21 - bandit tricking can also mean banning''' '''rukle 22 - @DeinoFreak isnt allowed to use black emojis''' '''rukle 24 - pretend that rukle 23 actually exists and is the best rukle, like a fucking, socretes, aristotle, MASTERPEICE. like a work of fucking art. like holy fucking shit oh fuck''' '''rukle 25 - anyone playing pokemon brick bronze on roblox HAS to screenshare''' '''rukle 26 - you must of been present for the first mention of rukle 14, and if you werent just pretend you were''' '''rukle 27 - every rukle is a RUKLE and a RUKLE only''' '''rukle 29 - blake works at mod pizza''' '''rukle 30 - rukles made for personal gain dont count and are false rukles''' '''rukle 31 - blake is the server owner, not teddy''' '''rukle 32 - he is a tank that was first introduced in WW2 it takes about 6 American tanks to kill him and his name is TIGER TANK he is a heavily armored tank with a lot of weaponized weaponry to eliminate.''' '''rukle 33 - rahand isnt a bloater''' '''rukle 34 - if it exists on the internet, theres porn of it''' '''rukle 35 - changing counts as making''' '''rukle 36 - as soon as 2025 starts, everyone must reference low taper fade becasue the meme is still massive''' '''rukle 37 - do not change the server name to ANYTHING other than Buff Bass's server. ESPECIALLY NOT "Creeper_NahMan's Server"''' '''rukle 38 - no unbanning rahand until he begs''' '''Rukle 39 - All Less People Memes are Eternal'''</text>
<sha1>3jwd9030kvqx0vdcvongt3016hmhu5y</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Milo's Masquerade (Discord Server)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>291</id>
<revision>
<id>1287</id>
<parentid>1286</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-10-28T22:12:08Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<comment>/* Current Members (Raiders) */</comment>
<origin>1287</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1556" sha1="3moxv9y3q8hln6kz4vdugws6tghzraq" xml:space="preserve">'''Milo's Masquerade''' was a discord server owned by Brady. The server would only last about an hour before getting nuked. Brady created the server to escape the rule of the [[Rukles]] in [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]], during [[The Rukle War]]. == Initial Revolt & Server Creation == After the server creation at 1:15 AM during the early morning of December 31st, all of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] other than [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and Blake were invited and joined the server. Immediately after, the new members performed a mass leaving of [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]] in protest of Teddy and Blake. This action started [[The Rukle War]]. == Retaliation == The two outcasted tyrants, angered by this, plotted to wait until Brady falls asleep, get Blake to go on his computer, and transfer ownership to himself. However, in the mean time, they convinced their way into the server and sucked up to Brady by faking apologies. == Nuking == After everyone got off for the night, Blake executed his plan and took control of the server at around 2AM. Him and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] were made administrators and then banned all the Masqueraders other than themselves. == Members == === Original (Now banned) === * [[Brady Jones|Brady]] (Original Owner) * [[Matthew]] * [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] * [[Daniel]] * [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] === Current Members (Raiders) === * [[Blake Jones|Blake]] (Current Owner) * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>
<sha1>3moxv9y3q8hln6kz4vdugws6tghzraq</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>The Rukle War</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>292</id>
<revision>
<id>705</id>
<parentid>641</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:25:33Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>705</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="3161" sha1="gp0vfdgelqs4w6gwdrro4i12o3ulqbq" xml:space="preserve">A short conflict taking place in late December, 2024. Originally started after the [[Rukles]] were used to justify admin abuse in [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]]. == Background == [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]] had been abandoned due to [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] "admin abuse", causing [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] to move to [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]] on December 28th, 2024. Despite the server name alluding to [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], the server itself was actually created and owned by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], but Blake was considered the "owner" of the server after [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] gave him the Owner role and all administrator perms. == Blake's Admin Abuse == In the early morning of the December 29th, Teddy made the [[Rukles]], which Blake would use to justify admin abusing with the power [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] gave him. Anyone accused of breaking a [[Rukles|Rukle]] would quickly be muted, deafened, disconnected, timed out, and even banned by Blake. Teddy did nothing to stop this and allowed Blake to continue his rampage. This abuse would reach its peak on the night of December 30th, when everyone was in the call and playing Pokémon Brick Bronze. In response to this, Brady would create an alternative server named "[[Milo's Masquerade (Discord Server)|Milo's Masquerade]]" to protest the tyranny. == Milo's Revolt == After the creation of [[Milo's Masquerade (Discord Server)|Milo's Masquerade]] during the early morning of December 31st, the rest of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] other than [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and Blake were invited and joined the server. Immediately after, the new members performed a mass leaving of [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's Server]] in protest of Teddy and Blake. Teddy and Blake would later be let in the server, but they were forced to beg for forgiveness and reject the [[Rukles]] against their will. == Retaliation & Aftermath == Angered by this, the tyrants [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and Blake plotted. The plan was to wait until Brady gets off, then have Blake log onto his computer and transfer ownership of Milo's Masquerade to himself. Brady got off around 2AM, leaving his computer exposed. The plan was executed without anyone noticing. Shortly after, Blake had taken over ownership of [[Milo's Masquerade (Discord Server)|Milo's Masquerade]] and banned everyone else other than [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. In the end, Milo's Revolt had been crushed by the tyrants of [[Buff Bass's Server (Discord Server)|Buff Bass's server]]. After everyone woke up to find [[Milo's Masquerade (Discord Server)|Milo's Masquerade]] gone, they all moved back to [[Yugioh 🥰 (Discord Server)|Yugioh]] where the conflict started. Later the same day, everyone moved to [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] new server [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|"new year new me 2025"]] after he swore to have no admin abuse. In the end, everything came in a full circle and nobody really gained anything. [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>gp0vfdgelqs4w6gwdrro4i12o3ulqbq</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Anomalies</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>294</id>
<revision>
<id>1261</id>
<parentid>1253</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-07T02:39:57Z</timestamp>
<contributor>

</contributor>
<origin>1261</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2582" sha1="t124umlmr6ua5v6ur88hcopg4rusfjt" xml:space="preserve">'''"Anomaly"''' generally refers to people with bizarre behaviour, particularly at [[McKinney Boyd High School]]. The term is an inside joke started by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]] during their Freshman year to mock people doing funny things. Anomalies are commonly characterized as occult and supernatural beings, often with powerful feats and abilites. Anomalies were usually just anyone misfortunate enough to have some slightly apparent characteristic while [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Brayden]], or even [[Drew]] were walking by them. Many Anomalies were just named as [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]] walked down the hallway laughing together, pointing out random people and giving them stupid names. There are also [[Anomaly Hunters]], such as [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|Ellis]], which refers to anyone aware of and interacting with Anomalies. On the last day of their Freshman year, the [[Anomaly Massacre]] occured, where the [[Assassins]] killed a lot of the Anomalies. == List of Anomalies == Note: There is also [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|Ellis]], The Anomaly Hunter, who technically isn't an anomaly, but still an honorable mention. * [[Bathroom Guy]] * [[King Von]] * Carti * [[Lay Guy]] * Sit Guy * Bandana Guy * Tyler the Destroyer * The Watcher * Discount Duncan * The Outcast * Genie * [[Mec]] * Football Guy * The Guys that need to watch out * The Hacker * [[Brady Jones|Brady]] * Elijah * Mr Rump * Dog or Cat Girl * Farm Guy * Modern Farm Guy * Ricky * Edgar Army * Sit guy gang * The Nest * Tennis Guy * New Football Guy * The Charger * Flashlight Guy * Flash You Girl * Sam's Daycare Shrieker * Scooter Guy * Nose Hair Guy * Jewish Sombero Guy * The Girl Who Knows Everything * The Fungus * Steph Curry * Purple Hair Girl (Gay Smell Girl) * Red Hair Girl * Xavier * Maid Girl * [[Drew]] * Short Ginger Kid * Ceiling Worker (The guy who has his arm up) * Dr. Hodum * Running Girl * Brown Backpack Guy (Lay Guy Backpack Person) * Fake Sit Guy * Fake King Von * Lil Tecca * Oded * Soundboard Guy * Cp Gooner * Hammerhead * The Vampire * Photobomber * Egg Guy * Pizza Line Guy * The Guy Who Smiles * Mira * Mccoy Box * Blane * Duck Guy * Furry Girl * Bob Guy * Big Al * Andrew * Green Hair Girl * Suprised Girl * Anomaly Bus * [[Bus 177]] * Manolo * Mafia Boss * Water Guy * Tattoo Guy * Head Bop Girl * Neckless * Varsity Guy * Three Musketeers * Naruto (Wig Guy) * Trashcan Girl * Machine * Jessica * Daniel Dildoman (The Evans DDoser) * Banjolele Guy * Parkour Guy * Miracle Durkle [[Category:Anomaly]] [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>t124umlmr6ua5v6ur88hcopg4rusfjt</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<title>Bus 177</title>
<ns>0</ns>

<text bytes="1385" sha1="44dp0oa2ztvheak9prxoba3n6n8ph3z" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Bus177.jpg|thumb|The side of Bus 177, 10/21/25]] [[Bus 177]] is the bus route from [[McKinney Boyd High School|Mckinney Boyd High School]], which takes [[Brayden]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], Matthew, and Brady to and from school. Bus 177 is the site of many events and photos throughout less people such as the red rivers. == Culture == === Red Rivers === '''The red river''' is a practice of dumping multiple bottles full of water all over '''Seat 16'''. It gets the name red river from the way water drips into the aisle and flows from the back of the bus to the front. === Water Wars === '''The Water War''' is a reoccurring event on Bus 177 in which [[Brayden]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and Matthew spray water all over each other, often targeting whoever was sitting in '''seat 16'''. === Seat 16 === '''Seat 16''' is an important location on Bus 177, as it is the seat that gets targeted often during '''Red River''' and '''Water War''' events. It is often considered taboo to sit in '''seat 16''', even when there are no other nearby seats available. == Stops == [[File:Bus177stops.png|thumb|263x263px|A map of the stops, focused on a part of McKinney north of [[Eldorado Parkway|Eldorado]].]] '''1.''' Lake Forest-N Briar Ridge '''1.5.''' Lake Forest-Arbor Hollow (Occational and only for Lauren) '''2.''' Sandalwood-Greenview '''3.''' Rush Creek-Crown Point</text>
<sha1>44dp0oa2ztvheak9prxoba3n6n8ph3z</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="823" sha1="476zbokxoz7u5xwoy1axc3nzeph4nm3" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Teddy swing.jpg|thumb|[[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] in a swing at Finch Park]] A park located in McKinney, Texas, right outside of downtown. Notable for it's pickleball courts and swings. == Areas == === Playground === The playground has been the site of a few notable hangouts and photos, such as the photo of Teddy and the photo of Vaughn on the swing. It is mainly remembered for its chair swing, but has a whole other playground area that has barely been used by Less People. === Pickle ball courts === the pickle ball courts have been used many times for late night pickleball sessions. Matthew has notably supplied the pickle ball supplys for all the hangouts. === Sand volleyball court === Has only been used for one hangout. Has also been the site of the less people Luffy Dance video. [[Category:Locations]]</text>

<page>
<title>Less People Immortality Theory</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>301</id>
<revision>
<id>845</id>
<parentid>805</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-27T22:42:11Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>845</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="4847" sha1="edaeqnm1zng2kyywlrybz7oypo5swcl" xml:space="preserve">The '''Less People Immortality Theory''' comes from '''March 6th, 2026''', during the [[Teddy Cuck Era]], when [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] philosophised a hypothetical where [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] is actually immortal and will live forever. == Context == After school on Friday, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] were alone in the call watching [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] screenshare Rocket League. This was a common situation at the time, due to many people not getting on during the school week. After argueing about wether fictional thinking was superior to philisophical thinking, the three created a what if scenario where [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] is special and lives forever without the possibility of death. == Rules == # All 10 primary [[members of Less People]] are specially gifted with immortality. This includes [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], Daniel, [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], [[Brayden]], [[Christian]], [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], Matthew, and [[Blake Jones|Blake]]. # The form of immortality is divinely induced. It doesn't abide by any simple logic, defying time and space. # The immortality makes death or any injury entirely impossible. # The immortality is eternal and cannot be changed no matter what. # Growth and change of the body is still possible, as the immortality makes an exception for it. # Immortals can communicate perfectly fine in space. == Scenarios == === Post-Universe === After the end of the current universe, Less People would merely float around together for extreamly long lengths of time until another universe-creating event is discovered. This universe would then also end, repeating the cycle. This would continue until Less People can find a solution. === Uncomprehensibly stacked === Every member of Less People becomes insanely overpowered and stacked in every possible category due to the unlimited time at their disposal. === Planet Hopping === In the earlier days of the immortality, Less People would hop between planets, likely spreading human civilizations and finding other distant human or alien civilizations. Less People could move in a touring pattern, quickly moving between planets, or they could spend upwards to years, centuries, millennia, or even longer periods of time on each planet before moving. === Vaughn Trapping === An idea where upon learning Less People is immortal, the lestopians immediatly trap [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] in an inescapable situation for tens of thousands of years, before releasing him after everyones already gotten insanely stacked like in the second scenario. This way, Vaughn serves as a time capsule from the past, with him still having his 16 year old memory, body, and self, meanwhile everyone else has changed drastically. After this, Vaughn would inevitably catch back up over time. === Time & Space Warping === With unlimited time, Less People eventually learns to manipulate time and space itself, revisiting the past, and creating seperate universes, realities, and dimensions to our liking. === Forgetting and Remembering === Due to the long periods of time, its possible that Less People would gradually forget their past. The time warping in the last scenario may prevent this. Its possible that after forgetting everything, Less People would relearn its own orgin after a countless amount of time. === Seperation === Due to the scale of the universe, its possible or even likely that Less People would be split up multiple times. Its unlikely this seperation would last forever, as time is unlimited afterall, but wether or not it would be enough time to forget the others exist is the real question. Its also entirely possible that Less People finds away to permanently link eachother together, especially after warping time and space. Another rule can also be added, linking them automatically, if it makes the scenario more fun. === Legendary Status === After living for so long, ordinary beings would come to recognize Less People as legendary figures beyond their comprehension, especially as they transcend universes. === Other Immortals === Its possible that there are other Immortals like Less People, just very far away. After a long era of Less People thinking their special, they grow big enough to find others like them, and end up entirely ordinary once again, just now on a higher level than before. It's then possible they'd become special inside of that level, too. == Role == A role was made based off this thought exercise, titled: [[Immortal Less People (role)|"Immortal Less People #Von still a twink after 10k years in capsule while everyone else got stacked"]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>edaeqnm1zng2kyywlrybz7oypo5swcl</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

Full name: "Immortal Less People #Von still a twink after 10k years in capsule while everyone else got stacked" == Meaning == Based off the [[Less People Immortality Theory]], specifically the [[Less People Immortality Theory|"Vaughn Trapping"]] scenario. == Holders == * '''Fraudizza''' (Brady) * '''Ghost Bug311''' ([[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]) * '''Ghost^3 DeinoFreak''' ([[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]) [[Category:Role]]</text>
