<page>
<title>The Mulity Menace</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>312</id>
<revision>
<id>640</id>
<parentid>578</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T20:48:48Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>640</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1276" sha1="47cr1kdk50gdlu4xqoy7vsk81ebdb06" xml:space="preserve">'''The Mulity Menace''' is a mysterious being that roams the lands of the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server,]] haunting those who do not protect their homes with a whither painting. It gained its name after its first appearance in [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] while building the '''Mulity Tower.''' It is viewed as a supreme being in the [[Whislamic]] texts and is seen as the most evil among the other supreme beings, including the Ender Dragon, Warden, and Herobrine. == Sightings == # The first sighting was during construction of the '''Mulity Tower''', when a mysterious item was sticking out of the wall, which frightened [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. # The second sighting happened while [[Brayden]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Christian]], and [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] were touring the world on a happy ghast. While flying through the [[Monk Mountain|Monk Mountains]], an invisible being equipped with an elytra soared through the mountain range, flying in and out of the different structures of Brayden's castle. It caused only minor confusion until someone clipped the event with medal, and looked back to see it was the invisible '''Mulity Menace''' who just flew by them. [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]] [[Category:Minecraft]]</text>
<sha1>47cr1kdk50gdlu4xqoy7vsk81ebdb06</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Lestopia September Return</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>313</id>
<revision>
<id>699</id>
<parentid>659</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T21:10:42Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>699</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="4957" sha1="ec1ia5dyvedgxa0guu5j3dl9lfmxnvb" xml:space="preserve">The return to the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]] in '''September 2025''', following the failure of [[Rape River (Minecraft Server)|Rape River Server]]. == Background == === Debate === After the [[Rape River (Minecraft Server)|Rape River Server]] failed, there was a debate over whether to make a new server for Fall Break, or to return to the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Server]]. A poll would be held on September 28th to settle the debate. Hating the [[Rape River (Minecraft Server)|last servers]] results, Teddy, Duncan, Brayden, Christian, and Daniel all voted to return to the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|old server]]. Blake, Brady, and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] voted to make a new server. Matthew advocated for a new server, but his opinion was ignored after he immediately quit the [[Rape River (Minecraft Server)|last server]] after dying to [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] death pit, and because he had barely touched the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia server]]. === Compromise === To get Blake, Brady, and Rahand to agree to the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia server]], a compromise was made to have a [[The Great Migration (Lestopia MC Server)|large migration]] away from all pre-existing infrastructure into untouched land. This migration was full of conflict and war, involving almost all players on the server. However, the migration lasted not but a few days, before the people who initially vouched for migration, Blake and Brady, got bored of the server, the original players on the server returned to Lestopia. === Ownership Transfer === Because [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] couldn't start the server due to technical difficulties, ownership was transferred to [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]]. This would lead to major conflicts later in the server, and eventually the end of the '''September return.''' == Thriving == The September return to the server post-migration was arguably the most prosperous time for the server. A multitude of upgrades and expansions were made around [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] and the [[The Holy Lands|Holy Land,]] including the remodeling of the Holy Land, which brought the territory to its former glory, and the addition of '''downtown Lestopia''' across the ocean west of [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. The main [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] area received massive improvements, including the construction of Tung Tung Tung Sahur, the industrial coal factory, the building of the dorm rooms atop Mulity Tower, which brought new life to the once plain and flat cobblestone tower, and much more. This was also a time of peak activity for the server, with players on daily, especially during Fall break. break. Major progress and inspiration circulated around Less People, making this age of the sever the most productive in the servers history. == The Downfall == === Backstory === after the transfer of ownership to Rahand, the power became very unstable, as Rahand in Minecraft history was known to abuse his admin powers when someone made him mad, and even just for fun, as seen in the downfall of Rape River. One player on the server who made Rahand particularly mad was Brayden. Brayden and Rahand were once on good terms on the server, until one time, they decided to have a battle in the large Colosseum in Mega Builds, where Brayden would steal some of Rahand's loot and fly away as a prank. Furious, Rahand would use commands to kill Brayden and get his loot back. In a turn of events, Rahand would be unable to find his Pants and Helmet after killing Brayden due to a glitch. Rahand would go on to blame Brayden for this event despite him swearing it wasn't him countless times. Although he didnt believe Brayden, he forgave him for the time being. === The Fateful Day === It was as normal a day as any other, during this period of the server. Brayden was working on the downtown district, Daniel was working on his lighthouse, Rahand was also online, his actions are unknown. Daniel finished his lighthouse, and asked that Brayden and Rahand come check it out. While Daniel was showing Brayden and Rahand the lighthouse, Rahand pulled out his soul sand and wither skeleton skulls, and started constructing a Wither. Brayden, realizing the wither was inside Lestopia, Broke one of the skulls in an effort to prevent the summoning the Wither and inevitable destruction of the City. This only enraged Rahand, causing him to chase down Brayden for 10 minutes, trying to kill him. After Brayden successfully escaped Rahand, and flew into the end, Rahand banned Brayden, and refused to unban him. === Inactivity === The server remained alive for a few days after, but with the absence of Brayden, one of the most active players on the server, It ultimately died out, thus ending the September return, possibly the greatest era in the history of the server. [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>ec1ia5dyvedgxa0guu5j3dl9lfmxnvb</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>World Spawn (Lestopia MC Server)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>314</id>
<revision>
<id>644</id>
<parentid>570</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T20:50:03Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>644</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1050" sha1="2ldnunn6cs4u7m25u7c9jlvowi31ap1" xml:space="preserve">The '''World Spawn''' in the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]] is a jungle at the coordinates "0, 0". == Landmark == At World Spawn, there is a nether portal and an ender chest, surrounded by signs greeting new players. The portal is connected to the [[Nether Highway Network (Lestopia MC Server)|nether highways]], with a sign telling new players to go West to [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. == History == * After the server began, the first players hid in the jungle trees nearby World Spawn for the first night, placing crafting tables and torches. In the morning, the players would move westward to eventually find [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]], where they would settle down. * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] returns to World Spawn to build a nether portal, which would be connected to the [[Nether Highway Network (Lestopia MC Server)|nether highways]]. Teddy would soon after summon two Wither at World Spawn. * The trees at world spawn are burned by an unknown player. [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]]</text>
<sha1>2ldnunn6cs4u7m25u7c9jlvowi31ap1</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>The Great Migration (Lestopia MC Server)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>315</id>
<revision>
<id>706</id>
<parentid>653</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:25:40Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>706</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="682" sha1="nrdxq2i6hgr8pnfchmzlb3jd4dahka5" xml:space="preserve">A large migration in the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]] during September 2025, immediatly after the [[Lestopia September Return|September Return]]. The migration carried the server members thousands of blocks away from all pre-existing infrastructure to new, completely untouched and unloaded chunks. The migration was considered a failure, as most migrants immediatly returned to [[World Spawn (Lestopia MC Server)|World Spawn]] shortly after arriving at the migration. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], and Blake would stay behind at the migration site. [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>nrdxq2i6hgr8pnfchmzlb3jd4dahka5</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Rahand Lestopia Shutdown</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>316</id>
<revision>
<id>719</id>
<parentid>718</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:55:53Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>719</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="975" sha1="03o4bdoj6r4ogc0vx7b9ta78kvzl5ax" xml:space="preserve">The '''Rahand Lestopia Server Shutdown''' was when [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] shutdown the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]] in early '''December 2025''', as a result of the [[ShirleyTheSheep2 Ban]]. After [[Brayden]] was banned, construction on downtown [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] was halted, and server activity rapidly decreased. After refusing to unban [[Brayden]], [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] shutdown the server due to a lack of players in order to save his money. The server was temporarily brought back up in '''February 2026''' for Daniel and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. It was later shutdown again after [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] refused to unban [[Brayden]] for a second time. This marked the end of the second period of activity in the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Server]] after the [[Lestopia September Return|return in September]]. # [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>03o4bdoj6r4ogc0vx7b9ta78kvzl5ax</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<title>The Whither</title>

<id>318</id>

<text bytes="563" sha1="rwewzzhs2f7d8wn02wpk72loibu7yfc" xml:space="preserve">(Pronounced ''With-Her'') The god and main deity of [[Whislamic|Whislam]], not to be confused with the regular minecraft Wither. Most notably from the [[Lestopia (Minecraft Server)|Lestopia Minecraft Server]], but also making other appearances (for example: [[Rape River (Minecraft Server)|Rape River Server]]). The Whither was introduced by [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi Kenzo]] at the end of the [[First Migration (Lestopia MC Server)|First Migration]] from spawn to [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia (the city)]]. <page>
<title>ShirleyTheSheep2 Ban</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>324</id>
<revision>
<id>721</id>
<parentid>700</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:58:25Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>721</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1305" sha1="qv3xsjh0kw5lipawik2uosdn4blt9of" xml:space="preserve">This event refers to the banning of [[Brayden|Shirleythesheep2]], who was banned on November 16, 2025 by [[Rahand - The Muslim|rsmzxz]] after stealing a Wither Skeleton. He was forgiven on March 30th, 2026. == Justification == -[[Brayden]] once killed [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] in the [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] Colosseum during a 1v1. After this, Brayden took all of his stuff prompting Rahand to /kill him. While the exact cause is unknown, some of Rahand's valuables were lost after killing Brayden. -Brayden repeatedly stole Rahand's wither skulls, which is what prompted him to ban him. == Contraversy == The ban was denounced by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Daniel]]. Rahand was taunted for banning Brayden, resulting in a period of ghosting by [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]]. == Demands == [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] stated that [[Brayden]] could only be unbanned after apologizing, then refusing to unban him after he did apologize unless he would record himself stating that. # He was sorry for ever making Rahand mad while on minecraft and other games # He would never try to annoy or make him mad again, and if he did, Rahand would permanently ban him from the server, and Brayden would have to be happy and okay with it, [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>qv3xsjh0kw5lipawik2uosdn4blt9of</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1161" sha1="0qqfxo80ifhbvpl3y8x6vqtpw3s4qkx" xml:space="preserve">''from [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2026]] and [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]]'' '''low taper fade''' is a role given to the 10 primary [[members of Less People]]. == Meaning == At the beginning of a new year, it is [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] tradition to drag the "low taper fade" meme into the new year, along with a list of other memes that is added to each year. In [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2025]], it was originally only given to those who took part in the dragging, but it was later given to everyone who is a primary Less People member. == Holders == ([[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|new year new me 2026]]) * 43old * 43old2 * AdminOf_ThePizza * [[Duncan - The Devil|Akemi's Waifu]] * [[Rahand - The Muslim|Coolest dude ever]] * [[Christian|Dani G. German]] * Fraudizza * [[Teddy Pemberton|Ghost Bug311]] * [[Duncan - The Devil|Ghost^3 DeinoFreak]] * ilymilo * JumiGoober * Marioawesome * [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahford Lee Watne]] * [[Brayden|Shirley The Sheep Watne]] * [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Theodore Lee Pemberton]] [[Category:Role]]</text>
<sha1>0qqfxo80ifhbvpl3y8x6vqtpw3s4qkx</sha1>
</revision>

<page>
<title>April 4th Car Crash</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>345</id>
<revision>
<id>734</id>
<parentid>730</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-21T02:46:43Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>734</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="4466" sha1="o9dw3ej4wf7fb96wkppb30k3wtuicu0" xml:space="preserve">[[Category:Events]] The '''April 4th Car Crash''', also known as the '''4/4 Incident''' or the '''Scoggins Car Crash''', was a [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] tragedy on '''April 4th, 2026'''. While leaving [[Scoggins Middle School]], [[Civdih]] was t-boned by a Ford Bronco, leaving the car totaled. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] were injured in the incident, and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Brayden]] were also victims. It marked the beginning of the [[Even Day Curse]], and started off the notorious month of April 2026. The crash happened as [[Civdih]] was exiting the [[Scoggins Middle School]] parking lot, attempting to cross the median and turn left. A Ford Bronco traveling 50mph struck [[Civdih]] in the back right passenger side, where [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] was seated. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] was (likely) knocked to the side, colliding his head agaisnt [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] shoulder, fracturing both. [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] also lost his glasses. == Injuries == === '''Civdih''' === [[Civdih]] was totaled in the wreck. Major damage was inflicted on the back-right side of the car, with the back windows being shattered. === '''Brayden''' === [[Brayden]] was mildly bruised by the crash. He also experienced ringing in his ears for a prolonged period following the crash. He was able to return home immediatly and recover on his own without any major disruptions to everyday life. === '''Vaughn''' === [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] experienced a fractured left scapula & mild concussion. During the crash, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] likely hit his head on his shoulder, causing the fracture. He was immediatly knocked into shock following the crash, and was shortly voluntarly unresponsive following the crash. He stayed in [[Civdih]] until paramedics helped him out. He was then taken to the hospital on an ambulence. He would leave the hospital the next day. He is still recovering, having to carry a sling in the meantime. === '''Teddy''' === [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] suffered a fractured skull (right orbital and upper sinus region), [[pneumocephalus]], and a mild concussion. He was immediatly put in shock at the moment of the crash, likely banging his head on [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] shoulder, fracturing both. After getting out of Civdih with [[Brayden]], he would wonder the crash site in a dazed state, and would experience memory loss for the next hour. Eventually, [[Brayden]] told a paramedic to take him on the ambulence after he kept asking Brayden what happened. Teddy would later remember everything other than the moment directly before and after the crash. He would leave the hospital 5 hours later. He would recover from the [[pneumocephalus]] and concussion in about 2 weeks, only being barred from [[cross country]] in the meantime. == Order of Events == # (3:15PM) [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] picks up [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]] in [[Civdih]] to go to the [[Scoggins Middle School|Scoggins Middle School Track]]. # (4:30PM) [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] arrives at the Track. # (5:30PM) All four get in [[Civdih]] to go to Matthew's work at [[Feng Cha]]. # (5:35PM) [[Civdih]] crashes in the intersection; [[Brayden]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] get out of the car while [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] insists on staying. # (5:40PM) [[Brayden]] calls him mom. # (5:42PM) [[Brayden]] alerts the rest of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|nynm2026]]. "Change of plans we got t boned" # (5:50PM) Police and Paramedics arrive. # (5:57PM) [[Brayden]] takes the first photo of the crash. # (6:00PM) [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] get on the ambulence. # (6:01PM) [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] calls his mom. # (6:18PM) [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] arrive at the hospital. # (6:58PM) [[Brayden]] messages [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. # (8:10PM) [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] messages [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]. # (8:23PM) [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] messages the [[Goodnight (Discord Group Chat)|Goodnight gc]], sending the "sweet dreams my love" gif. # (9:07PM) [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] messages "Duncan fuck you" in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|nynm2026]]. # (10:30PM) [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] comes home. # (4/5/26) Vaughn comes home.</text>
<sha1>o9dw3ej4wf7fb96wkppb30k3wtuicu0</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<comment>Created page with "On April 8th, 2026, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] would summon multiple Withers over [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. In response, a large amount of the server got on to fight them off. == Beginning == The incident began after [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] invited everyone to sleep with him in his Lestopia [[Whislam|Whislamic]] Church. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Brayden]] and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] would show up to his bed, and would struggle by breaking & replac..."</comment>
<origin>702</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1902" sha1="8eqic4gp9e02pqcwh09gnp2vbeq4e5n" xml:space="preserve">On April 8th, 2026, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] would summon multiple Withers over [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. In response, a large amount of the server got on to fight them off. == Beginning == The incident began after [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] invited everyone to sleep with him in his Lestopia [[Whislam|Whislamic]] Church. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Brayden]] and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] would show up to his bed, and would struggle by breaking & replacing the bed to sleep in it. In the end, Teddy and Rahand would end up accidently sleeping together to pass the night. Teddy would call Duncan a 'cuck' for this, which angered him to activate the withers hiding in the mountain bunker. == Battle == The majority of the server would get on to fight off the withers, including [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Christian]], [[Brayden]], [[Daniel]], and [[Matthew]]. The withers rose thousands of blocks into the sky, making it challenging to find and kill them. In the battle, Duncan was killed by the withering effect, and lost all of his inventory in the ocean west of [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]]. == Outcome == In the end, Lestopia only sustained minor damage. Many holes were left in the ground and on buildings exteriors, but overall nothing was destroyed. The primary loss was [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] inventory. After the main set of withers, some more would be spawned in just for the sake of it. When messing around with the new withers, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] was blown up, and [[Brayden]] took everything he had besides a stack of dirt & 7 iron nuggest that [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] had given him as a reward. After convincing Teddy it had been blown up the moment he died, he would return his stuff a few days later. [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>8eqic4gp9e02pqcwh09gnp2vbeq4e5n</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

They are built to make sacrifices [[The Whither]], and usually go down to Bedrock, although a pit of any amount of depth can still be constituted as a Whither Pit if they are built for [[The Whither|The Whither's]] sake. == List of Whither Pits =..."</comment>
<origin>713</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>

<text bytes="674" sha1="4bb81zlb66665viwsb6r0hsu30wb6fw" xml:space="preserve">'''Whither Pits''' are a common trope in [[Whislam|Whislamic]] religious architecture. First built in [[Lesopotamia - Rape River|Lesopotamia]] by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], they have become a staple tradition in[[Whislam]]. They are built to make sacrifices [[The Whither]], and usually go down to Bedrock, although a pit of any amount of depth can still be constituted as a Whither Pit if they are built for [[The Whither|The Whither's]] sake. == List of Whither Pits == * [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia Whislam Church Lava Pit]] * [[Lesopotamia - Rape River|Rape River Pit]] * [[Lestopia (city)|Indoor Lestopia Whislam Church Pit]] * [[Lestopia (city)|Blake Statue Pit]]</text>
<sha1>4bb81zlb66665viwsb6r0hsu30wb6fw</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>The Night of Broken Glass (Lestopia MC Server)</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>348</id>
<revision>
<id>717</id>
<parentid>716</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-14T22:55:26Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>717</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="553" sha1="7xb5sis5ly6uvv853zkxt9s9cxxlygf" xml:space="preserve">'''The Night of Broken Glass''' was an event in [[Lestopia (city)|Lestopia]] when [[Brayden]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] burned [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] house & smashed his windows. This came after Vaughn copied Teddy's house, inciting anger in the community. [[Mistreatment of Vaughn|Vaughn's general persecution]] may have also played a role. The name comes from the fact that [[Brayden]] had read a passage in school about the real life "Night of Broken Glass". [[Category:Minecraft]] [[Category:Lestopia MC Server]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>7xb5sis5ly6uvv853zkxt9s9cxxlygf</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Mistreatment of Vaughn</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>349</id>
<revision>
<id>1332</id>
<parentid>980</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-10-29T00:48:19Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1332</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="6146" sha1="pwe0uksxteqj19a4ipaa4n3bqbb0oho" xml:space="preserve">The '''mistreatment of Vaughn''' refers to the general disliking toward [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] by people, resulting in Vaughn receiving hate and mistreatment, justifiable or not. == Notable Cases == * Vaughn was once exiled from Less People during the [[Second Brady-Vaughn Drama|Second Vaughn-Brady Drama]]. * Vaughn's Celina friends once pooped in his older sister's closet. * Another set of Vaughn's Celina friends allegedly sexually assaulted him during the [[Celina Twink Party]]. * In the summer of 2026, Vaughn sent a girl from his school $30 at 3AM so she could doordash herself Burger King, with the condition that she "wouldn't ignore him next school year." * Vaughn is commonly insulted online by complete strangers, often in their first interaction. Less People attributes this to him being naturally unlikeable. == Justification == === Offensive Humor & Dragging === Vaughn’s humor notably involves of repeated insults and threats. This is viewed as very annoying, as many of his insults are unoriginal and plagiarized, and he drags them into oblivion. Vaughn will also drag instances or events of people making mistakes, constantly reminding people over and over. Vaughn's humor mostly consists of ultra-masculinity, gymcel motivation, and offensive jokes, usually including slurs, racism, sexism, ableism, homophobia, transphobia, antisemitism, islamophobia, and general religiophobia, or anything else that may offend someone whom he is insulting. This is likely the result of his dad's projection of his antisemitism and sexism down on him, as his dad is a self-proclaimed fan of Andrew Tate and the wider manosphere. [[File:Vaughn game is game.png|thumb|Vaughn (artic ships) talking about his kiss.]] === '''Gymcel''' === After getting unadded across multiple socials by his crush, Vaughn began to become super invested in the gym, telling his friends that she would come back after he gets serious gains. During this journey, he grew increasingly more inactive in Less People, as his attention shifted toward garnering the attention of girl's at his school through parties and online flirting. This would eventually result in him getting his first kiss in his car while he was driving her and her friend home. Despite this girl's negative reputation as being promiscuous, and the fact that she had a boyfriend at the time, Vaughn came back to Less People to flaunt his victory, championing "game is game," only to be quickly insulted and labelled a "dick-kisser." Vaughn got sick in the coming days after this kiss. === Bum Behavior === Vaughn is known as a bum in Less People for his reputation of never doing anything. He has done zero extracurriculars, sports, or other out-of-school programs in his life. In the summer of 2026, it was exposed that Vaughn had spent his break doing nothing other than watching TikTok and Instagram, not even playing Video Games like he used to. During the Less People Lock-In, Vaughn attempted an exercise routine, but he gave up after 3 days. === Stupidity & Social Intelligence === Vaughn is recognized as lacking social intelligence, often making very stupid and frustrating decisions. This is likely the main reason people struggle to get along with him. He often calls on others for help with basic socialization, makes weird assumptions and interpretations toward others, and generally acts in an ignorant manner. === Hobbies & Lying === On top of his bum behavior, Vaughn also has attempted numerous times to larp talents, skills, and hobbies. This usually comes after he does a select activity once or twice, and then he’ll adapts his entire personality around it. He notably did this with diving, traveling, shooting, hiking, rock climbing, and [[Vaughn Watch Collecting|watch collecting]]. He’ll also try to flex these hobbies as much as possible, often adding them to his social media bios and making posts of them. Vaughn once contraversially bought a bible and claimed to be a Christian, but never read it, and denied the divinity of Jesus Christ and the existence of the Holy Trinity. He later returned to athiesm. === Watch Flexing === In 2026, Vaughn developed an obsession with [[Vaughn Watch Collecting|watch collecting]]. He will often irrelevantly bring up his watches, flex them in social media posts, and rub them in when he can. At least it’s a real hobby. === Discord Friends === Vaughn has numerous online friends, mostly on Discord, whom some of which are very pathetic. Vaughn sometimes sends videos of them demeaning him and getting very angry at each other over little things. Vaughn’s relationship with them is considered unhealthy, and his association with them adds to the idea that he lacks intelligence. === Siege Raging === During the [[Rainbow Six Siege|Rainbow Six Siege era]] of Less People, Vaughn put in many hours playing with randoms to rank up and garner skill faster than everyone else. When he would play with Less People, he would insult his teammates for not being on his skill level and lacking game knowledge. After losing, he would descend into a fit of rage. This would lead to him being viewed as toxic and abnormal shortly after the [[The Great Merger|Merger]] === Brady Exile === In the [[Brady-Vaughn Instagram Confessions Incident]], Vaughn slandered [[Brady Jones|Brady]] as a whore on an Instagram account based on [[McKinney Boyd High School|Brady’s school]]. Vaughn would be blocked by Brady, who refused to be in any communication with him. Because Brady would leave any group chat with Vaughn, Vaughn was exiled by the rest of Less People to get Brady back. During this time, Vaughn was seen as a weirdo for his actions against Brady. The incident gave [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only]] a terrible first impression of Vaughn during the [[The Great Merger|Merger]]. === Earlier Justifications === In [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] times, Vaughn was less despised but still considered very immature. During Less People’s elementary years, Vaughn was known for being easily angered, cursing, and making lewd jokes about sexual themes. Vaughn also would send pornography and gory content on occasion.</text>
<sha1>pwe0uksxteqj19a4ipaa4n3bqbb0oho</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

January 30th, 2026.[[File:Duncan car civdih pretty parking lot.jpg|thumb|Civdih in a parking lot, March 5th, 2026.]]]] [[File:Duncan streetrace.jpg|thumb|241x241px|[[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] in Civdih during the [[First Celina Sleepover]].]] '''Galaxy Civdih Jr.''' was [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] first car, lasting about a year before getting totaled on [[April 4th Car Crash|April 4th, 2026]]. <text bytes="463" sha1="tu9xf63w1x2b2m5i8ymudz1qygozucl" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Teddy brain bubble.jpg|thumb|219x219px|Teddy's Pneumocephalus (The black space at the top)]] '''Pneumocephalus''', also know as [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] '''"Brain Bubble"''', was the condition inflicted on [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] by the [[April 4th Car Crash|April 4th, 2026 Car Crash]]. It was mostly harmless, but caused bloody sneezes and had a 6% morality rate. It would subside without intervention 2 weeks after the crash, on April 17th, 2026.</text>

<page>
<title>Even Day Curse</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>356</id>
<revision>
<id>869</id>
<parentid>868</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-05-03T05:34:39Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>869</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="768" sha1="80knpxxvyn97v96qvda9loe5o91u1xl" xml:space="preserve">The '''Even Day Curse''' was an idea created by [[Daniel]], serving as an explanation for all the bad things that were happening in April 2026. The rule is simple, if the date is even, it will be a bad day. This usually means some kind of damage or problem being afflicted on a member of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. In the later part of the month, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] beads signaled to him that the Even Day Curse had switched to odd days. They would later switch back to Even Days. == List of Events related to the curse == * 4/4/26- [[April 4th Car Crash|Scoggins Car Crash]] * 4/4/26- Sawyer Car Crash * 4/16/26- Matthew's Dad's Sideswipe * 4/27/26- Matthew's Brother Crashes * 4/30/26- [[Brady Jones|Zachylikespizza]] dies skydiving</text>
<sha1>80knpxxvyn97v96qvda9loe5o91u1xl</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Brady Jones</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>357</id>
<revision>
<id>913</id>
<parentid>912</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-06-15T09:43:58Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>913</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2483" sha1="tp5yrxoaluzv8r5t0boy59e031kg9c1" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Brady Jones|aliases=Pizza, AdminOf_TheWars|relatives=Blake Jones|affiliation=Less People|birthDate=10/5/2009|birthPlace=Arlington, Texas|gender=Male|height=5'9|weight=140|eyes=Brown|image=Brady heavy jacket.jpg|imagecaption=|race=White|hair=Brown/Dirty Blonde}} '''Brady Jones''', also known as '''“Pizza”''' or '''“AdminOf_TheWars”''', is the playful younger brother of [[Blake Jones|Blake]], and co-founded [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] (specifically the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]]) alongside [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] in 2019. Brady is described as very calm, lighthearted, and giggly. Despite this, many bad things happen to him. He often ends up a victim. Any attempt to ragebait him is worthless because he’ll likely just laugh it off, even if he’s secretly resentful. Besides his rocky relationship with [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], Brady is friendly, unthreatening, and overly-forgiving in nature. He’d never hold a grudge with you, unless you're [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. His main con is his slothfulness. He rarely ever actually gets on. Instead, he lurks in the messages of whatever server everyone's active in. He occasionally suffers long periods of hiatus. He is most active during breaks, specifically Summer break. For education, he currently attends [[McKinney Boyd High School]]. His taste is the worst in the world. = History = Born October 5th, 2009 to the [[Jones Family|Jones family]], he grew up close with his older brother, [[Blake Jones|Blake]]. In his early youth, he would start to play Soccer. While attending [[Wolford Elementary School|Wolford Elementary]], he befriended [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and they would create the [[TEO (Discord Server)|TGG discord server]] in September 2019. He also made it into the [[Gifted & Talented|GT program]], where he’d meet [[Matthew Pereira|Matthew]]. At [[Evans Middle School]], he would join [[Evans Cross Country Team|XC]] and Track & Field in 7th and 8th grade. He alone carried long distance events at the track meets.[[File:Brady mog.jpg|thumb|Brady on [[Bus 177]] mogging [[Brayden]].]] = Alias = * Zachylikespizza ** Pizza ** Fraudizza ** Ghost Pizza * AdminOf_TheWars * Skybomber118 * abjinc * way2fast91 (His fathers username, shared with Blake) * turdball202 * naruto runner * dragonslayer2009360 (shared with [[Blake Jones|Blake]])[[File:Brady holding club blake teddy topgolf.jpg|thumb|Brady at [[Topgolf Allen|Top Golf]]]]</text>
<sha1>tp5yrxoaluzv8r5t0boy59e031kg9c1</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="942" sha1="m3iddmvdpgqg8tmlgosn3qbpmzg4kgq" xml:space="preserve">'''Topgolf Allen''' is a Topgolf location in [[Allen, Texas]]. There have been many [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] hangouts here (see [[List of Top Golf hangouts|List of Topgolf Hangouts]]).<gallery> File:Less people top golf quarter zips.jpg|(left to right) [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Brayden]], [[Christian]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], aswell as [[Blake Jones|Blake]] through the window. File:Duncan sybau no caption.jpg|[[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] doing the sybau pose. File:Blake roman point.jpg|[[Blake Jones|Blake]] pointing at screen like a Roman Emperor. File:Brady holding club blake teddy topgolf.jpg|[[Brady Jones|Brady]], with [[Blake Jones|Blake]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] standing behind him. File:Christian brady teddy duncan friendship squad.jpg|(left to right) [[Christian]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] on couch. </gallery></text>
<sha1>m3iddmvdpgqg8tmlgosn3qbpmzg4kgq</sha1>

<text bytes="2098" sha1="0j7hph6qk3s6ugrgmd87pqnlicm0p4n" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Blake Jones|aliases=43old, Eggs and bakey|relatives=Brady Jones|affiliation=Less People|birthDate=May 7th, 2008|gender=Male|height=6'4|weight=190|eyes=Pale Blue|image=Blake pretty.jpg|race=White|hair=Dirt Blonde}} '''Blake Jones''', also known as “'''43old'''”, is the father figure of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], and [[Brady Jones]]'''’''' older brother. Originating from the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]], Blake is the oldest in the group by two years. He stands high at 6’4, and has a large passion for sports. His language and sense of humor is very distinguished. It can be described as cool and composed, and occasionally frenzied. He knows a large library of phrases, which he commonly integrates into his speech. Despite morally denouncing it, Blake will still ragebait you if the opportunity presents itself. Nonetheless, he is the most mature and reasonable in the group. [[File:Blake roman point.jpg|thumb|301x301px|Blake at [[Topgolf Allen|TopGolf]] pointing at screen.[[File:Blake in car frustrated dad.jpg|thumb|259x259px|Blake frustrated in his car.]]]] For education, he currently attends [[McKinney Boyd High School|McKinney Boyd High School.]] {{Quote | text = ong we ALL crank hog | speaker = 43old | receiver = | source = nynm2025 }} == Aliases == * 43old * Eggs and bakey * hitherewelcome90 * way2fast91 (from his father, shared with [[Brady Jones|Brady]]) * dragonslayer2009360 (shared with [[Brady Jones|Brady]]) == Gallery == <gallery> File:Blake suit fancy chico.jpg File:Blake homelander.jpg File:Brady blake california 1.jpg File:Brady blake teddy 167 169 daves hot chicken.png File:Blake in car frustrated dad.jpg File:Blake store jacket 4.jpg File:Blake girlfriend.jpg File:Blake scholarship.jpg File:Brady blake california 2.jpg File:Teddy blake volleyball argue.png File:Blake bronco hat.jpg File:Blake roman point.jpg File:Blake jones football evans.png File:Blake winner.jpg File:Blake brady lastdayofschool.jpg File:Brady matthew blake drinking.jpg File:Duncan blake laying down teddy tpose field.jpg </gallery></text>
<sha1>0j7hph6qk3s6ugrgmd87pqnlicm0p4n</sha1>

<text bytes="755" sha1="avvk39qxl2r4yl58nc2ch6taxf2wpgq" xml:space="preserve">'''The Evans Split''' refers to the last day of school at [[Evans Middle School]] in '''May 2024'''. [[Brady Jones|Brady]], Daniel, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], Matthew, [[Brayden]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Christian]] were all 8th graders at the time, and would be split between [[McKinney Boyd High School|Boyd]] and [[McKinney High School|High]] in the year after. This would be the last time most of Less People shared the same school. {| class="fandom-table" |+High School Devisions after Evans. ![[McKinney Boyd High School]] ![[McKinney High School]] ![[Richardson High School]] |- |[[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] |[[Daniel]] |[[Christian]] |- |[[Brady Jones|Brady]] |[[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] | |- |[[Brayden]] | | |- |[[Matthew]] | | |}</text>
<sha1>avvk39qxl2r4yl58nc2ch6taxf2wpgq</sha1>
</revision>

<text bytes="881" sha1="nrqnbo0z7gn9riso4dqjx1ekiaml8h5" xml:space="preserve">'''Goodnight''' is a discord group chat owned by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]. Originally created to discuss [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand's]] return after the [[ShirleyTheSheep2 Ban|Brayden banning crashout]] on '''March 5th, 2026'''. It would later be repurposed by Duncan to a [[Goodnight Gif Streak|goodnight gif]] sending center. It would temporarily be used during the [[April 4th Car Crash]], when [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] was explaining his injuries. A list of goodnight gifs used in the GC nightly can be found on the "[[Goodnight Gif Streak]]" page. == Members == * [[Brady Jones|AdminOf_ThePizza]] * [[Duncan - The Devil|DeinoFreak]] (owner) * [[Rahand - The Muslim|Buff Bass]] * [[Teddy Pemberton|Bug311]] * [[Christian|Dani G. German]] * [[Daniel|Marioawesome]] * [[Brady Jones|Pizza]] * [[Brayden|Shirley the Sheep]] [[Category:Discord]] [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>
<sha1>nrqnbo0z7gn9riso4dqjx1ekiaml8h5</sha1>
</revision>

<page>
<title>Shirley The Sheep</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>398</id>
<revision>
<id>822</id>
<parentid>814</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-26T03:48:35Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>822</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1201" sha1="6cv3pz49yilhj7waiyj4v4oq4u6sr1a" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Shirley 0.png|thumb|Shirley from Shaun the Sheep.]] '''Shirley The Sheep''' is a nickname/username for [[Brayden]], based off of the '''Shaun The Sheep''' character '''"Shirley"''', but has since become a prevelant inside joke. == Shirley vs Surely == It became a joke that when people would say "surely", they were actually referencing Shirley, or [[Brayden]]. Infact, upon hearing the word "surely", any [[Members of Less People|member of Less People]] will become instantly lobotomized like a giant electric shock. Because of this, Lestopians will often purposely mispell "surely" as "shirley". == Shirley Plushie == [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Matthew]] are considering buying Brayden a Shirley the Sheep plushie. == Shoop == [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and [[Daniel]] decided to change [[Brayden|Brayden's]] nickname in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]] from "Shirley The Sheep Watne" to "Shirely The Shoop Watne". Why this was done was forgotten, but it's likely because Teddy mispronounced his the word "sheep". [[Matthew Pereria|Matthew's]] nickname was also changed, from "juan two big cocks" to "juan two big shoops" just because.</text>
<sha1>6cv3pz49yilhj7waiyj4v4oq4u6sr1a</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="315" sha1="pqdzjkqkv0jieacg72dpzgbylaamj2a" xml:space="preserve">A cafe that serves "Blue Goo", originally discovered by [[Brayden]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and some others that I forgot after the [[Downtown Hide & Seek]]. It was later revisited on [[Quarterzips|Quarterzip Day]], where [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] ordered some Macha and stole [[Blake Jones|Blake's]] drink.</text>

<text bytes="646" sha1="9m3b5fgw31nbxx6hy8j4tzov4mv8ez6" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Less people top golf quarter zips.jpg|thumb|255x255px|(left to right) [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Brayden]], [[Christian]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] in quarterzips at [[Topgolf Allen|Topgolf]].]] On '''November 25th, 2025''', [[Blake Jones|Blake]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Christian]], and [[Brayden]] all met up to buy quarterzip tops at Walmart in honor of the meme (Qz's vs Nike Techs). <page>
<title>Goodnight Gif Streak</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>416</id>
<revision>
<id>844</id>
<parentid>843</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-04-26T04:41:12Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>844</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2673" sha1="s9zh7e69jeekk91m112wb19gilonhdk" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Goodnight first use.png|thumb|198x198px|The first Goodnight gif from [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]]. January 11th, 2026.]] The '''Goodnight Gif Streak''' refers to the consistent sending of goodnight gifs before bed as a showing of love within [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. It originally started on '''January 11th, 2026''', by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]]. Since then, it has grown to encompass more of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], and now has been almost entirely moved to the [[Goodnight (Discord Group Chat)|Goodnight gc]]. Despite being called a streak, it has been broken numerous times, mostly by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]. Before the streak, goodnight gifs were already used, although it wasn't nightly. The Gojo/Yuri goodnight gif from [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], for example, has been used since January 2025. == Rules == The rules are very loosely defined, but some are generally recognizable. # Send a goodnight gif every night, even when not active. If you have no internet, an attempt should atleast be made. Failure to do so will be taken as an insult. # The gif should be sent either directly before bed, or in repliance to someone else's gif. == Bad Night == Once, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] was upset at him chemistry homework. Instead of sending a normal goodnight gif, he wrongfully took out his anger by sending a bad night gif. In response, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] referenced "[[Nightmares Incoming]]". == List of Gifs == [[File:Anime-couples.mp4|left|thumb|The gif that started it all. Used by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] nightly, and sometimes some others.]][[File:Gojo-yuri (1).mp4|thumb|Used by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] nightly. It's been used before the streak since January 2025, but not every night.|left]] [[File:Picmix.com 13489747.gif|left|thumb|Used by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] nightly]] [[File:Sweet-dreams-good-night.mp4|thumb|Previously used by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]|left]] [[File:Super-mario-mario.mp4|left|thumb|Used by [[Daniel]] alternating nightly.]] [[File:Super-mario-bros-video-game.mp4|left|thumb|Used by [[Daniel]] alternating nightly.]] [[File:Mario-mario-64.mp4|left|thumb|Used by [[Daniel]] alternating nightly.]] [[File:Dj-pizza.mp4|left|thumb|Used by [[Brady Jones|Brady]] nightly]] [[File:Counting-sheep-cant-sleep.mp4|left|thumb|Used by [[Brayden]] nightly]] [[File:Omori-basio-omori.mp4|left|thumb|Used by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] once on the Bad Night.]]</text>
<sha1>s9zh7e69jeekk91m112wb19gilonhdk</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>AI Grandma</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>420</id>
<revision>
<id>1103</id>
<parentid>1102</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T19:41:04Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1103</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="896" sha1="qhkynnmcw47rwp64he25fyome236pmw" xml:space="preserve">[[Category:Events]] [[File:AiGrandma.png|thumb|Messages from #general in [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM2025]].|247x247px]] '''AI Grandma''' refers to an old lady who approached [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Blake Jones|Blake]], [[Christian]], and [[Matthew]] while they were eating ice cream at [[Braums|Braums,]] and began talking asking them about their sports and warning them about Artificial Intelligence (AI). This happened on '''September 1st, 2025''' during the [[Braums Hangout|Braums hangout]]. Earlier that day, it had been observed that [[Brayden]] knew every inside joke in Less People, so they decided to purposely gatekeep (or "keep it niche" as it was spoken at the time) AI Grandma from [[Brayden]]. They also created the [[Braums Lost Media Photo|Lost Media Photo]] for the same reason. [[Category:Characters]]</text>
<sha1>qhkynnmcw47rwp64he25fyome236pmw</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Teddy's Trail of Tears</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>423</id>
<revision>
<id>1125</id>
<parentid>1124</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T22:34:07Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1125</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1040" sha1="jyye6gvtoxyjknco15yqmyyxqcad90s" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Trail of tears map 2.png|thumb|283x283px|Teddy's entire walking trail on 9/1/2025, including the [[Braums Hangout|Braum's hangout]]. Made with Google Earth.]] '''Teddy's Trail of Tears''' was on '''September 1st, 2025''', when [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] walked through a rainstorm to get to [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] house, in an attempt to avenge himself and [[Brayden]] after [[Braums Hangout|The Rain War]]. This was directly following the end of the [[Braums Hangout|Braums hangout]]. The journey was 5.19 miles (8.35km) in total (including the [[Braums Hangout]]). On the journey, his phone got waterlogged, so his original plan to get there and then call his parents to go home was foiled. [[File:Trail of tears 1.png|thumb|The only photo [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] managed to take.]] On arrival, he managed to take one photo, but it was a struggle due to the screen being super wet and half the buttons not working. [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] was napping at the time, so [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] just walked home.</text>
<sha1>jyye6gvtoxyjknco15yqmyyxqcad90s</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Braums Hangout</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>424</id>
<revision>
<id>1127</id>
<parentid>1118</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T22:36:12Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1127</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="3247" sha1="oo1w89m604wmq1m0t7dq7xz9xwy4n2v" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Braums lost media.png|thumb|282x282px|The [[Braums Lost Media Photo|"Lost Media"]] photo]] A hangout on '''9/1/25''' that started at the [[Evans Middle School|Evans Field]] and moved to [[Braums]] soon after. It notably included [[AI Grandma]], The Rain War, and [[Teddy's Trail of Tears|Teddy's Trail Of Tears]]. A photo was taken, but an agreement was made to keep it niche from [[Brayden]]. == Participants == * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] * [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] * [[Daniel]] * [[Blake Jones|Blake]] * [[Brady Jones|Brady]] * [[Matthew]] * [[Christian]] * [[Brayden]] (arrived later) == Sequence of Events == === Evans Field === The group originally agreed to meet at [[Evans Middle School]] to play on their field. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] was the first to arrive after he ran there from his house, and he chased geese until [[Matthew]] also arrived. The rest would arrive in their cars soon after. They snuck onto the field and played with soccer balls and footballs. === Braums === After they were done for the day, they (except [[Brady Jones|Brady]] & [[Blake Jones|Blake]]) walked down Eldorado Parkway to [[Braums]] to get ice cream. On the way, they played music from the soon to be [[Drew Wine McKinney Tx Spotify Playlist|Drew Wine McKinney Tx playlist]]. [[Blake Jones|Blake]] drove himself over, and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] rode along with him. At [[Braums]], they were joking about how [[Brayden]] knew every Less People inside joke, and how they needed to make something new that he didn't know. They agreed to take a picture, which became known as the "[[Braums Lost Media Photo|Lost Media Photo]]". Soon after, they were approached by [[AI Grandma]], who asked them about their sports and warned them about the future of AI. They agreed to keep that from [[Brayden]], too. === The Rain War === [[File:RainWar.png|thumb|268x268px|[[Brayden]] standing next to his lick mark on [[Blake Jones|Blake's]] car.]] It began to rain. After [[Brayden]] arrived late, [[Blake Jones|Blake]] immediatly began trying to leave to go home, and he tried to take [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and [[Christian]] with him. To get out of the rain, he let [[Matthew]] and [[Daniel]] in the car temporarily (they weren't allowed to drive with [[Blake Jones|Blake]] and were waiting on their parents.) Not wanting to be left behind out in the rain, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]] began blocking the car in the parking lot, licking it and throwing ice cream at it. Blake could've just droven them home aswell, but he didn't want to due to Brayden's earlier antics in the car. The people in the car would roll down the windows to allow them to do this, sabotaging Blake. Blake would manage to drive away, and they chased after the car all around the local stores until he got enough distance to drop off [[Daniel]] and [[Matthew]] and then leave the lot. After Blake got away, [[Brayden]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Daniel]], and [[Matthew]] waited in the [[Braums]]. Eventually, their parents showed up to take them home, but [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] decided to walk over to [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] house for revenge. This became known as [[Teddy's Trail of Tears]]. [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>oo1w89m604wmq1m0t7dq7xz9xwy4n2v</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="571" sha1="4pvtm684byoh4a3d65puqmjklvi16zt" xml:space="preserve">The '''Bacchus Field Day''' was a hangout on 3/20/26. It took place at Bacchus Community Park in Frisco, where Less People played soccer and football interchangeably on Field 16. Originally, the plan was to go to [[Scoggins Middle School|Scoggins]], but it was moved to Bacchus because there were too many people. After the field, the attendees went to Chilli’s. == Attendance == * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] * [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] * [[Brady Jones|Brady]] * [[Matthew]] * [[Blake Jones|Blake]] * [[Christian]] * [[Sawyer]] * [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]</text>
<sha1>4pvtm684byoh4a3d65puqmjklvi16zt</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

Titled the "[[Matthew Aura Photo]]"]] [[File:Snowman.jpg|thumb|Snowman on [[Blake Jones|Blake's]] car.]] [[File:Teddy rich.jpg|thumb|[[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] holding [[Brady Jones|Brady's]] [[Brady's Money Stash|money]] inside the [[Jones Family|Jones]] house. <text bytes="475" sha1="1kptqh7l7u5g0cjgilar3tpdyhi3lr8" xml:space="preserve">'''Zoophilia''' is defined as having a sexual attraction to animals other than humans. == Vaughn == [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] is commonly accused of encouraging [[Milo]] to hump him by doing a dance similar to the "Jubilation" emote from [[Fortnite]]. This happened during a visit to Matthew's house during the [[First Snow Day]]. == Duncan == [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] has sometimes posed as a catboy. There is no good evidence he himself is a zoophile, though.</text>
<sha1>1kptqh7l7u5g0cjgilar3tpdyhi3lr8</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="613" sha1="cez7lvax8whmpwet7jd5mn4u92yag3d" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Milo|affiliation=Pereira Family|birthDate=February 19|race=Dog|gender=Male|eyes=Brown|hair=Brown/White}} '''Milo''' is the family dog of the Pereira's. <text bytes="635" sha1="5hvbf77nb4d7vljo15skd3xm09w2vpp" xml:space="preserve">A competetive multiplayer shooter game based around building and PvP. It is primairly played by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] nowadays, but [[Matthew]], [[Brayden]], and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] all used to also notably play it. During it's 2018 prime, it was played by almost everyone in Less People. When the Chapter 1 map was brought back in "Fortnite OG" in November 2023, [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] was revived to play it. It is famous for popularizing Battle Royale, Battle Passes, Item Shops, GaaS, virtual events, emotes, crossplay, and also having many pop culture collabs. [[Category:Games]]</text>
<sha1>5hvbf77nb4d7vljo15skd3xm09w2vpp</sha1>
</revision>

<text bytes="1568" sha1="dsd51bv9inrj1bv4zyd4cownk4gsaa1" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Matthew Pereira|aliases=ilymilo, MrMilo|occupation=Feng Cha|birthDate=8/25/09|gender=Male|height=5'11|weight=131lb|eyes=Brown|image=Matthew aura.jpg|imagecaption=Matthew in his winter clothing.|race=Indian-Asian|hair=Black}}'''Matthew Pereira''' is a member of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. He was born on August 25th, 2009. He currently attends [[McKinney Boyd High School]] as Class of 2028. He has a dog named [[Milo]], where most of his usernames like "'''ilymilo'''" and "'''MrMilo'''" come from. He comes from an Indian-Asian family. His sport is Soccer, which he has played since his early youth. He is very naturally talented in video games like [[Rocket League]] and [[Fortnite]], among others. His early soccer and gaming talent made him popular throughout elementary and middle school. He would famously carry people to higher ranks. He is known for his generousity. His primary issue is his inability to utilize his potential. From a young age, he exibited natural talent in soccer. He was banned from games on weekdays after not practicing piano when he was young, but despite this disadvantage, he still managed to become on of the best [[Fortnite]] and [[Rocket League]] players in his school. He likely could've gotten serious earnings or competed in championships in [[Fortnite]]. Today, his job makes it hard to seriously return to his old hobbies. He is a GT student. He previously was in orchestra and played piano. He has worked at [[Feng Cha Boba Shop|Feng Cha]] since April 20th, 2025. [[Category:Characters]]</text>
<sha1>dsd51bv9inrj1bv4zyd4cownk4gsaa1</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="907" sha1="mtgmc84ea71l5s9t0j976ukl4mibsxt" xml:space="preserve">Some of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] have made many attempts at learning how to draw, usually ending in defeat. == History == === Duncan-Brayden Wannabe Manga === In their middle school years, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Brayden]] wanted to create drawings to depict Duncan’s [[Akemi (verse)|imaginary Akemi universe]]. They were inspired by Manga. === Artistic People === In March 2026, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Brayden]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], and [[Daniel]] all attempted to learn to draw. They set goals to draw specific animals or subjects. (ex. Duncan wanted to draw cats, Brayden wanted to draw landscapes, Teddy wanted to draw frogs, both Teddy and Brayden wanted to draw small fat birds) This also led to the creation of the [[Artistic People (role)|Artistic People role]] in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]]</text>

<origin>925</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="3406" sha1="4ve90dqxldfy4nkpfrazjjp8malgex4" xml:space="preserve">'''Etymology''' is the study of name meanings. In this case, it is the meaning of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] members. == '''Brady''' == [[Brady Jones|Brady]] is irish and means "descendent of Bradach” and Bradach means someone who is broad and spirited, so Brady basically means “descendant of someone strong and energetic”. == '''Brayden''' == [[Brayden]] is also irish and means "descendent of Bradan” which is salmon. In irish, salmon was a big symbol of wisdom, so Brayden basically means “descendent of wise salmon”. While not the actual origin, it can also mean “landscape”, “hill”, or “valley” in scottish, which is funny because [[Drawing in Less People|Brayden wanted to learn to draw landscapes]]. == '''Blake''' == [[Blake Jones|Blake]] is basically meaningless in the modern day, but originally it actually had two exact opposite meanings, both from old english. It meant either “dark” or “light”. Depending on which meaning you choose, it could refer to someone with dark or olive skin, or someone who is pale, faint, unhappy, and fair-haired. == '''Christian''' == [[Christian]] is literally just the same as the word, means “follower of Christ” in reference to Jesus and Christianity. == '''Daniel''' == [[Daniel]] is a jewish name meaning “God is my judge”. Basically means “theres nothing to worry about cuz God will decide what happens to me”, like in the biblical story where he gets thrown into a pit of lions and comes out unscathed. Associated with being wise during tough times. == '''Rahand''' == [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] in kurdish means “multiple dimensions, layers, and aspects”. Basically means that there are many different understandings and perspectives of Rahand. Its a very niche name in kurdish, and is actually more common in arabic n persian where it means “free person” or “liberator”. == '''Duncan''' == [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] is named after the Shakespeare character, King Duncan. In the play, hes a lil sensitive father-figure who rules with generosity and moral order, but is deceived and killed in a plot to overthrow the monarchy. The actual name itself is scottish and means “dark warrior”, though its associated with leadership and nobility due to its historical prevalence in the scottish royal family. == '''Vaughn''' == [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] is welsh name meaning “little” and “small”. It was likely more of a compliment though, used like “little guy who we cherish”, especially in older iterations like “fychan”. Ironic bcuz we dont really cherish vaughn, but he is little! == '''Matthew''' == [[Matthew]] is a jewish name meaning “Gift of Yahweh”, who is the God from Judaism and Christianity. Its a religious name, associated with bringing positivity and being valuable. In the Bible, he was originally a Roman tax collector before leaving to join Jesus as one of the 12 Apostles. Because of this, he was considered a traitor by Israelis. == '''Theodore''' == [[Teddy Pemberton|Theodore]] is very similar to Matthew, It means “Gift from God”. Unlike Matthew, it isnt religious, and refers to god and divinity in general beyond religion. In fact, it predates Christian God. originating in ancient Greece, it was also associated with being a valuable boon. its oldest iterations expands it to “Gift from God to evil”.</text>
<sha1>4ve90dqxldfy4nkpfrazjjp8malgex4</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>808 Crashout Factorization</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>452</id>
<revision>
<id>932</id>
<parentid>931</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-06-17T00:13:53Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>932</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2385" sha1="gcz7gchwtt8bscjpgrvbtzagt3pcmg9" xml:space="preserve">'''808 Crashout Factorization''' refers to a chain of messages in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]] starting on 6/16/26. Participants reply to eachother progressively smaller and smaller factors of the number "808" in the format of "[small factor] x [big factor]". For example: "404 x 2" or "50.5 x 16" or "0.39453125 x 2048". The unspoken rule is that the same person can't factor twice in a row, so you have to rely on others to reply to you with the next factorization in the chain. Rounding is also not allowed. == Orgin == [[File:808.png|thumb|200x200px]] It started after [[Brady Jones|Brady]] was at the doctor, and he mentioned someone "crashing out" in the waiting room because he was told he had to go back to his car to get his drivers license. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] then replied "Is his last name 808? [[Window Licker (emoji)|:Window_Licker:]]", and then [[Brayden]] replied "whats an 808?", to which [[Brady Jones|Brady]] responded "404 x 2". == Notes & Patterns == * The bigger decimal follows the "Powers of two" series. (2^n) * Every decimal ends with a 5, and every decimal after "50.5 x 16" ends with a 25. * After "25.25 x 32", a pattern emerged where every decimal's ending alternates between 625 and 125. * "0.024658203125 x 32768" was originally sent as "0.02465820135 x 32768" due to an error in [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] calculations. == Chain Message Log == {| class="fandom-table" |+ !# !Factors !Sender !2^n |- |1 |808 |[[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] |0 |- |2 |404 x 2 |[[Brady Jones|Brady]] |1 |- |3 |202 x 4 |Teddy |2 |- |4 |101 x 8 |Brady |3 |- |5 |50.5 x 16 |Teddy |4 |- |6 |25.25 x 32 |Brady |5 |- |7 |12.625 x 64 |[[Daniel]] |6 |- |8 |6.3125 x 128 |Teddy |7 |- |9 |3.15625 x 256 |[[Brayden]] |8 |- |10 |1.578125 x 512 |Teddy |9 |- |11 |0.7890625 x 1024 |Daniel |10 |- |12 |0.39453125 x 2048 |Teddy |11 |- |13 |0.197265625 x 4096 |Daniel |12 |- |14 |0.0986328125 x 8192 |Teddy |13 |- |15 |0.04931640625 x 16384 |Daniel |14 |- |16 |0.024658203125 x 32768 |Teddy |15 |- |17 |0.0123291015625 x 65536 |Daniel |16 |- |18 |0.00616455078125 x 131072 |Teddy |17 |- |19 |0.003082275390625 x 262144 |Daniel |18 |- |20 |0.0015411376953125 x 524288 |Teddy |19 |- |21 |0.00077056884765625 x 1048576 |Daniel |20 |- |22 |0.000385284423828125 x 2097152 |[[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] |21 |- |23 |0.0001926422119140625 × 4194304 |Daniel |22 |}</text>
<sha1>gcz7gchwtt8bscjpgrvbtzagt3pcmg9</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="1730" sha1="9fc1k12lvehhh8hrbf52ztyx5jtek7d" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Daniel Magno|image=Daniel jesus.jpg|aliases=Mario, Marioawesome, JumiGoober|birthDate=September 6th, 2009|race=Hispanic|gender=Male|height=5'7|weight=135|eyes=Brown|hair=Black}} '''Daniel Magno''', also known as "'''Mario'''" or "'''Marioawesome'''" and "'''JumiGoober'''", is a key member of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. Attending [[McKinney High School|McKinney High]], he comes from the [[4 Only (orgin)|4 Only side]]. He's apart of Orchestra alongside [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]]. His name comes from the fact that he really likes Mario, infact he even has Mario crucified on his bedroom wall. He may also be Jesus, based on a [[Daniel Jesus Photo|photo]] from [[2025 Corpus Christi Orchestra Trip|Corpus Christi]]. Having the poorest computer in the group, he literally can't play some games. [[File:Daniel point.jpg|thumb|319x319px|Daniel from a facecam]] [[File:Daniel holding frog.jpg|thumb|Daniel holding a pufferfish in [[2025 Corpus Christi Orchestra Trip|Corpus Christi]].]] [[File:Daniel bed corpus christi.jpg|thumb|Daniel on a bed in [[2025 Corpus Christi Orchestra Trip|Corpus Christi]]]] [[File:Daniel boobs.png|thumb|230x230px|Daniel pretending he has boobs.]] [[File:Daniel mexico vs nigeria.jpg|thumb|Young Daniel repping Mexico]] [[File:Daniel new orleans bus laying femboy.jpg|thumb|260x260px|Daniel on the way to [[2026 New Orleans Orchestra Trip|New Orleans]].]] He is humble and unpretencious in nature, but he may also be seen as too modest and lacking in obvious vanity. He is very reliable and supportive, being consistently active. He is almost always in call very soon after it starts, and he even stays when he's not involved with whatever everyone else is doing.</text>
<sha1>9fc1k12lvehhh8hrbf52ztyx5jtek7d</sha1>
</revision>

<text bytes="895" sha1="51af9bnlxand5p3piks5nk3d4b957i8" xml:space="preserve">The '''Evans Cross Country Team''' between '''2022-2023''' may have been the worst cross country running team ever. At [[Evans Middle School]], it was notable for consistently placing last in every meet, the coaches having no clue what they're doing, and being full of potential-less bums. Nonetheless, it was very fun. Before meets, the team would pass the time by playing Retro Bowl and it's spinoffs, or they'd [[Cross Country Shenanigans|mess around]] exploring the meet area, playing pinecone football, or [[2023 Evans XC Meet|running though sewage tunnels]]. In one instance, two of the runners kissed on the bus. For Less People, it was noteworthy for bringing [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Matthew]], [[Daniel]], and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] together. == Notable Members == * [[Max]] * Ryan * Colton * Connor * [[Brady Jones|Brady]] * [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] * [[Matthew]] * [[Daniel]]</text>
<sha1>51af9bnlxand5p3piks5nk3d4b957i8</sha1>
</revision>

He plays Soccer and is in Orchestra. During his time at [[Evans Middle School]], he was also in [[Evans XC Team|Cross Country]].</text>

<text bytes="1582" sha1="8zoebjb42ix71tc6m659gq2d0uat8ue" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Duncan daniel corpus christi.jpg|thumb|206x206px]] The '''2025 Corpus Christi Orchestra Trip''' was a short [[McKinney High School|McKinney High]] Orchestra vacation starting on April 25th and ending on April 29th, 2025. It notably included [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] and [[Daniel]]. [[Max]] was also there. [[Daniel]] had a lab coat for some reason. The trip was succeeded by the [[2026 New Orleans Orchestra Trip]]. [[File:Daniel bed corpus christi.jpg|thumb|[[Daniel]] laying on a bed in a hotel room]] == Notable Events == === Daniel Vs. Duncan === On the night of April 25th, [[Daniel]] fought [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] on the beach in the dark with towels. [[File:Danielvsduncan.jpg|thumb|[[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] fighting [[Daniel]]]] [[File:Daniel jesus.jpg|thumb|[[Daniel Jesus Photo]]]] === Daniel Jesus Photo === Also on the night of April 25th, [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] took a photo of [[Daniel]] in a T-pose looking like Jesus with his long lushous hair. This became known as the [[Daniel Jesus Photo]]. === "Something nicked my dick" === On the late night of April 26th, [[Daniel]] was swimming in the ocean when something nicked his dick. At midnight, he sent the message: "Never going to a beach again, Something in the ocean nicked my dick and around it are scratches and the tip is dry blood a bit" === Boat === [[File:Daniel holding frog.jpg|thumb|[[Daniel]] holding a pufferfish]] On April 27th, they went on a boat and held aquatic animals or something. [[Daniel]] wore a labcoat and held a pufferfish, a crab, and a boring fish.</text>
<sha1>8zoebjb42ix71tc6m659gq2d0uat8ue</sha1>
</revision>

<page>
<title>Rainbow Six Siege</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>471</id>
<revision>
<id>976</id>
<parentid>972</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-06-17T13:15:14Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
