</contributor>
<comment>/* Notable Players */</comment>
<origin>976</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="8729" sha1="rhp8fhu3ieci5nip75qrpunjjrp5pgi" xml:space="preserve">'''Tom Clancy's Rainbow Six Siege''' is an FPS game that [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] played consistently from April 2024 to August 2025. It is likely the longest that Less People have ever stuck to a single game, lasting a over a year. They would mostly play a mix between custom matches and ranked, and would have mixed results. As a group, they would usually peak around Platnium or Emerald at most. It was originally found in the [[Lethal (Discord Server)|Lethal sever]] on the late night of March 28th, 2024, when the game was in the Deadly Omen season (Y9S1). [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] had played the game a year earlier, but still had very little experience. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] would gift the game to [[Brady Jones|Brady]] and [[Blake Jones|Blake]] because Brady intially didn't think he would play it much. [[Matthew]], [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], [[Ellison]], and [[Christian]] would also get it at the same time. [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] would get the game a few months later on September 1st, 2024, and eventually so would [[Brayden]] on October 19th, 2024. Less People would mostly leave the game after August 2025 mostly due to sheer overplay, but also because of it's dying public popularity. By summer 2026, even [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], who had been playing with randoms seperately from the group, would quit Siege. == Skill == Information from the [[Booster Boy Essay]]. After first unlocking Ranked, [[Blake Jones|Blake]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Ellison]], and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] would go 0-14 (blame [[Ellison]]). [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] would go off on his own to play with randoms on Discord stacks, and he would occationally come back to find everyone else worthless, and he would get excessively mad at this. After being pretty terrible for 2 seasons, the [[Less People (Friend Group)|Lestopians]] would have a breakthrough after [[Brady Jones|Brady]] began to lock in. They finally made it out of Bronze and into Gold lobbies. At this time, the late joiners were by far the worst (but [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] was probably the best of them), [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Blake Jones|Blake]] held a rivalry for 4th, and then [[Matthew]] would hold 3rd. Since [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] would rarely play with the group, it was unclear wether [[Brady Jones|Brady]] or him were better for awhile. [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] was widely the first "good" Siege player to emerge from [[Less People (Friend Group)|the group]]. His main strengths were gun fights due to his natural FPS talent and his game sense gained from his rando discord stacks, but he was despised by the others for his toxicity and boosting. [[Brady Jones|Brady]] would soon surpass his natural talent with his hardwork, and he would remain the "best" for the majority of the time they played Siege, despite never making any major achievements. [[Brady Jones|Brady]] was a decent fragger, but he neglected teamwork and relied on carrying everyone else. By the end of Siege in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], it would actually be [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] to emerge as the undeniable G.O.A.T. of the friend group, not only topping both [[Brady Jones|Brady]]<nowiki/>and [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] prime gun fighting, but also terramogging them with game sense. In the end, the top 7 rankings probably go: 1. [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] 2. [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] 3. *[[Brady Jones|Brady]] (even at his prime) (big gap) 4. [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] 5. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] 6. [[Brayden]] (could arguably be #5) 7. *[[Blake Jones|Blake]] <nowiki>*</nowiki>It's worth noting that [[Brady Jones|Brady]] was better than [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] for the vast duration of the time they played Siege, and he likely would've continued to be better if he didn't get bored and quit. It's unlikely he would've been able to beat [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], though, as [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] had far superior game sense and played more often. <nowiki>*</nowiki>[[Blake Jones|Blake]] had a bad computer during the Siege era, so he could've been much higher. He also stopped playing a little before everyone else, which is why he's ranked the lowest. In his prime, he was better than [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. == Notable Players == Information from the [[Booster Boy Essay]]. === Vaughn === After being the first to unlock ranked, [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] would mostly go on his own to play with random stacks he found on Discord. This carried him to Gold by the end of Deadly Omen, and then Diamond in New Blood. Due to this and his natural talent, he would quickly become way more skillful and knowledgable in the game than anyone else in Less People. When he would queue with [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], however, he would get very mad over any mistakes due to this knowledge gap, causing him to often crashout. This would earn him his reputation as a bad teammate, and he would get boosting allegations from everyone, being nicknamed "Booster Boy". His reign as the best would end when [[Brady Jones|Brady]] beat him in a custom match tournament on December 21st, 2024. After this, he would still stack with randoms, boosting his way to Champion. By August 2025, he still regularly played Siege and held the highest rank in the group, but [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] still skill-mogged him. He no longer queued with [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], only randoms. Even after losing multiple 1v1s, he still claims he's the best due to his rank, depsite obvious fraud evidence. === Brady === When [[Brady Jones|Brady]] first got siege, he thought he would only play it for a day or two. He would get hooked regardless. After awhile of being a mid player, Brady would take matters into his own hands and become the obvious best in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] (besides [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]) through practice in Collison Point (Y9S4). He would establish himself as a high fragger, carrying the rest of the stack with his often double-digit kills. Although he was stuck in Gold for awhile, this was considered to be due to the rest of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] holding him back. He would eventually obtain Platnium, and on December 21st, 2024, he would defeat [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] in a 1v1 tournament championship, becoming the Lestopian "King of Siege". His success wouldn' last, though, as he slowed down and eventually stopped playing entirely after August 2025. His inactivity would leave [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] to fill his gap and overtake him, aswell as [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] to catch back up. His legacy is contraversial, as although he was a great alternative compared to [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] and stuck with the group, he wasn't exactly the best at teamplay and strategy in retrospective, instead prioritizing going off on his own and fragging. === Rahand === After getting the game 5 months late in September 2024, [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] was far behind in the beginning. By the prime of Siege in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] after the December 21st tournament, he would establish himself as being one of the better players. He was generally overall better than [[Blake Jones|Blake]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], but still significantly worse than [[Brady Jones|Brady]] and [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. By consuming lots of Siege content, he would learn the game better than anyone else in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], and his skill was steadily increasing. Sadly, everyone else would mostly stop playing Siege just before [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] really got his chance to shine, but he would still undeniably surpass [[Brady Jones|Brady's]] prime in rank and skill by playing on his own with randoms ([[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] was still playing too, but he wouldn't queue with [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]]). In early January 2026, he would beat [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] twice in a row in a 1v1, proving he's better despite his Emerald rank (Vaughn was Champion at the time). By the end of his consistent gameplay, he was significantly better than prime [[Brady Jones|Brady]] and also largely better than [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. Not only was he by far the best in game sense, but he was also the best at gun fights, high-mid diffing [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] in 1v1 exchanges. Unfortunately, he would never get the Diamond rank that he deserves.</text>
<sha1>rhp8fhu3ieci5nip75qrpunjjrp5pgi</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Booster Boy Essay</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>472</id>
<revision>
<id>975</id>
<parentid>974</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-06-17T13:09:23Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>975</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="9315" sha1="idhpkno53iogrqvk631adgmtvyq38dw" xml:space="preserve">The '''Booster Boy Essay''' was an arguementative [[Rainbow Six Siege|R6 Siege]] essay written by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] on January 9th, 2025 at 9:11 PM in [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|NYNM2025]]. This was in repliance to [[Matthew]] saying he had better recoil control than [[Brady Jones|Brady]]. The essay starts by explaining and criticising [[Matthew|Matthew's]] earlier arguement with [[Blake Jones|Blake]]. It then shifts into listing off two "booster boys", [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] and [[Matthew]], and accuses them of boosting in [[Rainbow Six Siege|Siege]]. == The Essay == # Ok? Just because you have good recoil doesn’t make you better than Brady. Matthew, you may be one of the better siege players here, but your still boosted. Boosting in this gc has reached a unhealthy amount. almost everyone is "boosted" in some manor or fashion on siege. Since "boosting" just means achieving an undeserved title to represent skill, or "rank" as its called in siege, you could make an argument that every single person in this gc whose played siege and every single person whose ever been in this gc and played siege was boosted. However, can you really even blame someone for being boosted? At the end of the day, all you were trying to do was rank up while playing with your friends. Really, siege's ranked 2.0 system should also be blamed for the boosting problem. If you assume that the boosting was unintentional by the player, then its just all ubisofts fault and Ubisoft’s fault ONLY. Sadly, no matter whose fault it is, it's still a problem. boosted players who claim that they are more skillful than others might use their rank to back it up. Since it’s possible that they were boosted, this might not be treated as a reliable source like its intended to be. # After all, rankings were created in the first place so that you can compete against other players to achieve badges proving your skill and knowledge within the game of Tom Clancy’s Rainbow Six Siege. If it’s become an unreliable method to demonstrate skill, then its completely lost its purpose. Ubisoft clearly failed to establish rankings as an efficient testament and method to calculate skill, and you can’t just use something like KD either because it just doesnt tell the full story like ranks suppose to. The system clearly needs a rework to remodel itself in a more working method (which I will get to later). Until then, ranks aren’t techy, and cant be trusted. For example, on January 8th, 2025 (4:48PM CST) in the “new year new me 2025” discord server, Matthew Pereria and Blake Jones were arguing over whether school would be cancelled or not, when Matthew suddenly insulted blakes siege talent by exclaiming “this is why ur silver!”. Even though the original argument had nothing to do with r6, its still a great example of how ranked is still treated as a sincere judgement in many disciplines within the LSP gc. # After this, blake, knowing that ranking is inaccurate, uses other metrics of skill to prove hes not a complete ass shitter at the game (even though he is). This is an example of how skill should be measured (even if hes a fucking shitter). Instead of ranking, you should use actual statistics to measure someones skill, and not just one like K/D or W/L because that doesn’t actually tell the whole story. Someone could play support and benefit the team even more than some of their teammates, but still finish with the worst k/d or total kills in the entire lobby. Instead of just using one statistic, try to incorporate as many as possible. Anyway’s, its time to actually talk about HOW people are boosted. First, we will start with the first ever booster boy. Vaughn Lee Rutherford # '''Booster Boy #1 - Vaughn Rutherford''' Boosted to Diamond. When we first started playing siege, we all had such a small understanding of the game that we’d get shit on if we ever even attempted to compete with other siegers. Instead, we just stayed in our private custom matches playing against each other and occasionally tried standard matches against others. Initially, we were all on pretty much equal footing. Infact, this starting era was really the golden age of customs for this reason. There were no unfair teams or bad players, just fun to be had. I had played the game previously, but it was a long time ago and I was shit at the game back then and quit at level 20. # Vaughn began to rapidly grow in skill. Firstly, vaughn already had experience with other shooters like COD from when he was young and had thousands of hours on him, so once he got into the rhythm of siege, a lot of his skill was carried over, including his aim, which would become one of his most powerful assets. Secondly, vaughn had view many good players including streamer champs, pro league players, and even higher ranked players who he found on discord. These factors caused him to soar in skill as well as game knowledge. He quickly learned many things about siege that some of us wouldn’t learn until weeks or even months later. Vonkmad27 dominated the gc, and quickly unlocked ranked. He would act extremely toxic when we fell short of his absurd expectations, which would make it funnier when he would later be surpassed. We entered the Vaughn Era, where Vaughn would sweat his ass out, getting shit on in high ranked lobbies because his teammates wanted to use him to smurf, and getting swamp ass staying up late in shooting range. This, however, was where Vaughn became boosted. # The emeralds, diamonds, and champs he would queue with would win almost every game because they were smurfing, and this would cause vaughns elo to go to the moon. While me, ellison, brady, and blake were on a 14 losing streak after unlocking ranked because blake and brady were shitters back then, he soon reached gold, then plat, then emerald where he would finish year 9 season 1 deadly omen at. Now, while vaughn was undoubtedly good and far better than us, he was certainly NOT emerald. Hell, maybe he wasnt even on the skill level of gold yet (he did reach gold level skill in later seasons). Once I even shit on him in a 1v1 after I felt extra locked in and hyper intelligent and decided that I should run the 1s with vaughn. When his siege skill was insulted, he ALWAYS brought up his rank. Later, brady would coin the nickname “booster boy” for vaughn, which would evolve into “Booster BoyACEPTION” or whatever the hell it was. Eventually, Brady Jones would rise in power after watching a shit ton of spoit and other peeps. # After establishing himself as a deserving gold, it became widely considered that he was now probably better than vaughn. we ran a siege tourney to see everyones updated standings when compared to each other on December 21st, 2024. In the Finale, Brady faced Vaughn on Chalet. Vaughn would inevitably get shit on once again in a 1v1, ending the Vonkmad27 era and beginning the iloveumilo era. This 1v1 also proved that Vaughn was boosted, because he, a diamond rank, couldn’t even beat a gold level player (maybe plat its hard to remember exactly how good brady was at the time of the 1v1). For this reason, Vaughn is the perfect example of a boosted player and the first boosted boy in the history of the Less People GC. # '''Booster Boy #2 - Matthew Pereria''' Our second booster boy is matthew, boosted to Platinum (allegedly). As mentioned before, after blake was done arguing with matthew i got a lil cocky and called him a bitch when I said “matthew your boosted by brady” (4:49PM CST). Now, completely fr, I was really just kinda ragebaiting because matthew arguments against blake were lowkey ass and it pissed me off a little and matthew looked boosted anyway so it just seemed like the right move. Anyway, in this argument I would discover matthew’s boostification after checking his stats on r6 tracker and would begin the boosting allegations against him. Of course, they are only allegations, and you could just arguement that he WAS actually plat level at the time of his rank up and then just HAPPENED TO IMMEDIATLY START SLUMPING THE SECOND HE GOT INTO PLAT FOR 11 STRAIGHT DAYS. Like that shit was longer than even my slump. # But anyways, the theory goes that for the 9 days (that he was allowed to play, he cant play on weekdays) that Matthew was plat, at the time of the argument, he had not won ONE singular ranked game solo queuing in plat lobbies. Infact, he had never even gone positive. The only times he went positive or won was when he was queuing with brady, rahand, and duncan, who were all gold at the time, so he would end up in gold lobbies. Hell, sometimes he would even smurf in silver lobbies. Further more, when confronted about this, his only comeback to the allegations was that i’m silver, which proves nothing. This makes him even more sus (like among us). Eventually matthew said that it was because he was slumping, which I already went over. You could say that he just wasnt playing good in those 7 solo queue plat games because of lack of warmups or not being able to play during the week, but not warming up is YOUR fault and this was during winter break. Therefore, case in point, Matthew's a booster boy and him saying “better recoil with f2 on my sens than a r4c on braddys” was very ignorant.</text>
<sha1>idhpkno53iogrqvk631adgmtvyq38dw</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Less People Lock-In</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>474</id>
<revision>
<id>1279</id>
<parentid>982</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-26T02:51:40Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1279</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="702" sha1="jf7yf6cijt7jalwtxhct5fcs1rajkfe" xml:space="preserve">The '''Less People Lock-In''' refers to the period of time after April 2026 where [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] undergoes drastic individual self-improvements. This comes to justify the concept of [[Less People Exceptionalism|Less People exceptionalism]] using material success that can be more easily seen by others, and to make Less People the ultimate friend group. The idea is mostly supported by [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Brady Jones|Brady]]. The lock-in is usually focused on subjects like exercise, reading, studying, [[Less People Looksmaxxing|looksmaxxing]], and activity in Less People, but it really encompasses any productive improvement.</text>
<sha1>jf7yf6cijt7jalwtxhct5fcs1rajkfe</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="783" sha1="pjcwczavd1lb2u6a0vq7z6i94rtg72p" xml:space="preserve">The '''McKinney Boyd XC Team''', or just '''MBHS XC''' for short, is a cross country running team for [[McKinney Boyd High School]]. From [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Drew]], and Ryan's perspective, the team is also relevant for the [[Cross Country Shenanigans]], such as [[Doomsweek]]. == Runners == * Joaquin De La Cruz (2025) * Caleb Millard (2024) * Zachary Martin (2023) * Teddy Radtke (2019) * Ryan Caldwell (2028) * [[Drew|Drew Wine]] (2028) * [[Teddy Pemberton]] (2028) * Asad Zohidov (2028) * Noah Seiller (2028) * Justin Long (2028) [Quit in 2026] * Andrew Hayes (2028) [Quit in 2026] * Callan Mitchell (2028) * Jacob Todd (2029) == Coaches == * Head Coach Keith Pierce * Assistant Coach Maggie Steines * Assistant Coach John Matlock * Assistant Coach Gage Jackson *</text>
<sha1>pjcwczavd1lb2u6a0vq7z6i94rtg72p</sha1>

<text bytes="849" sha1="98xo9yc1cdbo4kojgnvhxrjlu6rwq27" xml:space="preserve">{{Character|name=Drew Wine|birthDate=April 4th, 2010|race=White|gender=Male|height=5'6|weight=124ibs|eyes=Green|hair=Ginger}} '''Drew Wine''' is a student at [[McKinney Boyd High School|McKinney Boyd]], and he makes occational cameos in [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. He runs [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|cross country]] and plays band for his school. == History == In his Freshman year, he befriended [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] in [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|MBHS XC]], who introduced him to the rest of [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]]. In his Sophmore year, he [[Anomaly Hunters|hunted Anomalies]] alongside [[Brayden]]. Meanwhile, he planned [[Doomsweek]] in [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|Cross Country]] with Ryan and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], while the infamous [[Cross Country Shenanigans]] occured.</text>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="878" sha1="pbw520yvr5sytyj9uw9qtsqby47307m" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Rahand iceberg.png|thumb|A slanderous image mocking Rahand's crashouts]] '''Rahand "Breaks"''' or '''"Crashouts"''' refers to the repeated phenomenon when [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] temporarily leaves the group, often citing ragebait and tense emotions. This started after Rahand moved to Colorado in 2024. [[Brayden]] is often linked to these crashouts. They are generally percieved as pedantic by the other 9 members. After the [[BBC or Twink Incident]], he hasn't since returned to [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]] (excluding occasional messages). == History == # [[Rahand Realm Rampage Incident|Realm Rampage Spirit Bomb]] (2024) # [["Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time" Incident|"Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time"]] (2025) # Gartic Phone Humor (2025) # Brayden Banning (2026) # Rocket League (2026) # [[BBC or Twink Incident|BBC or Twink]] (2026) #</text>
<sha1>pbw520yvr5sytyj9uw9qtsqby47307m</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>"Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time" Incident</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>488</id>
<revision>
<id>1092</id>
<parentid>1048</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T19:27:18Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1092</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1101" sha1="h6gb6pqgyoo33gn2ust2gltduj7zmuc" xml:space="preserve">The phrase '''"Don't threaten me with a good time"''' refers a quote from [[Blake Jones|Blake]] that caused [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] to [[Rahand Break Controversies|crash out]] and take a break from Less People on '''January 14th, 2025'''. This came after [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] lost a 1v1 to a Bronze V in [[Rainbow Six Siege|Siege]], and [[Brayden]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and [[Blake Jones|Blake]] began ragebaiting him. When Rahand threatened to block everyone, Blake laughingly replied with "Don't threaten me with a good time!". Rahand proceeded to immediatly leave the call and go silent. He would later tell [[Brady Jones|Brady]] in private dms that he was going to wait a week, and then ghost everyone in Less People. He would return to the group about a week later. This event would lead to the creation of [[Rahand-spy-team-6|#rahand-spy-team-6]], and [[Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time Gif|a gif]] created by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] of Rahand's face with the text "Don't threaten me with a good time" glitching on it. [[Category:Events]] [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>h6gb6pqgyoo33gn2ust2gltduj7zmuc</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time Gif</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>489</id>
<revision>
<id>1042</id>
<parentid>1040</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-12T22:19:17Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1042</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="968" sha1="2jmd7t7n01ksfzxti97ve3tccsa941x" xml:space="preserve">[[File:DontthreatenmewithagoodtimeV2compressed.gif|thumb|The GIF:]] The '''Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time GIF''' was created by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] on January 15th, 2025. It came after [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] [[Rahand Break Controversies|crashed out]] over a quote from [[Blake Jones|Blake,]] "[["Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time" Incident|Don't threaten me with a good time]]". The gif is mostly comprised of an image of [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand's]] face, with a glitch effect flashing altered versions of the image with the quote subtly appearing. It was created to mock the irony of Rahand getting ragebaited. == References == The GIF contains two references to other Less People jokes. * [[Blonk]] * Rahand's old discord PFP of a cat. * [[Vaughn Absolute Cinema]] <gallery caption="The frame-by-frame makeup of the GIF:"> File:Rahand.png|alt= File:2.png File:3.png File:4.png File:5.png File:6.png File:7.png File:8.png File:9.png </gallery></text>
<sha1>2jmd7t7n01ksfzxti97ve3tccsa941x</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Blonk</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>503</id>
<revision>
<id>1091</id>
<parentid>1064</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T19:26:42Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1091</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1244" sha1="az7ufuic1fm0y1k7jn9m9yko8lk3n4c" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Blonk2.PNG|thumb|The Blonk Image]] '''Blonk''' refers to an old inside joke/urban legend from the [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG side]]. "Blonk" is the malevolent counterpart of [[Blake Jones|Blake]], or sometimes his dark side depending on the depiction. The image paired with the joke is an altered black-and-white image of [[Blake Jones|Blake]]. Blonk is often considered a rival to [[Vonk]], with the name likely being inspired by [[Vonk]]. == Orgin == [[File:Blake logan jones censored.png|thumb|The Black Bar version, the predecessor to the Blonk image]][[File:Blonk3.PNG|thumb|The original unedited photo that became the Blonk photo. It was lost media until 7/19/25.]]The original Blonk image was created on '''June 25th, 2023''' after [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] sent a picture of [[Blake Jones|Blake]] wearing headphones in [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]], and Blake jokingly threatened to report the image for personnal privacy. Teddy then created an edited version of the picture with a black bar covering Blake's eyes, and then created the Blonk photo. The original unedited image was lost for two years, but it was rediscovered by [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] on July 19th, 2025. [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>az7ufuic1fm0y1k7jn9m9yko8lk3n4c</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="429" sha1="n23rao9j23jxtt3mr5m8nsk5iiacm8x" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Vaughn absolute cinema.png|thumb|The image version]] '''Vaughn Absolute Cinema''' is an image/GIF of [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] in the Absolute Cinema meme. It was created by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and uploaded to Tenor<ref>https://tenor.com/view/vaughn-lee-rutherford-absolute-cinema-von-vonkmad27-bug311-gif-18228464022412917069</ref> in late '''April 2025'''. It has since accumulated 961 shares as of 7/12/26.</text>
<sha1>n23rao9j23jxtt3mr5m8nsk5iiacm8x</sha1>
</revision>

<title>Vonk</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>506</id>
<revision>
<id>1093</id>
<parentid>1062</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T19:27:41Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1093</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="475" sha1="hgt0982faer27eht5dzpofabea6u5j6" xml:space="preserve">'''Vonk''' was a [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]] nickname for [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. It is likely a merger of the mispelled version of [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] name, "Von", which was common during the [[The Good Gang (Discord Server)|TGG]] days, and the [[Muck]] boss "Gronk". Vonk is sometimes depicted as a seperate malevolent entidy from [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]. The name would later inspire the creation of [[Blonk]]. [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>hgt0982faer27eht5dzpofabea6u5j6</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Muck</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>507</id>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="371" sha1="5vifvswhshbbptdhmsc9diqapf7nruc" xml:space="preserve">'''Muck''' is a fast-paced session-based survival game created by the youtuber ''Dani''. It was mostly played by [[The Good Gang (orgin)|TGG]]. [[Brady Jones|Brady]] was the best at Muck. [[Less People+ (Discord Group Chat)|Less People+]] was originally created to play Muck and Crab Game. The nickames [[Vonk]] and [[Blonk]] may be derived from the Muck boss, "Gronk".</text>
<sha1>5vifvswhshbbptdhmsc9diqapf7nruc</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="692" sha1="adlqk2e8t39at8rcz9fas434lpgyhzd" xml:space="preserve">'''#rahand-spy-team-6''' is a private text channel in [[New year new me 2025 (Discord Server)|New Year New Me 2025]]. To access the channel, you need the "[[Ragebait Team 6 (role)|Ragebait Team 6]]" role. The channel was created after the [["Dont Threaten Me With A Good Time" Incident|Don't Threaten Me With A Good Time crashout]], with the purpose of gathering information on [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] to monitor him while he's taking "a break". It's comprised of lots of random information about Rahand, some of it actual personnal information and some of it just funny jokes, for example: "favorite phrase: kill me!", and "least favorite phrase: don't threaten me with a good time".</text>
<sha1>adlqk2e8t39at8rcz9fas434lpgyhzd</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>BBC or Twink Incident</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>511</id>
<revision>
<id>1273</id>
<parentid>1086</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-26T02:39:38Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1273</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="2966" sha1="5jsdpfixwakdshhgxxru8dxwci1yzos" xml:space="preserve">The '''BBC or Twink Incident''' refers to when [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] had a [[Rahand Break Controversies|crashout]] over a would-you-rather question because it "conflicted with his morals". The crashout happened on the '''3rd of July, 2026'''. Since then, [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] has not returned to [[Less People (Friend Group)|Less People]], excluding occasional messages. == Story == On June 29th, 2026, [[Daniel]] was alone in the call with [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], just making small talk, when he asked the fateful question '''"Would you rather take it from big black cock or tiny white dick?"'''. This offended Rahand greatly, who quickly replied "Never ask me that again." Daniel was shocked but since he was alone he just moved on. A few days later on July 3rd, [[Brayden]], [[Daniel]], [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]], and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] were all in a call when [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] asked what everyone's favorite dorito flavour was, and [[Daniel]] asked feigning anger "Oh so you can ask what people's favorite doritos are but I can't ask what dick you would take?". Rahand began to argue that he doesn't have to answer questions that "wouldn't apply to him". Since Rahand refused to answer, [[Brayden]] decided to choose the "big black cock" option for him, and [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] remained silent. After that was settled, Brayden would ask [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] for another would-you-rather. Teddy answered with the Lethal Down Syndrome would-you-rather, and when Brayden was asking for the specifics of the scenario, Teddy clarified that the down syndrome person giving you the swirlie was black, to which Brayden replied "Oh Rahand, you're in luck." Rahand immediatly left the call. He would then move to [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026's]] general chat, where he'd argue that the question "goes against his morals". Here, [[Blake Jones|Blake]] would come along and begin ragebaiting, and Rahand would go crazy accusing everyone of being obsessed with twink dick. He would begin ranting about how the others "provide nothing to him" and how "he just gets ragebaited constantly". Eventually he'd decide that he's done, and he would ping [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Christian]], and [[Matthew]] to make a new gc if they wanted to play [[Fortnite]]. This would be the last message they'd hear from him. Despite his ragebait, Rahand would actually private message [[Blake Jones|Blake]] about how he's never coming back. == Re-Introduction Progress == * When Matthew visited Rahand's house during his [[Matthew's Colorado Trip|trip to Colorado]] for soccer, he joined the call on Rahand's account and communication was made between Rahand and those who were in the call on the fateful day. * Rahand sometimes sends messages in the TikTok group chat, [[Less teaples]]. * Rahand will send messages in [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]] every once and awhile.</text>
<sha1>5jsdpfixwakdshhgxxru8dxwci1yzos</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Matthew's Colorado Trip</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>512</id>
<revision>
<id>1198</id>
<parentid>1192</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-06T07:44:26Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1198</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="811" sha1="sqobihsvoq7r4e40svih8k61lwi7w9m" xml:space="preserve">'''Matthew's Colorado Trip''' was when [[Matthew]] visited Denver for soccer. The trip took place between July 8th and 13th, 2026. == Events == === Rahand House Visit === During the trip, [[Matthew]] visited [[Rahand - The Muslim|Rahand]] because he was nearby. At the time, Rahand was [[Rahand Break Controversies|mid-crashout]] from the [[BBC or Twink Incident]], so this was seen as a heartwarming visit during tough times. === Broken Leg === [[Matthew]] broke his leg on July 11th after being hit by a larger player during a game. When he was in the hospital, there were gunshots and police nearby. Back at his team's hotel, his teammates pressed the player who broke his leg, narrowly avoiding a fight. This event would later inspire the [[Matthew's new pair of legs|"Matthew's new pair of legs" joke]].</text>
<sha1>sqobihsvoq7r4e40svih8k61lwi7w9m</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

</contributor>
<comment>Created page with "The '''Pinkertons''' are the opposite of the [[Pemberton|Pembertons]], making them [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] direct enemy. They originate from Red Dead Redemption 2, and are only related to the Pembertons because of their similar names, which are only differentiated by 3 letters. [[Category:Inside Joke]]"</comment>
<origin>1090</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="307" sha1="bdhtmovp57vj567eza28w7s81g2vfqc" xml:space="preserve">The '''Pinkertons''' are the opposite of the [[Pemberton|Pembertons]], making them [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] direct enemy. They originate from Red Dead Redemption 2, and are only related to the Pembertons because of their similar names, which are only differentiated by 3 letters. [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>

<page>
<title>Braums Lost Media Photo</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>517</id>
<revision>
<id>1107</id>
<parentid>1106</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T21:13:43Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1107</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="731" sha1="t9l9rgyy100m13kt2e8rlbyww5cupm0" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Braums lost media.png|thumb|257x257px|The "lost media" photo.]] The '''Braums Lost Media Photo''' was a photo taken at [[Braums]] on September 1st, 2025, during the [[Braums Hangout]]. It includes [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], [[Blake Jones|Blake]], [[Matthew]], [[Daniel]], and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. It was purposely distorted to give off the impression of "lost media". The photo was taken because [[Brayden]] "knew too many inside jokes", and they agreed to never send the picture so that Brayden wouldn't understand what they were talking about when they reference it. [[AI Grandma]] was gatekept for the same reason, but that one was unplanned. [[Category:Media]] [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>t9l9rgyy100m13kt2e8rlbyww5cupm0</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>Drew Wine McKinney Tx Spotify Playlist</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>520</id>
<revision>
<id>1114</id>
<parentid>1113</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-07-13T21:43:19Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1114</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="917" sha1="e2xogg2gazski2ede5lypty6puk8qn8" xml:space="preserve">[[File:DrewWineMcKinneyTxSpotifyCover.png|thumb|The cover of the playlist. It features [[Drew|Drew Wine]] getting fouled in a [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|MBHS XC]] hotel room boxing match.]] '''Drew Wine McKinney Tx'''<ref>Drew Wine McKinney Tx https://open.spotify.com/playlist/4rG9tMgA8qIWhLO568Sgzk?si=4d68ba51366743ed</ref> is a Spotify playlist made by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], [[Christian]], [[Matthew]], and [[Daniel]] on '''September 2nd, 2025''' that consists of an assortment of cringey, weird, and bizzare songs. It is often ironically played on car rides. A second playlist called "'''(IMPROVED) Drew Wine McKinney Tx'''"<ref>(IMPROVED) Drew Wine McKinney Tx https://open.spotify.com/playlist/4dRmCjuA2u0rxOZtOYVo5q?si=8f408f568a134d6c</ref> was made later on September 27th to exclude songs that were almost always skipped. [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]] is also a contributor on this one.</text>
<sha1>e2xogg2gazski2ede5lypty6puk8qn8</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<page>
<title>NPC Builders Demo</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>552</id>
<revision>
<id>1197</id>
<parentid>1191</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-06T07:44:18Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1197</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="4148" sha1="djlgottu72ql7uc7zifo0q85h1hjxwm" xml:space="preserve">The '''NPC Builders Demo''' is a [https://roblox.com Roblox game] where NPC builders attempt to recreate your prompt using toolbox models. Making the models using btools. On July 29th, 2026, [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] discovered the game, and it was played by him, [[Daniel]], [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan]], and [[Brayden]] for one night. They discovered many different builds, and met some people aswell. == Builds == <gallery> File:MarioStatue.png|"Mario" prompted by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. The first build, meant to be a recreation of [[Daniel|Daniel's]] roblox avatar. File:The builders.png|"Build a clone of yourself" prompted by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. The NPC for some reason dipicted itself as yellow "Pilk Balls". File:Build me.png|"Build me" prompted by Teddy. You can see whats supposed to be Teddy standing on a podium on the left. The built model also had some sort of house to go with it. File:TeddysTimeMachine.png|(Inside "Build Me") Teddy's 2011 Time Machine. File:TeddysComputer.png|(Inside "Build Me") Teddy's computer. File:Image.png|"[[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]]" prompted by Teddy File:43oldOddieOS.png|"[[Blake Jones|43old]]" prompted by Daniel, it's supposed to be a reference to [[Blake Jones|Blake]]. Blake was nicknamed "OddieOS" on discord due to this. File:TheRake.png|"Deinocheirus" prompted by Teddy. It's supposed to be a reference to [[Duncan - The Devil|Duncan's]] favorite dinosaur. Instead, it built some kind of train. Punching the nose makes a ball fall out and roll around. File:Lestopia.png|"Less People" prompted by Daniel. Instead it made a Lemur Kart game for the "Lii". File:TheAI.png|"Build yourself" prompted by Duncan. The second attempt from the to depict itself. It seems reminisent to one of [[Brady Jones|Brady's]] avatars. File:ShirleyTheSheep.png|"Shirley The Sheep" by Brayden. File:TheRakeWorld.png|"The Rake World" by Brayden and Teddy File:Old tungtungtungsahur.png|The original "Tung Tung Tung Sahur" by Brayden. Also nicknamed "Vaughn in the future". File:RahandWouldYouRather.png|"Rahand would you rather" by Daniel. Reference to the [[BBC or Twink Incident|BBC or Twink Crashout]].<blockquote></blockquote> File:LessPeople.png|"Less People" by Brayden. The name of [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]] was temporairly changed based on this. File:RahandOrDuncan.png|"Rahand" by Brayden. Punching him makes his three stomachs go rolling. File:MallowGrave.png|"Mallows Grave" by Brayden. Came after Mallow tried to trick Teddy and Brayden into jumping off the edge of the map, but it backfired and she fell into the void instead. File:MatthewsNewLegs.png|"[[Matthew's new pair of legs]]" by Teddy. Reference to when [[Matthew]] broke his legs on his [[Matthew's Colorado Trip|Denver trip]]. File:FreddyFazbear.png|"Freddy Fazbear" by Brayden. When you punch the hat it becomes taller. File:Titan.png|"Titan" by Brayden. He has a button on his back. File:Laclapbomb.png|"Laclapbomb" by Daniel. It's supposed to be [[Brayden]], but it turned out to be a boat instead. File:Spinosaurus.png|"Large Ankylosaurus", "Bluefin Tuna", and "Tiger" all by Brayden File:Bug311spider.png|"Bug311" by Brayden, supposed to be [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] File:Propaganda.png|"Propaganda" by Brayden File:PropagandaPoint.png|"Propaganda Point" by Brayden, a long wall of Fallout propaganda bisecting the map. File:Hangingguy.png|"City" by Brayden. Instead it made a man hanging by a chain. </gallery> == People == === Mallow === A girl that kept detroying [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy's]] Deinochirus. Her and Teddy had multiple fights. She revealed that her uncle died in combat, and that she bleeds a lot. Her and Teddy had some sexual relations. === Luka === Another homewrecker that would punch holes in Purple Guy. He was dressed as Angel Devil from Chainsaw Man. === evil_foodeat3r === A friend of Luka who would defend him in times of need. === hamster === Some annoying asshole who got ragebaited by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and wouldn't let it go for like 20 minutes. He would run up to them and go "what did I do?" over and over again. [[Brayden]] was very annoyed by him.</text>
<sha1>djlgottu72ql7uc7zifo0q85h1hjxwm</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<title>Matthew's new pair of legs</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>553</id>
<revision>
<id>1196</id>

<text bytes="502" sha1="bh53t6qme3udm0itfzx8tblkfddsnbn" xml:space="preserve">[[File:MatthewsNewLegs.png|thumb|The Original, from NPC Builders Demo (July 29th, 2026)]] '''Matthew's new pair of legs''' is an inside joke mostly between [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]], referencing [[Matthew's Colorado Trip|Matthew's broken legs]] from late 2026. It originated from the [[NPC Builders Demo]] night on July 29th, 2026, when [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] prompted "Matthew's new pair of legs", and the game built a decal of the NBA player Klay Thompson. [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>bh53t6qme3udm0itfzx8tblkfddsnbn</sha1>

<page>
<title>Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>554</id>
<revision>
<id>1248</id>
<parentid>1247</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-07T02:11:10Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1248</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1091" sha1="pcaqfd3irkkmrfd1r2m0d0bb90iynq8" xml:space="preserve">'''Ellis''', famously titled '''"The Anomaly Hunter"''', is a student at [[McKinney Boyd High School]] and an alleged [[Anomaly Hunters|Anomaly Hunter]]. Being a [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|school cross country runner]], he notably sprints through the cafeteria and halls. No matter the destination, near or far, he'll always get there in a short haste. Legend states that he possesses powerful eyes, which can sense [[Anomalies]], making him one of the few people aware of [[Anomalies]]. The eyes are said to also make him extreamly powerful, allowing him to defeat even the most strongest [[Anomalies]], like [[King Von]]. His sprints are often explained as him rushing to attend to occult [[Anomalies|Anomaly]] buisness. He is considered the most powerful being in the [[Anomalies|Anomaly Verse]], being stronger than every [[Anomalies|Anomaly]] and every other [[Anomaly Hunters|Anomaly Hunter]]. Ironically, despite all of this, Ellis doesn't actually know anything about the [[Anomalies]] at all. He died whist trying to escape the [[Anomaly Massacre]]. [[Category:Anomaly]]</text>
<sha1>pcaqfd3irkkmrfd1r2m0d0bb90iynq8</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Anomaly Hunters</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>555</id>
<revision>
<id>1260</id>
<parentid>1259</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-07T02:39:10Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1260</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1322" sha1="p7rk5j4y1ikugeoc5ukqd0cx08hb1po" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Teddy mec.png|thumb|[[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] posing on [[Mec|the Mec]].|294x294px]] '''Anomaly Hunters''' are people who are aware of, pointing out, or interacting with [[Anomalies]]. The definition encompasses the conceptualizers of [[Anomalies]] [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] & [[Brayden]], aswell as [[Drew]], [[Brady Jones|Brady]], and alledgedly [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|Ellis]]. According to legend, [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|Ellis]] is also the only Anomaly Hunter to actually combat the [[Anomalies]] antics and rival them in power. He is considered the strongest Anomaly Hunter by the others, even holding the nickname [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|"The Anomaly Hunter"]]. He has no relations with the other Anomaly Hunters, who are all interdependent of eachother. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]] created the concept of [[Anomalies]], and are responsible for the vast majority of their names. They became aware of [[Anomalies]] in late 2025, during their Freshman year at [[McKinney Boyd High School|McKinney Boyd]]. [[Brady Jones|Brady]] and [[Drew]] were both made aware of the [[Anomalies]] by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]]. [[Drew]] is especially credited for naming and interacting with many [[Anomalies]] in his Sophomore year alongside [[Brayden]]. [[Category:Anomaly]]</text>
<sha1>p7rk5j4y1ikugeoc5ukqd0cx08hb1po</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<comment>Created page with "'''Cross Country Shenanigans''' encompass any action in Cross Country that is absurd or rule-breaking. == The Worst Trio == The Worst Trio, also sometimes called "The Bum Trio", "The Unholy Trio", or "The Trouble Trio", consists of [[Drew]], Ryan, and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. They are called this because they are usually responsible, or atleast intertwined, with whatever trouble was happening on the [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|MBHS XC Team]]. They are the origin..."</comment>
<origin>1221</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="6033" sha1="nb655buqdnmaxioljbp0p93xqgi4x0q" xml:space="preserve">'''Cross Country Shenanigans''' encompass any action in Cross Country that is absurd or rule-breaking. == The Worst Trio == The Worst Trio, also sometimes called "The Bum Trio", "The Unholy Trio", or "The Trouble Trio", consists of [[Drew]], Ryan, and [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. They are called this because they are usually responsible, or atleast intertwined, with whatever trouble was happening on the [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|MBHS XC Team]]. They are the original conspirators of Doomsweek, the roots of the Sophomore Jumpings, and the scourges of coach. Although not aligned with the trio, Asad also tags along with them as an observer. == Sophomore Year Jumpings == During the 2025-2026 school year, some of the Sophomores of the [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|MBHS XC Team]] would indulge in "jumpings", which were basically just dogpile tickle and water bottle fights. The Worst Trio had a large part in this, being the primary culprits the vast majority of the time. The target of these mass attacks were decided by who had made the biggest offense of the day. Since anything actually worth offense was rare, these "offenses" were usually small things, like insulting someone at the breakfast table, or turning off someone's laptop. After being offended or crossed, the offended one would try to organize a team to suprise attack the offender. People would often work as double-agents, though, meaning that you could trust nobody, and your own team might turn on you at the instant of the planned strike in a moment of sudden betrayal and trickery. The methods of attack used in these operations were either hands or squirtable water bottles. In the case of hands, they were really inefficent. Nobody wants to unironically hurt the target, so they usually just involve running up on the target, grabbing them, pulling them, pushing them, punching them below the head, kicking them, tickling them, and dragging them. Teddy would utilize his fingers to jab the target aswell. The goal of these jumpings were to either get the target on the ground in a state of defensivelessness submission, or steal something of value from them. These jumping almost always failed, as half the team would abandon you a second after the strike begins, often leaving the target and coordinator in a one-on-one fight, in which case the spooked target will usually rock the now stranded coordinator. In the case of water bottles, it was a different story. Water bottles were treated as full on weapons, you could hold a whole table at gunpoint with one and taste the fear in the air. Standoffs and hostage situations were very common, which were often the basis for later jumpings. In the jumpings, water bottle attacks had a much higher success rate, as it's much easier to just soak the target from a range and run away than to physically force him to the ground and pry something from his hands/bag. Water represented power and control. Being wet represented your own bloodshed, shame and defeat, the symbol of being brutalized. Although easier than the hand attacks, water bottles were much more obvious to coach, and were therefore far less common. In the later part of the year, the amount of jumpings reached a peak. Practically everyday, people would be "slimed". The infamous slime squad would plot the next target and attack hours before school ever starts. Rivalries and alliances lasted days, and poltics appeared in the morning Cross Country breakfasts. Eventually, the Worst Trio got in trouble with a police officer for spraying Justin, and would get scolded and sat by coach for a week. After this, the jumpings were toned down a notch, as fear of disipline rose. This all helped create sentiments of insanity and disorder, which would eventually inspire [[Doomsweek]]. == MBHS Locker Room == == MBHS Mid-Meet Incidents == During the meets of the [[MBHS Cross Country Running Team|MBHS XC Team]], multiple incidents would transpire. === Toliet Water Incident === At one 2024 meet, Ryan and [[Drew]] took Callan's water bottle and filled it up with toliet water. After failing to trick Callan into drinking it, Ryan takes a sip from it infront of Callan to prove it's safe. Callan still refuses to drink from it, and now Ryan has willingly consumed toliet water. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] was absent from this meet. === Eggplant Incident === At one of the 2-mile 2025 meet, the Worst Trio bought an eggplant from a store that the team stopped at prior to their meet, aswell as some watermelons and waterguns. At the meet, they began to joke about the eggplant as a superstitious symbol of hope and success. They promised people who finished a bite of the eggplant, and urged their teammates to finish faster to get a bite of the eggplant as soon as possible. The watermelons were also smashed on a pavilion's pavement and eaten off the floor for good luck. The superstitions of the eggplant did actually work on Teddy and Andrew Hayes, who PR'd by a decent margin, inspired by the eggplant. As they ran, they stuck together and yelled to the other runners homosexual things. === Ron Poe Tower Incident === At the 2026 Ron Poe Stadium Track Meet, a large group of MBHS XC runners, joined by some track kids aswell, ascended the Ron Poe Stadium's tower after finding the elevator unlocked. It was then used as part of a hide-and-seek game. == Evans XC == In the [[Evans XC Team]] from 2022-2023, conditions were terrible. Ineffective practice methods caused poor performance, and team morale was non-existant. Everyone on the team knew they were probably the worst in the district. This sparked a sense of carelessness, which led to a lot of pre-meet messing around. In 2023, the 8th graders would commonly play pinecone football or make out as a passtime. During the preparation time for the [[2023 Evans XC Meet|2023 Evans Meet]], the 8th graders left to explore the area around the meet, including a drained sewage tunnel. This resulted in all the explorers getting terrible times in the actual meet.</text>
<sha1>nb655buqdnmaxioljbp0p93xqgi4x0q</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="702" sha1="8ozxmern5qintjfdjngls96stoizdhj" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Bathroom guy.png|thumb|He can be seen in the stall on the right.]] '''Bathroom Guy''' was an [[Anomalies|Anomaly]] at [[McKinney Boyd High School]]. He earned his named after being caught hiding in the girls bathroom. He was caught after he appeared in the background of two girl's selfie photo. The girls, who hadn't been aware of him at the time of taking the selfie, had noticed him after reviewing the photo. Thinking the photo was very funny, one of the girls gave it to [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Drew]], who then showed [[Brayden]]. After this, he was nicknamed '''"Bathroom Guy"''' and was hunted down. His red backpack served as his main identifying feature. [[Category:Anomaly]]</text>
<sha1>8ozxmern5qintjfdjngls96stoizdhj</sha1>
</revision>

<text bytes="809" sha1="py76xprs7go16qm93y7jx5iqts46eei" xml:space="preserve">[[File:King von 2.jpg|thumb|King Von as a senior nearing graduation.]] '''King Von''' was an [[Anomalies|Anomaly]] at [[McKinney Boyd High School]]. He was named "King Von" due to his resemblance to the late rapper. He was easily identifiable by his black & red Naruto Akatsuki-themed backpack and his black durag cap. King Von is considered one of the most dangerous [[Anomalies]], being a serial killer and all. He was often painted as [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|Ellis']] anomaly rival. He was going to graduate in the Class of 2025. His picture was temporarily displayed on the college-bound board in the hallway. [[File:King von 3.jpg|thumb|King Von and his iconic backpack.]] Unfortunately, he died before he could graduate after making a last stand in the [[Anomaly Massacre]]. [[Category:Anomaly]]</text>
<sha1>py76xprs7go16qm93y7jx5iqts46eei</sha1>
</revision>

<page>
<title>Anomaly Massacre</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>565</id>
<revision>
<id>1270</id>
<parentid>1269</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-09-07T02:46:10Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<origin>1270</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1430" sha1="a478cilt4pl79uo8ddmdruqie5vtru7" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Layguy dead.jpg|thumb|237x237px|Sculpture of [[Lay Guy]] dead]] The '''Anomaly Massacre''' happened at [[McKinney Boyd High School]] on the last day of school, May 22nd, 2025. Only lasting a few minutes, it left atleast 5 [[Anomalies]] and [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|Ellis]] deceased. It was carried out by the [[Assassins]][[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|.]] The motives of the [[Assassins]] in killing the [[Anomalies]] are unknown. The attack was sudden and swift, with the [[Anomalies]] having no prior knowledge of what was coming, and no time to react to the attack or fight back. == Chronology == # The [[Assassins]] snipe [[Sit Guy]], who was sitting in his chair as usual. # [[Lay Guy]], who was laying nearby [[Sit Guy]], attempted to get away by hopping on one leg (His other leg cannot be straightened). He was soon shot aswell. # [[The Outcast]] dies somehow. The exact way he died was forgotten, but it had something to do with pizza. # [[The Charger]] dies after a suicide bombing, in which he rushed a group of [[Assassins]], screeching and shaking until his detonation, blowing up himself and the [[Assassins]]. # [[King Von]] makes his last stand agaisnt the [[Assassins]]. He takes down a few before he falls. # [[Ellis - The Anomaly Hunter|Ellis]], realizing that any effort to fight back is futile, sprints out to the parking lot. He is then shot and killed. [[Category:Anomaly]] [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>a478cilt4pl79uo8ddmdruqie5vtru7</sha1>
</revision>
</page>
<page>
<title>Lay Guy</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>566</id>

<text bytes="515" sha1="mkwn26acvop2hvsu4qmca5bf06h0ujr" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Layguy.png|thumb|Recreational art of Lay Guy]] '''Lay Guy''' was an [[Anomalies|Anomaly]] at [[McKinney Boyd High School]]. He was notorious for laying down on some bricks overlooking the cafeteria in the main hall. His posture is always exactly the same, with his left leg propped up, and the right leg straight, one hand holding either a phone or book. [[File:Layguy photo.jpg|thumb|Lay Guy (left) and Brown Backpack Guy (middle)]] He died in the [[Anomaly Massacre|Anomaly Massacre.]] [[Category:Anomaly]]</text>
<sha1>mkwn26acvop2hvsu4qmca5bf06h0ujr</sha1>
</revision>

<text bytes="674" sha1="esgvb2fd1h7mf5wsf4affra09s6zf9p" xml:space="preserve">The '''Vaughn Gym Arc''' refers to the period of time that [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] has attended the gym. It started on '''July 5th, 2026''', when [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]], [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]], and [[Matthew]] first checked out a local Planet Fitness, and continues to the present. It is considered part of the [[Less People Lock-In]], although it is now highly criticized by the other members as it has consumed [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] entire personality, and is all he ever does or talks about now. [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn]] loves to document his progress on social media like TikTok, Snapchat, and Instagram. [[Category:Events]]</text>
<sha1>esgvb2fd1h7mf5wsf4affra09s6zf9p</sha1>
</revision>

<text bytes="1048" sha1="9eg84c2esjgzgec1rv3yrm187cbwzot" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Sportsyou.png|thumb|287x287px|The Main Page]]The '''Less People SportsYou Team''' was created sometime in early 2026, with the purpose of messaging during school and coordinating [[Mr. Huddleson|FlexTime's]]. It replaced the [[Less People Google Doc]]. Ever since, it has been the catalyst for unique inside jokes, news, media, and [[Fantasy Football]]. Despite having members from 3 schools, it's mostly only used by the [[McKinney Boyd High School|McKinney Boyd]] students. [[File:Sportsyou first media.png|thumb|284x284px|The first message: a picture of a cat with no caption.]] == Notable Inside Jokes == * Cat Photo * [[Mr. Huddleson|Mr. Huddleson's FlexTime]] * [[Celina Twink Party]] * Capybara Bus Guy * Paper Towel Dispenser Heist * More People * Snapchat Era * The Feast * Mogging Crows * [[Class Of '09 Daniel]] * AI Fantasy Football PFPs * Bradyi dont think aj brown will be all that good w Bones * because it you!!! * [[Mayday Melee]] * [[i have no mouth but i must scream]] * [[Mr. Anderson Bathtub]] [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>
<sha1>9eg84c2esjgzgec1rv3yrm187cbwzot</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="642" sha1="c3bqfxxiz1p8083dq5iv7gvx1341h4k" xml:space="preserve">{{Discord_Server|title1=less people|creation_date=August 26th, 2025|abandoned_date=January 23rd, 2026|link=https://docs.google.com/document/d/1s2eoBcdDSAHXrD9MP5WY2vVL7gSQrTuEjzuWtRUAqKQ/edit?usp=sharing|owner=Teddy|member_count=9|image1=doc.png}} The '''less people''' Google Doc was a web document that served as a group chat during school hours from August 2025 to January 2026. Originally, it was created by [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] so the students of [[McKinney Boyd High School]] could coordinate their [[Mr. Huddleson|FlexTimes]] together. It was replaced by the [[SportsYou Team|Less People SportsYou Team]]. [[Category:Group Chat]]</text>

<page>
<title>I have no mouth but i must scream</title>
<ns>0</ns>
<id>583</id>
<revision>
<id>1309</id>
<parentid>1308</parentid>
<timestamp>2026-10-28T23:36:05Z</timestamp>
<contributor>
<username>OfficerBugg</username>
<id>51322662</id>
</contributor>
<comment>Adding categories</comment>
<origin>1309</origin>
<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="1438" sha1="go5zf8ixeuq2dg7wegj2yc0eywf3cjx" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Ihavenomouthbutimustscream.png|thumb|288x288px|The Inciting Message Chain]] [[File:A.M..png|thumb|211x211px|One of the fanarts found by [[Brayden]].]] The '''i have no mouth but i must scream''' inside joke began on October 20th, 2026, in the [[SportsYou Team|Less People SportsYou]] after [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] made a post about his messages not going through. Whist doing this, he made a reference to the 1967 dystopian horror short story, "I Have No Mouth, and I Must Scream," by Harlan Ellison. [[Brady Jones|Brady]] coincidentlely happened to google search Teddy's quote, and uncover the reference. This kickstarted a series of callbacks to this exchange. Teddy soon after also referenced the quote "Cogito Ergo Sum," or "I think therefore I am," from Rene Descartes (or as they know him, the creator of the Rule of Signs from pre-calc), who's quote is featured in the short story. Emphasis was put on the characters A.M. (or the Allied Mastercomputer), and Ted, the protagonist from which the quote comes from, who ironically happens to share his name with [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]]. [[Brayden]] liked to apply the nicknames "Adaptive Manipulator" and "Aggressive Menace" for A.M. [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brayden]] would once google A.M. fanart when they we're left alone in FlexTime one day, and they even made an AI generated picture of [[Mr. Anderson Bathtub|Mr. Anderson]] as A.M. [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>go5zf8ixeuq2dg7wegj2yc0eywf3cjx</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<model>wikitext</model>
<format>text/x-wiki</format>
<text bytes="847" sha1="fch76vfgyny9sdcfvc9mmgwue1eb97m" xml:space="preserve">[[File:MrAndersonBathtub.png|thumb|300x300px|The original AI generated image]] [[File:Mr. Anderson.png|thumb|200x200px|The alleged source material]] The Mr. Anderson Bathtub photo was airdropped to [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] sometime in his Sophomore year (2026) during his Computer Science period with Mr. Anderson, presumably by an indian kid named [[Vivaan]]. The image is AI generated, and features the teacher, Mr. Anderson, standing next to a bathtub with a pleasent composure, appearing almost photogenic, as if he was a model in a professional photograph. [[File:AMmrAnderson.png|thumb|232x232px|AI generated adaptation of Mr. Anderson as [[I have no mouth but i must scream|A.M.]] by [[Brayden]].]] The photo would circulate in the [[SportsYou Team|SportsYou]], and would also be recreated. [[Category:Characters]] [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>fch76vfgyny9sdcfvc9mmgwue1eb97m</sha1>
</revision>
</page>

<text bytes="505" sha1="n0jusz4ujw2y5g5gcs4i7ugxfbcrkg5" xml:space="preserve">[[File:Daniel-jeffery.png|thumb|291x291px]] A screenshot from Class Of '09 by [[Matthew]], posted on the [[SportsYou Team|SportsYou]] toward the last day of school in his Sophomore year (May 2026). It features the in-game character Jeffery, wearing a Mario shirt, and bragging about the porn on his phone. He told [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] that he posted the screenshot because it reminded him of [[Daniel]], as both of them like Mario, and both of them like internet porn, too. [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>n0jusz4ujw2y5g5gcs4i7ugxfbcrkg5</sha1>

<text bytes="940" sha1="5o2x8rfobccrm64n4adxu50jsb6lchx" xml:space="preserve">The Mayday Melee refers to an inside joke among [[Teddy Pemberton|Teddy]] and [[Brady Jones|Brady]] regarding the Patriots vs. Bears thursday game on October 22nd, 2026. The joke started because Teddy and Brady were facing off in [[Fantasy Football]] that week, and they both had starting quarterbacks Maye and Williams in that order. According to the joke, North Korea was going to nuke Chicago on game night, destroying Soldier Field, and taking the QBs with it. At lunch, they began to ask the google AI "why might we be worried about a nuclear attack on chicago this thursday?", and it would respond with numerous funny guesses. One of its conclusions was that it was because "Drake Maye's middle name is Lee, meaning headlines would read 'Maye-Lee in Soldier Field', which sounded kind of like 'Meelee'," hence the name "Mayday Melee." Brady had Teddy agree to not tell [[Brayden]] or [[Matthew]] about this. [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>5o2x8rfobccrm64n4adxu50jsb6lchx</sha1>
</revision>

<text bytes="977" sha1="98b8kqb0c2r7ww9ksvibk307nqav4h2" xml:space="preserve">The '''Celina Twink Party''' was when a bunch of [[Vaughn Lee Rutherford|Vaughn's]] school friends (who looked like twinks) came over to his house. They wore his dad's coat (which they never returned), slept with Vaughn, and took a very progressive photo in Vaughn's mirror. Less People would mock Vaughn and his friends for this photo later on, spreading the photo through [[New year new me 2026 (Discord Server)|NYNM2026]] and [[SportsYou Team|SportsYou]]. Vaughn once claimed that these same people attempted to force Vaughn to suck them off, and that he narrowly escaped it by inches, though Vaughn now denies this. If true, this is another example of the [[Mistreatment of Vaughn]]. These friends are not to be confused with the ones that pooped in his older sister's closet previously.[[File:Celina Twink party.png|thumb|The Celina Twink Party mirror photo (Vaughn pictured on the right) (His dad's coat is worn by the phone holder)|348x348px]] [[Category:Inside Joke]]</text>
<sha1>98b8kqb0c2r7ww9ksvibk307nqav4h2</sha1>
</revision>
